```
    86→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    87→    if options is None:
    88→        return default
    89→    return getattr(options, name, default)

    92→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    93→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    94→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    95→        return state.get("scan_path")
    96→    return scan_path
    97→
    98→
    99→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
   100→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   101→        return True
   102→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   103→    if not scores:
   104→        return True
   105→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
   109→    return (
   110→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   111→        and counts_toward_objective_backlog(item)
   112→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   117→    return [
   118→        item for item in items
   119→        if is_triage_finding(item)
   120→    ]
   121→
   122→
   123→def _assessment_request_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   124→    return [
   125→        item for item in items
   126→        if is_assessment_request(item)
   127→    ]
   128→
   129→
   130→def _active_cluster_issue_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
   131→    """Return issue IDs owned by clusters that are active planned work."""
   132→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   133→        return set()
   134→    active_ids: set[str] = set()
   135→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   136→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   137→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict) or not cluster_is_active(cluster):
   138→            continue
   139→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   140→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id and issue_id not in skipped_ids:
   141→                active_ids.add(issue_id)
   142→    return active_ids
   143→
   144→
   145→def _all_cluster_issue_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
   146→    """Return issue IDs owned by any cluster (regardless of status)."""
   147→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   148→        return set()
   149→    all_ids: set[str] = set()
   150→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   151→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   152→            continue
   153→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   154→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id:
   155→                all_ids.add(issue_id)
   156→    return all_ids
   157→
   158→
   159→def _merge_execution_candidates(
   160→    *,
   161→    all_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   162→    explicit_objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   163→    plan: dict | None,
   164→    review_issue_ids: set[str],
   165→    assessment_request_ids: set[str],
   166→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   167→    """Merge queue-owned execution items with objective defaults."""
   168→    explicit_queue_ids = _live_planned_queue_ids(plan)
   169→    active_cluster_ids = _active_cluster_issue_ids(plan)
   170→
   171→    queued_non_review_items = [
   172→        item
   173→        for item in all_issue_items
   174→        if item.get("id", "") in explicit_queue_ids
   175→        and item.get("id", "") not in assessment_request_ids
   176→        and (
   177→            item.get("id", "") not in review_issue_ids
   178→            or item.get("id", "") in active_cluster_ids
   179→        )
   180→    ]
   181→
   182→    execution_candidates: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   183→    seen_execution_ids: set[str] = set()
   184→    for item in [*explicit_objective_items, *queued_non_review_items]:
   185→        item_id = str(item.get("id", ""))
   186→        if not item_id or item_id in seen_execution_ids:
   187→            continue
   188→        seen_execution_ids.add(item_id)
   189→        execution_candidates.append(item)
   190→
   191→    anchored_execution_items = [
   192→        item
   193→        for item in execution_candidates
   194→        if item.get("id", "") in explicit_queue_ids
   195→    ]
   196→    return execution_candidates, anchored_execution_items
   197→
   198→
   199→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   200→    plan: dict | None,
   201→    state: StateModel,
   202→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   203→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   204→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   205→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   206→        return review_issue_items
   207→
   208→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   209→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   210→        return []
   211→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   212→        return []
   213→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   214→        return []
   215→    return review_issue_items
   216→
   217→
   218→def _subjective_partitions(
   219→    state: StateModel,
   220→    *,
   221→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   222→    threshold: float,
   223→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   224→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   225→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   226→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   227→    return initial, postflight
   228→
   229→
   230→def _workflow_partitions(
   231→    plan: dict | None,
   232→    state: StateModel,
   233→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   234→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   235→        return [], [], []
   236→    scan_items = [
   237→        item
   238→        for item in (
   239→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   240→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   241→        )
   242→        if item is not None
   243→    ]
   244→    postflight_workflow = [
   245→        item
   246→        for item in (
   247→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   248→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   249→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   250→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   251→        )
   252→        if item is not None
   253→    ]
   254→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   255→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   256→
   257→
   258→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   259→    plan: dict | None,
   260→    *,
   261→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   262→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   263→    anchored_execution_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   264→    explicit_queue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   265→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   266→    review_backlog_present: bool,
   267→    postflight_assessment_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   268→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   269→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   270→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   271→) -> str:
   272→    persisted_phase = (
   273→        current_lifecycle_phase(plan) if isinstance(plan, dict) else None
   274→    )
   275→    postflight_sticky = (
   276→        isinstance(plan, dict)
   277→        and bool(plan.get("plan_start_scores"))
   278→        and not postflight_scan_pending(plan)
   279→        and persisted_phase in {
   280→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
   281→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
   282→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
   283→        }
   284→    )
   285→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   286→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   287→    if postflight_sticky:
   288→        if postflight_assessment_items:
   289→            return PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
   290→        if postflight_workflow_items:
   291→            return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   292→        if triage_items and review_backlog_present:
   293→            return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   294→        if postflight_review_items:
   295→            return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   296→        if triage_items:
   297→            return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   298→    if anchored_execution_items:
   299→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   300→    if scan_items:
   301→        return PHASE_SCAN
   302→    if explicit_queue_items:
   303→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   304→    # Postflight sequence is ordered, not opportunistic:
   305→    # scan -> subjective review -> score/workflow -> triage.
   306→    if postflight_assessment_items:
   307→        return PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
   308→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   309→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   310→    if triage_items and review_backlog_present:
   311→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   312→    if postflight_review_items:
   313→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   314→    if triage_items:
   315→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   316→    return PHASE_SCAN
   317→
   318→
   319→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   320→    phase: str,
   321→    *,
   322→    explicit_queue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   323→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   324→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   325→    postflight_assessment_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   326→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   327→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   328→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   329→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   330→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   331→        return initial_review_items
   332→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   333→        return explicit_queue_items
   334→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   335→        deferred_items = [
   336→            item for item in scan_items
   337→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   338→        ]
   339→        if deferred_items:
   340→            return deferred_items
   341→        return [
   342→            item for item in scan_items
   343→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   344→        ]
   345→    if phase == PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT:
   346→        return postflight_assessment_items
   347→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   348→        return postflight_review_items
   349→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   350→        return postflight_workflow_items
   351→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   352→        return triage_items
   353→    return []
   354→
   355→
   356→def build_queue_snapshot(
   357→    state: StateModel,
   358→    *,
   359→    options: object | None = None,
   360→    plan: dict | None = None,
   361→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   362→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   363→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   364→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   365→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   366→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   367→    )
   368→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   369→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   370→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues((state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})), scan_path)
   371→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   372→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   373→
   374→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   375→        state,
   376→        scan_path=scan_path,
   377→        status_filter="open",
   378→        scope=scope,
   379→        chronic=chronic,
   380→        forced_ids=_live_planned_queue_ids(effective_plan),
   381→    )
   382→    objective_items = [
   383→        item for item in all_issue_items
   384→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   385→    ]
   386→    executable_objective_ids = _executable_objective_ids(
   387→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   388→        effective_plan,
   389→    )
   390→    all_clustered_ids = _all_cluster_issue_ids(effective_plan)
   391→    if (
   392→        isinstance(effective_plan, dict)
   393→        and not _live_planned_queue_ids(effective_plan)
   394→        and all_clustered_ids & executable_objective_ids
   395→    ):
   396→        executable_objective_ids -= all_clustered_ids
   397→    explicit_objective_items = [
   398→        item for item in objective_items
   399→        if item.get("id", "") in executable_objective_ids
   400→    ]
   401→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   402→    assessment_request_items = _assessment_request_items(all_issue_items)
   403→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   404→        effective_plan,
   405→        state,
   406→        review_issue_items,
   407→    )
   408→    review_issue_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in review_issue_items}
   409→    assessment_request_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in assessment_request_items}
   410→    explicit_queue_items, anchored_execution_items = _merge_execution_candidates(
   411→        all_issue_items=all_issue_items,
   412→        explicit_objective_items=explicit_objective_items,
   413→        plan=effective_plan,
   414→        review_issue_ids=review_issue_ids,
   415→        assessment_request_ids=assessment_request_ids,
   416→    )
   417→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   418→        state,
   419→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   420→        threshold=target_strict,
   421→    )
   422→    # Suppress subjective dimension items when review issues already cover
   423→    # the same dimension — the review issues are more actionable and will
   424→    # surface in the review-postflight phase instead.
   425→    postflight_assessment_items = [
   426→        item
   427→        for item in subjective_postflight_items
   428→        if not (
   429→            item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   430→            and int((item.get("detail") or {}).get("open_review_issues", 0)) > 0
   431→        )
   432→    ] + list(assessment_request_items)
   433→    postflight_review_items = [
   434→        *executable_review_items,
   435→    ]
   436→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   437→        effective_plan,
   438→        state,
   439→    )
   440→
   441→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   442→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   443→        effective_plan,
   444→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   445→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   446→        anchored_execution_items=anchored_execution_items,
   447→        explicit_queue_items=explicit_queue_items,
   448→        scan_items=scan_items,
   449→        review_backlog_present=bool(review_issue_items),
   450→        postflight_assessment_items=postflight_assessment_items,
   451→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   452→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   453→        triage_items=triage_items,
   454→    )
   455→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   456→        phase,
   457→        explicit_queue_items=explicit_queue_items,
   458→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   459→        scan_items=scan_items,
   460→        postflight_assessment_items=postflight_assessment_items,
   461→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   462→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   463→        triage_items=triage_items,
   464→    )
   465→
   466→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   467→    backlog_items = [
   468→        item for item in (
   469→            [
   470→                *objective_items,
   471→                *initial_review_items,
   472→                *postflight_assessment_items,
   473→                *review_issue_items,
   474→                *scan_items,
   475→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   476→                *triage_items,
   477→            ]
   478→        )
   479→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   480→    ]
   481→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   482→        1 for item in objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   483→    )
   484→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(explicit_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   485→
   486→    return QueueSnapshot(
   487→        phase=phase,
   488→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   489→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   490→        all_postflight_assessment_items=tuple(postflight_assessment_items),
   491→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   492→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   493→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   494→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   495→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   496→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   497→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   498→        planned_objective_count=len(explicit_objective_items),
   499→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   500→            1
   501→            for item in execution_items
   502→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   503→            and counts_toward_objective_backlog(item)
   504→        ),
   505→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   506→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   507→        assessment_postflight_count=len(postflight_assessment_items),
   508→        subjective_postflight_count=len(subjective_postflight_items),
   509→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   510→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   511→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   512→    )
   513→
   514→
   515→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   516→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   517→    if phase in {
   518→        PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL,
   519→        PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT,
   520→        PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT,
   521→    }:
   522→        return "review"
   523→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   524→        return "workflow"
   525→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   526→        return "triage"
   527→    return phase
   528→
   529→
   530→__all__ = [
   531→    "PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT",
   532→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   533→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   534→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",

   537→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   538→    "QueueSnapshot",
   539→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   540→    "coarse_phase_name",
   541→]
```

```
     2→
     3→Builds workflow stage items, score checkpoint items, create-plan items,

    44→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    45→)
    46→
    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→# Dimension key normalization
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50→
    51→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    52→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    53→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    54→    target = cleaned.lower()
    55→
    56→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    57→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    58→            return str(dim_key)
    59→    return slugify(cleaned)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    63→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    64→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    65→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    66→    return {
    67→        cleaned.lower(),
    68→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    69→        slugify(cleaned),
    70→        canonical.lower(),
    71→        slugify(canonical),
    72→    }
    73→
    74→
    75→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→# Subjective strict scores
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→
    79→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    80→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    81→    if not dim_scores:
    82→        return {}
    83→
    84→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    85→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    86→    for entry in entries:
    87→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    88→        if not name:
    89→            continue
    90→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    91→        [REDACTED](name)
    92→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    93→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    94→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    95→            if not key:
    96→                continue
    97→            aliases.add(key)
    98→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    99→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   100→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   101→        for alias in aliases:
   102→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   103→    return scores
   104→
   105→
   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107→# Synthetic item builders
   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→
   110→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   111→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   112→
   113→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   114→    """
   115→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   116→    order_set = set(order)
   117→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   118→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   119→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   121→    present_names = {
   122→        name
   123→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   124→        if sid in present_ids
   125→    }
   126→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   127→    if not present_names:
   128→        return []
   129→
   130→    issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   131→    open_review_count = sum(
   132→        1 for f in issues.values()
   133→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→        and is_triage_finding(f)
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   138→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   140→        if name not in present_names:
   141→            continue
   142→        if name in confirmed:
   143→            continue
   144→
   145→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   146→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   147→        blocked_by = sorted(
   148→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   149→        )
   150→
   151→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   152→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   153→
   154→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   155→            "id": sid,
   156→            "tier": 1,
   157→            "confidence": "high",
   158→            "detector": "triage",
   159→            "file": ".",
   160→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   161→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   162→            "detail": {
   163→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   164→                "stage": name,
   165→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   166→                "runner_commands": [
   167→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   168→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   169→                ],
   170→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   171→            },
   172→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   173→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   174→        }
   175→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   176→        items.append(item)
   177→    return items
   178→
   179→
   180→def build_subjective_items(
   181→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   182→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   183→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   184→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   185→    if not dim_scores:
   186→        return []
   187→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   188→
   189→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   190→    if not subjective_entries:
   191→        return []
   192→    unassessed_dims = {
   193→        str(name).strip()
   194→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   195→            dim_scores
   196→        )
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   200→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for issue in issues.values():
   202→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   203→            continue
   204→        if is_triage_finding(issue):
   205→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   206→            if dim_key:
   207→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   210→    def _prepare_command(
   211→        cli_keys: list[str],
   212→        *,
   213→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   214→    ) -> str:
   215→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   216→        if cli_keys:
   217→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   218→        if force_review_rerun:
   219→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   220→        return command
   221→
   222→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   223→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   224→        if not name:
   225→            continue
   226→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   227→        [REDACTED](name)
   228→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   229→        cli_keys = [
   230→            str(key).strip().lower()
   231→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   232→            if str(key).strip()
   233→        ]
   234→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   235→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   236→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   237→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   238→            name in unassessed_dims
   239→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   240→        )
   241→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   242→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   243→            continue
   244→        # Postflight assessment work covers every dimension still below the
   245→        # target threshold. Unassessed and stale dimensions remain special
   246→        # cases, but fresh under-target scores surface alongside them instead
   247→        # of being relegated to advisory-only status.
   248→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   249→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   250→        if open_review > 0:
   251→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   252→        else:
   253→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   254→        reason_tags = ["below target"]
   255→        if is_stale:
   256→            reason_tags.append("stale")
   257→        reasons = ", ".join(reason_tags)
   258→        summary = f"Subjective review needed: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%) [{reasons}]"
   259→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   260→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   261→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   262→            "file": ".",
   263→            "confidence": "medium",
   264→            "summary": summary,
   265→            "detail": {
   266→                "dimension_name": name,
   267→                "dimension": dim_key,
   268→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   269→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   270→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   271→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   272→            },
   273→            "status": "open",
   274→        }
   275→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   276→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   277→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   278→        items.append(item)
   279→    return items
   280→
   281→
   282→__all__ = [
   283→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   284→    "build_create_plan_item",
   285→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   286→    "build_import_scores_item",
   287→    "build_run_scan_item",
   288→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   289→    "build_subjective_items",
   290→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   291→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   292→]
   293→
```

```
    29→
    30→def _workflow_action_item(
    31→    *,
    32→    item_id: str,

    34→    primary_command: str,
    35→    detail: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    36→) -> WorkflowActionItem:

    51→
    52→def _confirm_attestation_hint(stage: str) -> str:
    53→    if stage == "observe":
    54→        return "I have thoroughly reviewed..."
    55→    if stage == "reflect":
    56→        return "My strategy accounts for..."
    57→    if stage == "organize":
    58→        return "This plan is correct..."
    59→    if stage == "enrich":
    60→        return "Steps are executor-ready..."
    61→    if stage == "sense-check":
    62→        return "Content, structure and value verified..."
    63→    return "..."
    64→
    65→
    66→def _runner_planning_tools(
    67→    *,
    68→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    69→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    70→) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    71→    tools = [
    72→        {"label": label, "command": command}
    73→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    74→    ]
    75→    if manual_fallback:
    76→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    77→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    78→    return tools
    79→
    80→
    81→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    82→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    84→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    85→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    86→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    87→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    88→            return stage, True
    89→
    90→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    91→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    92→        if sid not in order:
    93→            continue
    94→        return stage, False
    95→
    96→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    97→        return "commit", False
    98→
    99→    return None, False
   100→
   101→
   102→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
   103→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   104→    if stage is None:
   105→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   106→    if needs_confirm:
   107→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
   108→        return (
   109→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
   110→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
   111→        )
   112→    if stage == "commit":
   113→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   114→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   118→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   119→    if needs_confirm:
   120→        return []
   121→    if stage == "commit":
   122→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   123→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   124→        )
   125→    if stage:
   126→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   127→            only_stages=stage,
   128→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   129→        )
   130→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   131→        only_stages="observe",
   132→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   133→    )
   134→
   135→
   136→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   137→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
   138→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   142→
   143→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   144→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   145→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   146→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   147→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   148→
   149→    return _workflow_action_item(
   150→        item_id=WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   151→        summary=f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   152→        detail={
   153→            "strict": strict,
   154→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   155→            "delta": delta,
   156→            "planning_tools": _runner_planning_tools(
   157→                only_stages="observe",
   158→                manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   159→            ),
   160→        },
   161→        primary_command=triage_run_stages_command(only_stages="observe"),
   162→    )
   163→
   164→
   165→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   166→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if queued."""
   167→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    return _workflow_action_item(
   171→        item_id=WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   172→        summary="Create prioritized plan from review results",
   173→        detail={
   174→            "planning_tools": _create_plan_planning_tools(plan),
   175→        },
   176→        primary_command=_create_plan_primary_command(plan),
   177→    )
   178→
   179→
   180→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   181→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::import-scores`` if queued."""
   182→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   183→        return None
   184→    meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   185→    import_file = (meta.import_file if meta is not None else "").strip() or "issues.json"
   186→    quoted_import_file = shlex.quote(import_file)
   187→    packet_sha = meta.packet_sha256 if meta is not None else ""
   188→    explanation = (
   189→        "Review issues were imported but assessment scores were skipped "
   190→        "(untrusted source). Re-import the same batch with attestation to update dimension scores."
   191→    )
   192→    if import_file != "issues.json":
   193→        explanation += f" Expected import file: `{import_file}`."
   194→    if packet_sha:
   195→        explanation += f" Expected packet sha256: `{packet_sha}`."
   196→
   197→    return _workflow_action_item(
   198→        item_id=WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   199→        summary="Import assessment scores with attestation",
   200→        detail={
   201→            "explanation": explanation,
   202→            "expected_import_file": import_file,
   203→            "packet_sha256": packet_sha,
   204→        },
   205→        primary_command=(
   206→            f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import_file} --attested-external "
   207→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   208→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   209→        ),
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   214→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if queued."""
   215→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   216→        return None
   217→
   218→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   219→
   220→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   221→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   222→
   223→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta
   224→    # so the user sees old-baseline → current, not current → current.
   225→    prev_start = (plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   226→    if prev_start is None:
   227→        prev_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   228→    delta = round(strict - prev_start, 1) if prev_start is not None else None
   229→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   230→
   231→    return _workflow_action_item(
   232→        item_id=WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   233→        summary=f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   234→        detail={
   235→            "strict": strict,
   236→            "previous_plan_start_strict": prev_start,
   237→            "delta": delta,
   238→        },
   239→        primary_command=(
   240→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   241→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   242→        ),
   243→    )
   244→
   245→
   246→def build_run_scan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   247→    """Build a synthetic item for the first post-flight scan step."""
   248→    if not postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   249→        return None
   250→
   251→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   252→        item_id=WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
   253→        summary="Run post-flight scan to refresh queue and surface follow-up review work.",
   254→        detail={
   255→            "phase": "postflight_scan",
   256→        },
   257→        primary_command="desloppify scan",
   258→    )
   259→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"
   260→    return item
   261→
   262→
   263→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   265→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   266→        return []
   267→    deferred: list[str] = []
   268→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   269→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   270→            continue
   271→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   272→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   273→    deferred.sort()
   274→    return deferred
   275→
   276→
   277→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   278→    plan: dict,
   279→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   280→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   281→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   282→    if not deferred_set:
   283→        return 0, 0
   284→
   285→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   286→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   287→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   288→
   289→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   290→    cluster_count = 0
   291→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   292→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   293→            continue
   294→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   295→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   296→            continue
   297→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   298→        if not matched:
   299→            continue
   300→        cluster_count += 1
   301→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   302→
   303→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   304→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   305→
   306→
   307→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   308→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   309→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   310→    if not deferred_ids:
   311→        return None
   312→
   313→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   314→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   315→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   316→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   317→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   318→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   319→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   320→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   321→    backlog_cmd = 'desloppify plan backlog "*"'
   322→    subset_backlog_cmd = "desloppify plan backlog <cluster-or-id>"
   323→    wontfix_cmd = (
   324→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   325→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   326→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   327→        "--confirm"
   328→    )
   329→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   330→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   331→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   332→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   333→    )
   334→
   335→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   336→        item_id=WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   337→        summary=(
   338→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   339→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   340→            "must be reactivated, moved to backlog, or marked wontfix."
   341→        ),
   342→        detail={
   343→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   344→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   345→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   346→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   347→            "backlog_command": backlog_cmd,
   348→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   349→            "planning_tools": [
   350→                {
   351→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   352→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   353→                },
   354→                {
   355→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   356→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   357→                },
   358→                {
   359→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   360→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   361→                },
   362→                {
   363→                    "label": "Move a subset to backlog",
   364→                    "command": subset_backlog_cmd,
   365→                },
   366→                {
   367→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   368→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   369→                },
   370→            ],
   371→            "decision_options": [
   372→                {
   373→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   374→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   375→                },
   376→                {
   377→                    "label": "Move deferred work to backlog",
   378→                    "command": backlog_cmd,
   379→                },
   380→                {
   381→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   382→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   383→                },
   384→            ],
   385→        },
   386→        primary_command=reactivate_cmd,
   387→    )
   388→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"
   389→    return item
   390→
   391→
   392→__all__ = [
   393→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   394→    "build_create_plan_item",
   395→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   396→    "build_import_scores_item",
   397→    "build_run_scan_item",
   398→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   399→]
   400→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any, cast
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER
    10→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

    32→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    33→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    34→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    39→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    40→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    41→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    42→
    43→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    44→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    45→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -2         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    46→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -1     # Epic triage workflow stages
    47→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
    48→_RANK_ISSUE = 1           # Individual issues + assessed subjective
    49→
    50→def _workflow_stage_index(item: WorkQueueItem) -> int:
    51→    raw_index = item.get("stage_index")
    52→    if raw_index is not None:
    53→        if isinstance(raw_index, int):
    54→            return raw_index
    55→        if isinstance(raw_index, str):
    56→            stripped = raw_index.strip()
    57→            if stripped.lstrip("-").isdigit():
    58→                return int(stripped)
    59→        logger.warning("Invalid workflow stage index %r", raw_index)
    60→    return TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER.get(workflow_stage_name(item).lower(), 0)
    61→
    62→
    63→def enrich_with_impact(
    64→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]
    65→) -> None:
    66→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    67→
    68→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    69→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    70→    """
    71→    if not dimension_scores:
    72→        for item in items:
    73→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key="strict")
    77→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    80→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    81→    for entry in entries:
    82→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    83→        if not name:
    84→            continue
    85→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    86→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    87→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    88→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    89→
    90→    for item in items:
    91→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    92→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    93→
    94→
    95→def _compute_item_impact(
    96→    item: WorkQueueItem,
    97→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    98→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    99→) -> float:
   100→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
   101→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   102→
   103→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective issues):
   104→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
   105→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   106→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
   107→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
   108→        if entry:
   109→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   110→        return 0.0
   111→
   112→    # Mechanical issues: use detector -> dimension mapping
   113→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   114→    if detector:
   115→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   116→        if dimension:
   117→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
   118→            if entry:
   119→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   120→
   121→    return 0.0
   122→
   123→
   124→def build_issue_items(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scan_path: str | None,
   128→    status_filter: str,
   129→    scope: str | None,
   130→    chronic: bool,
   131→    forced_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   132→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   133→    scoped = path_scoped_issues((state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})), scan_path)
   134→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   135→    out: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   136→    forced_ids = forced_ids or set()
   137→
   138→    for issue_id, issue in scoped.items():
   139→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
   140→            continue
   141→        if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   142→            continue
   143→        if chronic and not (
   144→            issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   145→        ):
   146→            continue
   147→
   148→        # Evidence-only: skip issues below standalone confidence threshold
   149→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   150→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   151→        if issue_id not in forced_ids and meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   152→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   153→            issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   154→            if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
   155→                continue
   156→
   157→        item = cast(WorkQueueItem, dict(issue))
   158→        item["id"] = issue_id
   159→        item["kind"] = "issue"
   160→        item["action_type"] = meta.action_type if meta is not None else "manual_fix"
   161→        item["is_review"] = is_review_issue(item)
   162→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_issue(item)
   163→        item["review_weight"] = (
   164→            review_issue_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   165→        )
   166→        subjective_score = None
   167→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   168→            detail = detail_dict(issue)
   169→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   170→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   171→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   172→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   173→            )
   174→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   175→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   176→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_issue(
   177→            item,
   178→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   182→            continue
   183→        out.append(item)
   184→
   185→    return out
   186→
   187→
   188→def _natural_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
   189→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   190→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   191→
   192→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
   193→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   194→        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   195→
   196→    # Triage stage items: stage order, blocked after unblocked
   197→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   198→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   199→        stage_index = _workflow_stage_index(item)
   200→        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   201→
   202→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan)
   203→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   204→        return (_RANK_WORKFLOW, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   205→
   206→    if kind == "cluster":
   207→        # Clusters sort before individual issues, ordered by action type
   208→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   209→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   210→        )
   211→        return (
   212→            _RANK_CLUSTER,
   213→            action_pri,
   214→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   215→            item.get("id", ""),
   216→        )
   217→
   218→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   219→
   220→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   221→        return (
   222→            _RANK_ISSUE,
   223→            -impact,
   224→            subjective_score_value(item),
   225→            0,
   226→            0,
   227→            item.get("id", ""),
   228→        )
   229→
   230→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   231→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   232→    return (
   233→        _RANK_ISSUE,
   234→        -impact,
   235→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   236→        -review_weight,
   237→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   238→        item.get("id", ""),
   239→    )
   240→
   241→
   242→def item_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
   243→    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.
   244→
   245→    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
   246→    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
   247→    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.
   248→
   249→    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
   250→    """
   251→    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
   252→
   253→    if plan_pos is not None:
   254→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   255→        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
   256→        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
   257→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   258→            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   259→            stage_idx = _workflow_stage_index(item)
   260→            return (_TIER_PLANNED, plan_pos, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
   261→        return (_TIER_PLANNED, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   262→
   263→    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
   264→    group = _TIER_NEW if is_new else _TIER_EXISTING
   265→    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
   266→
   267→
   268→__all__ = [
   269→    "build_issue_items",
   270→    "enrich_with_impact",
   271→    "item_explain",
   272→    "item_sort_key",
   273→    "subjective_score_value",
   274→    "group_queue_items",
   275→]
   276→
```

```
1→"""Queue ranking and plan-aware finalization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, WorkQueueResult
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
     9→    enrich_plan_metadata,
    10→    separate_skipped,
    11→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    12→    stamp_positions,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import new_item_ids as _new_item_ids
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    16→    enrich_with_impact,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def _subjective_cluster_command(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> str:
    26→    cli_keys: list[str] = []
    27→    seen: set[str] = set()
    28→    for item in items:
    29→        detail = item.get("detail") or {}
    30→        cli_key = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
    31→        if cli_key and cli_key not in seen:
    32→            cli_keys.append(cli_key)
    33→            seen.add(cli_key)
    34→    if not cli_keys:
    35→        return "desloppify review --prepare"
    36→    return "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _collapse_postflight_subjective_review(
    40→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    41→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    42→    members = [
    43→        item for item in items
    44→        if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    45→        and not item.get("initial_review", False)
    46→    ]
    47→    if len(members) < 2:
    48→        return items
    49→
    50→    stale_count = sum(1 for item in members if bool((item.get("detail") or {}).get("stale")))
    51→    stale_suffix = f" ({stale_count} stale)" if stale_count else ""
    52→    cluster_item: WorkQueueItem = {
    53→        "id": "auto/subjective-review",
    54→        "kind": "cluster",
    55→        "summary": (
    56→            f"Re-review {len(members)} subjective dimensions below target{stale_suffix}"
    57→        ),
    58→        "members": members,
    59→        "member_count": len(members),
    60→        "primary_command": _subjective_cluster_command(members),
    61→        "action_type": "manual_fix",
    62→        "cluster_name": "auto/subjective-review",
    63→        "cluster_auto": True,
    64→        "cluster_optional": False,
    65→        "cluster_transient": True,
    66→        "execution_policy": "manual",
    67→        "execution_status": "review",
    68→        "confidence": "medium",
    69→        "detector": "subjective_assessment",
    70→        "file": ".",
    71→        "estimated_impact": max(
    72→            (float(member.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) or 0.0) for member in members),
    73→            default=0.0,
    74→        ),
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    first_member_id = members[0].get("id")
    78→    collapsed: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    79→    inserted = False
    80→    for item in items:
    81→        if item in members:
    82→            if not inserted and item.get("id") == first_member_id:
    83→                collapsed.append(cluster_item)
    84→                inserted = True
    85→            continue
    86→        collapsed.append(item)
    87→    return collapsed
    88→
    89→
    90→def finalize_queue(
    91→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    92→    *,
    93→    state: StateModel,
    94→    plan: dict | None,
    95→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    96→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    97→    """Apply ranking, plan ordering, limits, and explain metadata."""
    98→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    99→
   100→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   101→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   102→    items = _collapse_postflight_subjective_review(items)
   103→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   104→
   105→    total = len(items)
   106→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   107→        items = items[:opts.count]
   108→    if opts.explain:
   109→        for item in items:
   110→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   111→
   112→    return {
   113→        "items": items,
   114→        "total": total,
   115→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   116→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   117→    }
   118→
   119→
   120→def _plan_presort(
   121→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   122→    state: StateModel,
   123→    plan: dict | None,
   124→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   125→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting."""
   126→    if not plan:
   127→        return set(), []
   128→
   129→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   130→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   131→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   132→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   133→    items[:] = remaining
   134→    return new_ids, skipped
   135→
   136→
   137→def _plan_postsort(
   138→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   139→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   140→    plan: dict | None,
   141→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions."""
   144→    if not plan:
   145→        return
   146→    if opts.include_skipped:
   147→        items.extend(skipped)
   148→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   149→
   150→
   151→__all__ = ["finalize_queue"]
   152→
```

```
   119→class IssueDisposition(TypedDict, total=False):
   120→    """Per-issue intent/history accumulated across triage stages.
   121→
   122→    Observe writes verdict fields; reflect writes decision fields.
   123→    NOT derived state — ``plan["clusters"]`` and ``plan["skipped"]`` remain
   124→    authoritative for "what is actually true now."
   125→    """
   126→
   127→    # Observe writes (what did we find?):
   128→    verdict: str  # "genuine" | "false positive" | "exaggerated" | "over engineering" | "not worth it"
   129→    verdict_reasoning: str
   130→    files_read: list[str]
   131→    recommendation: str
   132→
   133→    # Reflect writes (what should we do about it?):
   134→    decision: str  # "cluster" | "skip"
   135→    target: str  # cluster name or skip reason
   136→    decision_source: str  # "observe_auto" (false-positive auto-skip) | "reflect" (strategy)

   210→    postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count: int

   212→
   213→
   214→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   215→    version: Required[int]
   216→    created: Required[str]
   217→    updated: Required[str]
   218→    queue_order: list[str]
   219→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   220→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   221→    active_cluster: str | None
   222→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   223→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   224→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   225→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs explicitly promoted from backlog into the queue
   226→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   227→    previous_plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   228→    refresh_state: RefreshState
   229→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   230→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   231→    subjective_defer_meta: dict[str, Any]
   232→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   233→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   234→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   235→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   236→
   237→
   238→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   239→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   240→    now = utc_now()
   241→    return {
   242→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   243→        "created": now,
   244→        "updated": now,
   245→        "queue_order": [],
   246→        "deferred": [],
   247→        "skipped": {},
   248→        "active_cluster": None,
   249→        "overrides": {},
   250→        "clusters": {},
   251→        "superseded": {},
   252→        "promoted_ids": [],
   253→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   254→        "refresh_state": {},
   255→        "execution_log": [],
   256→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   257→        "commit_log": [],
   258→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   259→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   260→    }
   261→
   262→
   263→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   264→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   265→
   266→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   267→    """
   268→    defaults = empty_plan()
   269→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   270→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   271→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   272→    subjective_defer_meta = plan.get("subjective_defer_meta")
   273→    if isinstance(subjective_defer_meta, dict):
   274→        subjective_defer_meta.pop("force_visible_ids", None)
   275→    epic_triage_meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
   276→    if isinstance(epic_triage_meta, dict):
   277→        epic_triage_meta.pop("triage_force_visible", None)
   278→
   279→
   280→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   281→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   282→    return {
   283→        name: cluster
   284→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   285→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   286→    }
   287→
   288→
   289→def live_planned_queue_ids(plan: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   290→    """Return substantive live queue IDs sourced only from ``queue_order``.
   291→
   292→    Overrides and clusters are ownership metadata — they must never expand
   293→    the live queue.  Only explicit ``queue_order`` entries count.
   294→    """
   295→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   296→        return set()
   297→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   298→    return {
   299→        str(issue_id)
   300→        for issue_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   301→        if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   302→        and issue_id
   303→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   304→        and not any(issue_id.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   305→    }
   306→
   307→
   308→def executable_objective_ids(
   309→    all_objective_ids: set[str],
   310→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None,
   311→) -> set[str]:
   312→    """Return objective IDs eligible for execution.
   313→
   314→    Before the plan tracks any queue work at all, all objective IDs are
   315→    implicitly executable. Once *any* queue items exist — including synthetic
   316→    review/workflow/triage items — execution becomes queue-driven and only
   317→    objective IDs explicitly present in ``plan["queue_order"]`` remain
   318→    eligible for ``next``.
   319→    """
   320→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   321→        return set(all_objective_ids)
   322→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   323→    queued_ids = {
   324→        issue_id
   325→        for issue_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   326→        if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   327→        and issue_id
   328→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   329→    }
   330→    live_queue_ids = live_planned_queue_ids(plan)
   331→    queued_objective_ids = all_objective_ids & live_queue_ids
   332→    if queued_objective_ids:
   333→        return queued_objective_ids
   334→    if not queued_ids:
   335→        return set(all_objective_ids) - skipped_ids
   336→    return set()
   337→
   338→
   339→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   340→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   341→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   342→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   343→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   344→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   345→
   346→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   347→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   348→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   349→    if overlap:
   350→        raise ValueError(
   351→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   352→        )
   353→
   354→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   355→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   356→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   357→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] must be an object")
   358→        if "kind" not in entry:
   359→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] missing required key 'kind'")
   360→        kind = entry["kind"]
   361→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   362→            raise ValueError(
   363→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   364→            )
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "ActionStep",
   369→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   370→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   371→    "IssueDisposition",
   372→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   373→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   374→    "Cluster",
```

```
1→"""Shared boundary-triggered plan reconciliation pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_issues
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import QueueSyncResult, is_synthetic_id
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.meta import append_log_entry
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import compute_subjective_visibility
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import set_lifecycle_phase
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import sync_subjective_dimensions
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import sync_triage_needed
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.workflow import (
    16→    ScoreSnapshot,
    17→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    18→    sync_create_plan_needed,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass
    23→class ReconcileResult:
    24→    """Mutation summary for one boundary-triggered reconcile pass."""
    25→
    26→    subjective: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    27→    auto_cluster_changes: int = 0
    28→    communicate_score: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    29→    create_plan: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    30→    triage: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    31→    lifecycle_phase: str = ""
    32→    lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def dirty(self) -> bool:
    36→        return any(
    37→            (
    38→                self.subjective is not None and bool(self.subjective.changes),
    39→                self.auto_cluster_changes > 0,
    40→                self.communicate_score is not None
    41→                and bool(self.communicate_score.changes),
    42→                self.create_plan is not None
    43→                and bool(self.create_plan.changes),
    44→                self.triage is not None
    45→                and bool(
    46→                    self.triage.changes
    47→                    or getattr(self.triage, "deferred", False)
    48→                ),
    49→                self.lifecycle_phase_changed,
    50→            )
    51→        )
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def workflow_injected_ids(self) -> list[str]:
    55→        injected: list[str] = []
    56→        for result in (self.communicate_score, self.create_plan):
    57→            if result is None:
    58→                continue
    59→            injected.extend(list(result.injected))
    60→        return injected
    61→
    62→
    63→def _current_scores(state: dict) -> ScoreSnapshot:
    64→    snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
    65→    return ScoreSnapshot(
    66→        strict=snapshot.strict,
    67→        overall=snapshot.overall,
    68→        objective=snapshot.objective,
    69→        verified=snapshot.verified,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _log_gate_changes(plan: dict, action: str, detail: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    74→    append_log_entry(plan, action, actor="system", detail=detail)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _persist_lifecycle(
    78→    plan: dict, state: dict, *, target_strict: float,
    79→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    80→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    81→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import coarse_phase_name
    82→
    83→    phase = coarse_phase_name(
    84→        queue_context(state, plan=plan, target_strict=target_strict).snapshot.phase
    85→    )
    86→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    87→
    88→
    89→def live_planned_queue_empty(plan: dict) -> bool:
    90→    """Return True when queue_order has no remaining substantive items.
    91→
    92→    Overrides and clusters are ownership metadata — they must never expand
    93→    the live queue.  Only explicit ``queue_order`` entries count.
    94→    """
    95→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    96→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    97→    return not any(
    98→        isinstance(item_id, str)
    99→        and item_id not in skipped
   100→        and not is_synthetic_id(item_id)
   101→        for item_id in order
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→def reconcile_plan(plan: dict, state: dict, *, target_strict: float) -> ReconcileResult:
   106→    """Run the shared boundary reconciliation pipeline."""
   107→    result = ReconcileResult()
   108→    if not live_planned_queue_empty(plan):
   109→        return result
   110→
   111→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   112→        state,
   113→        target_strict=target_strict,
   114→        plan=plan,
   115→    )
   116→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   117→
   118→    result.subjective = sync_subjective_dimensions(
   119→        plan,
   120→        state,
   121→        policy=policy,
   122→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   123→    )
   124→    if result.subjective.changes:
   125→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_subjective", {"changes": True})
   126→
   127→    result.auto_cluster_changes = int(
   128→        auto_cluster_issues(
   129→            plan,
   130→            state,
   131→            target_strict=target_strict,
   132→            policy=policy,
   133→        )
   134→    )
   135→    if result.auto_cluster_changes:
   136→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "auto_cluster", {"changes": True})
   137→
   138→    result.communicate_score = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   139→        plan,
   140→        state,
   141→        policy=policy,
   142→        current_scores=_current_scores(state),
   143→    )
   144→    if result.communicate_score.changes:
   145→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_communicate_score", {"injected": True})
   146→
   147→    result.create_plan = sync_create_plan_needed(
   148→        plan,
   149→        state,
   150→        policy=policy,
   151→    )
   152→    if result.create_plan.changes:
   153→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_create_plan", {"injected": True})
   154→
   155→    result.triage = sync_triage_needed(
   156→        plan,
   157→        state,
   158→        policy=policy,
   159→    )
   160→    if result.triage.injected:
   161→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_triage", {"injected": True})
   162→
   163→    result.lifecycle_phase, result.lifecycle_phase_changed = _persist_lifecycle(
   164→        plan,
   165→        state,
   166→        target_strict=target_strict,
   167→    )
   168→    if result.lifecycle_phase_changed:
   169→        _log_gate_changes(
   170→            plan,
   171→            "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   172→            {"phase": result.lifecycle_phase},
   173→        )
   174→
   175→    return result
   176→
   177→
   178→__all__ = ["ReconcileResult", "live_planned_queue_empty", "reconcile_plan"]
   179→
```
