```
1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
    25→
    26→[REDACTED]
    27→_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    28→[REDACTED]
    29→
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Helpers
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    42→    known_ids = triaged_ids | active_ids
    43→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - known_ids if known_ids else set()
    44→
    45→
    46→def _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta: dict) -> set[str]:
    47→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    48→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    49→    return triaged_ids | active_ids
    50→
    51→
    52→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    53→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    54→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    55→        while sid in order:
    56→            order.remove(sid)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    60→    order: list[str],
    61→    confirmed: set[str],
    62→    *,
    63→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    64→) -> list[str]:
    65→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    66→
    67→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    68→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    69→    """
    70→    stage_names = (
    71→        "observe",
    72→        "reflect",
    73→        "organize",
    74→        "enrich",
    75→        "sense-check",
    76→        "commit",
    77→    )
    78→    existing = set(order)
    79→    injected: list[str] = []
    80→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    81→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    82→            if skipped is not None:
    83→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    84→            order.append(sid)
    85→            injected.append(sid)
    86→            existing.add(sid)
    87→    return injected
    88→
    89→
    90→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    91→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    92→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    93→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    94→
    95→
    96→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    97→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    98→
    99→
   100→def _inject_triage_result(
   101→    order: list[str],
   102→    confirmed: set[str],
   103→    *,
   104→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
   105→    normalize: bool = False,
   106→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   107→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
   108→    if not injected:
   109→        return QueueSyncResult()
   110→    if normalize:
   111→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   112→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   116→    plan: PlanModel,
   117→    meta: dict,
   118→    *,
   119→    defer_state: dict,
   120→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   121→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   122→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   123→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   124→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   125→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   126→
   127→
   128→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   129→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   130→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   131→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   132→
   133→
   134→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   135→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   136→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   137→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   138→
   139→
   140→def _prune_stale_present_stages(
   141→    *,
   142→    plan: PlanModel,
   143→    state: StateModel,
   144→    order: list[str],
   145→    meta: dict,
   146→    last_hash: str,
   147→    confirmed: set[str],
   148→    recorded_unconfirmed: set[str],
   149→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   150→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   151→    if not last_hash or confirmed or recorded_unconfirmed:
   152→        return result
   153→    if not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta) and stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state):
   154→        return result
   155→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   156→    if new_since_triage:
   157→        return result
   158→    _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   159→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   160→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   161→    if current_hash:
   162→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   163→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   164→    result.pruned = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   165→    return result
   166→
   167→
   168→def _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   169→    *,
   170→    plan: PlanModel,
   171→    state: StateModel,
   172→    meta: dict,
   173→    last_hash: str,
   174→) -> None:
   175→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   176→    has_completed_stage = any(
   177→        isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("confirmed_at")
   178→        for v in stages.values()
   179→    )
   180→    if not has_completed_stage or meta.get("triaged_ids") or last_hash:
   181→        return
   182→    current_review = sorted(stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state))
   183→    if current_review:
   184→        meta["triaged_ids"] = current_review
   185→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   186→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   187→
   188→
   189→def _defer_or_inject_triage(
   190→    *,
   191→    plan: PlanModel,
   192→    state: StateModel,
   193→    order: list[str],
   194→    meta: dict,
   195→    confirmed: set[str],
   196→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   197→    new_since_triage: set[str],
   198→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   199→    decision = decide_triage_start(
   200→        plan,
   201→        state,
   202→        policy=policy,
   203→        explicit_start=False,
   204→        attested_override=False,
   205→    )
   206→    if decision.action == "defer":
   207→        defer_state = update_defer_state(
   208→            meta.get(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   209→            state=state,
   210→            deferred_ids=new_since_triage,
   211→            options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   212→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   213→            ),
   214→        )
   215→        meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   216→        escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   217→            defer_state,
   218→            state=state,
   219→            options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   220→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   221→            ),
   222→        )
   223→        if not escalated:
   224→            return _mark_triage_deferred(plan, meta, defer_state=defer_state)
   225→        _mark_triage_escalated(plan, meta)
   226→        return _inject_triage_result(
   227→            order,
   228→            confirmed,
   229→            skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   230→            normalize=True,
   231→        )
   232→
   233→    _mark_triage_ready(plan, meta)
   234→    return _inject_triage_result(
   235→        order,
   236→        confirmed,
   237→        skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def _sync_hash_change(
   242→    *,
   243→    plan: PlanModel,
   244→    state: StateModel,
   245→    order: list[str],
   246→    meta: dict,
   247→    confirmed: set[str],
   248→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   249→    current_hash: str,
   250→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   251→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   252→    if not new_since_triage and not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta):
   253→        new_since_triage = stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   254→    if new_since_triage:
   255→        return _defer_or_inject_triage(
   256→            plan=plan,
   257→            state=state,
   258→            order=order,
   259→            meta=meta,
   260→            confirmed=confirmed,
   261→            policy=policy,
   262→            new_since_triage=new_since_triage,
   263→        )
   264→
   265→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   266→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   267→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   268→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   269→    return QueueSyncResult()
   270→
   271→
   272→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   273→# Public API
   274→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   275→
   276→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   277→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   278→
   279→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   280→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   281→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   282→    resolved since then.
   283→
   284→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   285→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   286→    through the plan.
   287→    """
   288→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   289→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   290→
   291→
   292→def compute_open_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   293→    """Return the set of currently-open review/concerns issue IDs."""
   294→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   295→
   296→
   297→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   298→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   299→
   300→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   301→    """
   302→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   303→
   304→
   305→def sync_triage_needed(
   306→    plan: PlanModel,
   307→    state: StateModel,
   308→    *,
   309→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   310→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   311→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   312→
   313→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   314→
   315→    **Mid-cycle guard**: when the objective backlog still has work, triage
   316→    stages are NOT injected.  Instead, ``epic_triage_meta["triage_recommended"]``
   317→    is set so the UI can show a non-blocking banner.  Stages are injected
   318→    once the objective backlog drains (or on manual ``plan triage``). If
   319→    deferral repeats for multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are
   320→    injected in workflow/triage priority order with a forced-visibility marker.
   321→
   322→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   323→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   324→
   325→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   326→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   327→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   328→    """
   329→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   330→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   331→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   332→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   333→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   334→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   335→
   336→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   337→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   338→
   339→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   340→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   341→
   342→    if already_present:
   343→        return _prune_stale_present_stages(
   344→            plan=plan,
   345→            state=state,
   346→            order=order,
   347→            meta=meta,
   348→            last_hash=last_hash,
   349→            confirmed=confirmed,
   350→            recorded_unconfirmed=recorded_unconfirmed,
   351→        )
   352→
   353→    _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   354→        plan=plan,
   355→        state=state,
   356→        meta=meta,
   357→        last_hash=last_hash,
   358→    )
   359→
   360→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   361→        return _sync_hash_change(
   362→            plan=plan,
   363→            state=state,
   364→            order=order,
   365→            meta=meta,
   366→            confirmed=confirmed,
   367→            policy=policy,
   368→            current_hash=current_hash,
   369→        )
   370→
   371→    return result
   372→
   373→
   374→__all__ = [
   375→    "compute_open_issue_ids",
   376→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   377→    "is_triage_stale",
   378→    "sync_triage_needed",
   379→]
   380→
```

```
   117→    if not packet_sha256 and isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict):

   119→    return PendingImportScoresMeta(
   120→        timestamp=timestamp,
   121→        import_file=recorded_file,
   122→        normalized_import_file=_normalize_match_path(recorded_file) or "",
   123→        packet_sha256=packet_sha256,

   127→def pending_import_scores_meta(
   128→    plan: PlanModel,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
   131→    """Return queued score-import metadata without mutating the plan."""
   132→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   133→    if refresh_state is not None:
   134→        meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   135→            refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   136→        )
   137→        if meta is not None:
   138→            return meta
   139→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   140→    if issues_only_audit is None:

   149→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   150→    meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | dict[str, Any] | None,
   151→    *,
   152→    import_file: str,
   153→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   154→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   155→    """Return (matches, reason) for whether the import matches the pending batch.
   156→
   157→    Checks packet_sha256 first (strongest signal), falls back to normalized
   158→    file path.  Returns a single human-readable reason on mismatch.
   159→    """
   160→    normalized_meta = (
   161→        meta
   162→        if isinstance(meta, PendingImportScoresMeta)
   163→        else PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(meta)
   164→    )
   165→    if normalized_meta is None:
   166→        return True, ""
   167→
   168→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   169→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   170→
   171→    expected_hash = normalized_meta.packet_sha256
   172→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   173→    if expected_hash and current_hash:
   174→        if current_hash == expected_hash:
   175→            return True, ""
   176→        return False, f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash}"
   177→
   178→    expected_file = normalized_meta.normalized_import_file
   179→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   180→    if expected_file and current_file and current_file != expected_file:
   181→        return False, f"expected import file {normalized_meta.import_file}, got {import_file}"
   182→
   183→    return True, ""
   184→
   185→
   186→def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   187→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   188→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   189→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   190→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   191→    if refresh_state is not None:
   192→        refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
   193→
   194→
   195→def _pending_compare_timestamp(
   196→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   197→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any],
   198→) -> str:
   199→    if pending_meta is not None and pending_meta.timestamp:
   200→        return pending_meta.timestamp
   201→    return str(latest_issues_only.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   202→
   203→
   204→def _pending_import_scores_stale(
   205→    *,
   206→    order: list[str],
   207→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   208→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any] | None,
   209→    latest_trusted: dict[str, Any] | None,
   210→) -> bool:
   211→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in order:
   212→        return False
   213→    if latest_issues_only is None:
   214→        return True
   215→    if latest_trusted is None:
   216→        return False
   217→
   218→    latest_trusted_ts = str(latest_trusted.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   219→    compare_ts = _pending_compare_timestamp(pending_meta, latest_issues_only)
   220→    return bool(compare_ts and latest_trusted_ts and latest_trusted_ts >= compare_ts)
   221→

   231→        import_file=import_file,
   232→        import_payload=import_payload,
   233→        issues_only_audit=latest_issues_only,
   234→    ).to_dict()

   240→) -> bool:
   241→    """Return True when no unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain."""
   242→    if policy is not None:
   243→        return not policy.unscored_ids
   244→    return not stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
   245→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,

   249→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   250→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   251→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   252→    if item_id not in order:
   253→        order.append(item_id)
   254→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   255→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   256→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   257→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   258→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])
   259→
   260→
   261→def clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   262→    """Clear the ``previous_plan_start_scores`` sentinel.
   263→
   264→    Call this in import/scan pre-steps when a trusted import completes or
   265→    a cycle boundary resets.  The sentinel gates ``sync_communicate_score_needed``
   266→    — clearing it allows communicate-score to re-inject next cycle.
   267→    """
   268→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   269→
   270→
   271→_EMPTY = QueueSyncResult
   272→
   273→
   274→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   275→    plan: PlanModel,
   276→    state: StateModel,
   277→    *,
   278→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   279→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   280→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   281→
   282→    Injects when:
   283→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   284→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   285→
   286→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   287→    """
   288→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   289→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   290→
   291→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   292→        return _EMPTY()
   293→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   294→        return _EMPTY()
   295→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   296→
   297→
   298→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   299→    plan: PlanModel,
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   303→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   304→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   305→
   306→    Only injects when:
   307→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   308→    - At least one objective issue exists
   309→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   310→    - No triage stages are pending
   311→
   312→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   313→    """
   314→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   315→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   316→
   317→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   318→        return _EMPTY()
   319→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   320→        return _EMPTY()
   321→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   322→        return _EMPTY()
   323→
   324→    if not has_objective_backlog(state, policy):
   325→        return _EMPTY()
   326→
   327→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   328→
   329→
   330→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   331→    plan: PlanModel,
   332→    state: StateModel,
   333→    *,
   334→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   335→    import_file: str | None = None,
   336→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   337→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   338→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   339→
   340→    Only injects when:
   341→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   342→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   343→
   344→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   345→    """
   346→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   347→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   348→    refresh_state = _ensure_refresh_state(plan)
   349→    pending_meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   350→        refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   351→    )
   352→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   353→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   354→
   355→    if _pending_import_scores_stale(
   356→        order=order,
   357→        pending_meta=pending_meta,
   358→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   359→        latest_trusted=latest_trusted,
   360→    ):
   361→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   362→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   363→
   364→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   365→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   366→            _record_pending_import_scores(
   367→                refresh_state,
   368→                import_file=import_file,
   369→                import_payload=import_payload,
   370→                latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   371→            )
   372→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   373→        return _EMPTY()
   374→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   375→        return _EMPTY()
   376→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   377→    _record_pending_import_scores(
   378→        refresh_state,
   379→        import_file=import_file,
   380→        import_payload=import_payload,
   381→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   382→    )
   383→    return result
   384→
   385→
   386→class ScoreSnapshot:
   387→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   388→
   389→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   390→
   391→    def __init__(
   392→        self,
   393→        *,
   394→        strict: float | None,
   395→        overall: float | None,
   396→        objective: float | None,
   397→        verified: float | None,
   398→    ) -> None:
   399→        self.strict = strict
   400→        self.overall = overall
   401→        self.objective = objective
   402→        self.verified = verified
   403→
   404→
   405→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   406→    plan: PlanModel,
   407→    state: StateModel,
   408→    *,
   409→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   410→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   411→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   412→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   413→
   414→    Injects when:
   415→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims)
   416→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   417→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   418→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent)
   419→
   420→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   421→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   422→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   423→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   424→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   425→    re-inject.
   426→    """
   427→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   428→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   429→
   430→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   431→        return _EMPTY()
   432→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   433→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   434→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan:
   435→        return _EMPTY()
   436→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   437→        return _EMPTY()
   438→
   439→    if current_scores is not None:
   440→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   441→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   442→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   443→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   444→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   445→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   446→
   447→
   448→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   449→    plan: PlanModel,
   450→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   451→) -> None:
   452→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   453→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   454→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   455→        return
   456→    if scores.strict is None:
   457→        return
   458→
   459→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   460→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   461→        "strict": scores.strict,
   462→        "overall": scores.overall,
   463→        "objective": scores.objective,
   464→        "verified": scores.verified,
   465→    }
   466→
   467→
   468→__all__ = [
   469→    "PendingImportScoresMeta",
   470→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   471→    "clear_score_communicated_sentinel",
   472→    "import_scores_meta_matches",
   473→    "pending_import_scores_meta",
   474→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   475→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   476→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   477→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   478→]
   479→
```

```
    69→    _print_stale_review_prunes(stale_pruned)
    70→    _print_review_import_footer(
    71→        workflow_injected=workflow_injected,
    72→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
    73→    )
    74→
    75→
    76→def _print_new_review_items(state: dict, new_ids: list[str]) -> None:
    77→    if not new_ids:
    78→        return
    79→    print(
    80→        colorize(
    81→            f"  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review work item(s) added to queue.",
    82→            "bold",
    83→        )
    84→    )
    85→    issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
    86→    for finding_id in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
    87→        finding = issues.get(finding_id, {})
    88→        print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(finding_id)}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
    89→    if len(new_ids) > 10:
    90→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
    91→
    92→
    93→def _print_stale_review_prunes(stale_pruned: list[str]) -> None:
    94→    if not stale_pruned:
    95→        return
    96→    print(
    97→        colorize(
    98→            f"  Plan updated: {len(stale_pruned)} stale review work item(s) removed from queue.",
    99→            "bold",
   100→        )
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def _print_review_import_footer(
   105→    *,
   106→    workflow_injected: bool,
   107→    triage_injected: bool,
   108→) -> None:
   109→    print()
   110→    print(
   111→        colorize(
   112→            "  Review queue sync completed. Workflow follow-up may be front-loaded.",
   113→            "dim",
   114→        )
   115→    )
   116→    print()
   117→    print(colorize("  View execution queue:  desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
   118→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   119→    print(colorize("  View broader backlog:  desloppify backlog", "dim"))
   120→    print()
   121→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:", "yellow"))
   122→    print(colorize("    Run:    desloppify next", "yellow"))
   123→    if triage_injected and not workflow_injected:
   124→        print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
   125→        print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
   126→        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
   127→    print(
   128→        colorize(
   129→            "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",
   130→            "dim",
   131→        )
   132→    )
   133→
   134→
   135→def _review_delta_present(diff: dict) -> bool:
   136→    return any(
   137→        int(diff.get(key, 0) or 0) > 0
   138→        for key in ("new", "reopened", "auto_resolved")
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def _print_workflow_injected_message(workflow_injected_ids: list[str]) -> None:
   143→    if not workflow_injected_ids:
   144→        return
   145→    injected_parts = [f"`{workflow_id}`" for workflow_id in workflow_injected_ids]
   146→    print(
   147→        colorize(
   148→            f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   149→            "cyan",
   150→        )
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→def _build_import_sync_inputs(
   155→    diff: dict,
   156→    import_payload: NormalizedReviewImportPayload | None,
   157→) -> _ImportSyncInputs:
   158→    assessment_keys = (
   159→        imported_assessment_keys(import_payload)
   160→        if isinstance(import_payload, dict)
   161→        else set()
   162→    )
   163→    return _ImportSyncInputs(
   164→        has_review_issue_delta=_review_delta_present(diff),
   165→        assessment_keys=frozenset(assessment_keys),
   166→        covered_ids=tuple(
   167→            f"subjective::{dim_key}"
   168→            for dim_key in sorted(assessment_keys)
   169→        ),
   170→    )
   171→
   172→
   173→def _sync_review_delta(
   174→    plan: dict,
   175→    state: dict,
   176→    sync_inputs: _ImportSyncInputs,
   177→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   178→    if not sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta:
   179→        return None
   180→    return sync_plan_after_review_import(
   181→        plan,
   182→        state,
   183→        inject_triage=False,
   184→    )
   185→
   186→
   187→def _append_review_import_sync_log(
   188→    plan: dict,
   189→    diff: dict,
   190→    import_result: ReviewImportSyncResult | None,
   191→    import_scores_result,
   192→    pipeline_result: ReconcileResult,
   193→    *,
   194→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   195→) -> None:
   196→    if not (
   197→        import_result is not None
   198→        or import_scores_result.changes
   199→        or pipeline_result.dirty
   200→        or covered_ids
   201→    ):
   202→        return
   203→    subjective = pipeline_result.subjective
   204→    triage = pipeline_result.triage
   205→    append_log_entry(
   206→        plan,
   207→        "review_import_sync",
   208→        actor="system",
   209→        detail={
   210→            "trigger": "review_import",
   211→            "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   212→            "added_to_queue": import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else [],
   213→            "workflow_injected_ids": pipeline_result.workflow_injected_ids,
   214→            "triage_injected": bool(triage and triage.injected),
   215→            "triage_injected_ids": list(triage.injected) if triage is not None else [],
   216→            "triage_deferred": bool(triage and triage.deferred),
   217→            "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   218→            "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   219→            "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   220→            "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   221→                import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   222→            ),
   223→            "covered_subjective": list(covered_ids),
   224→            "stale_sync_injected": sorted(subjective.injected) if subjective is not None else [],
   225→            "stale_sync_pruned": sorted(subjective.pruned) if subjective is not None else [],
   226→            "auto_cluster_changes": pipeline_result.auto_cluster_changes,
   227→            "import_scores_injected": list(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", []) or []),
   228→            "import_scores_pruned": list(getattr(import_scores_result, "pruned", []) or []),
   229→        },
   230→    )
   231→
   232→
   233→def sync_plan_after_import(
   234→    state: dict,
   235→    diff: dict,
   236→    assessment_mode: str,
   237→    *,
   238→    request: PlanImportSyncRequest | None = None,
   239→) -> None:
   240→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
   241→    try:
   242→        state_file = request.state_file if request is not None else None
   243→        config = request.config if request is not None else None
   244→        import_file = request.import_file if request is not None else None
   245→        import_payload = request.import_payload if request is not None else None
   246→
   247→        plan_path = None
   248→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   249→        if state_file is not None:
   250→            plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   251→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   252→            return
   253→
   254→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   255→        sync_inputs = _build_import_sync_inputs(diff, import_payload)
   256→        trusted = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   257→
   258→        import_result = _sync_review_delta(plan, state, sync_inputs)
   259→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   260→            plan,
   261→            state,
   262→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   263→            import_file=import_file,
   264→            import_payload=import_payload,
   265→        )
   266→        if trusted:
   267→            clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   268→
   269→        result = ReconcileResult()
   270→        if live_planned_queue_empty(plan):
   271→            result = reconcile_plan(plan, state, target_strict=target_strict)
   272→
   273→        dirty = bool(import_result is not None or import_scores_result.changes or result.dirty)
   274→        if dirty:
   275→            _append_review_import_sync_log(
   276→                plan,
   277→                diff,
   278→                import_result,
   279→                import_scores_result,
   280→                result,
   281→                covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids,
   282→            )
   283→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   284→
   285→        if import_result is not None:
   286→            _print_review_import_sync(
   287→                state,
   288→                import_result,
   289→                workflow_injected=bool(result.workflow_injected_ids),
   290→                triage_injected=bool(result.triage and result.triage.injected),
   291→            )
   292→        _print_workflow_injected_message(result.workflow_injected_ids)
   293→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   294→        print(
   295→            colorize(
   296→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   297→                "dim",
   298→            )
   299→        )
   300→
   301→
   302→__all__ = ["PlanImportSyncRequest", "sync_plan_after_import"]
   303→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — sync plan queue metadata after a scan merge."""
     2→

     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod

    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.scan_issue_reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan

    35→
    36→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    37→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used."""
    38→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    39→    synthetic = [item for item in order if is_synthetic_id(item)]
    40→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    41→        return False
    42→    for item in synthetic:
    43→        order.remove(item)
    44→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    45→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
    46→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    47→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    48→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    49→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    50→    count = len(synthetic)
    51→    if count:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(
    54→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    55→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    56→                "yellow",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→    return True
    60→
    61→
    62→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    63→    return bool(
    64→        plan.get("queue_order")
    65→        or plan.get("overrides")
    66→        or plan.get("clusters")
    67→        or plan.get("skipped")
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
    72→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    73→        return False
    74→    recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
    75→    if recon.resurfaced:
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize(
    78→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    79→                "cyan",
    80→            )
    81→        )
    82→    return bool(recon.changes)
    83→
    84→
    85→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
    86→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
    87→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    88→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
    89→        return False
    90→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
    91→        return False
    92→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    93→    if scores.strict is None:
    94→        return False
    95→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
    96→        "strict": scores.strict,
    97→        "overall": scores.overall,
    98→        "objective": scores.objective,
    99→        "verified": scores.verified,
   100→    }
   101→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   102→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→def _has_objective_cycle(
   107→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   108→    plan: dict[str, object],
   109→) -> bool | None:
   110→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   111→    try:
   112→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_breakdown
   113→
   114→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   115→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   116→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   117→        return None
   118→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   119→
   120→
   121→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   122→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   123→) -> bool:
   124→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   125→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   126→        return False
   127→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   128→        return False
   129→
   130→    try:
   131→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   132→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   133→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   134→            score_display_mode,
   135→        )
   136→
   137→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   138→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   139→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   140→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   141→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   142→        return False
   143→    if not queue_empty:
   144→        return False
   145→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   146→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   147→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   148→    return True
   149→
   150→
   151→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   152→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   153→    plan: dict[str, object],
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Record that the cycle's post-review scan has completed."""
   156→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores") and current_unscored_ids(state):
   157→        return False
   158→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   159→        return False
   160→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   161→        plan,
   162→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   163→    )
   164→
   165→
   166→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   167→    plan: dict[str, object],
   168→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   169→) -> bool:

   171→    if seeded:
   172→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   173→        return True
   174→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   175→    if cleared:
   176→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   177→    return cleared
   178→
   179→
   180→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   181→    plan: dict[str, object],
   182→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   183→) -> bool:
   184→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   185→    if changed:

   187→            plan,

   189→            actor="system",
   190→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   191→        )
   192→    return changed
   193→

   199→) -> bool:

   201→    return bool(_apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state))

   204→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   205→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain."""
   206→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   207→        return False
   208→    return not live_planned_queue_empty(plan)
   209→
   210→
   211→def _display_reconcile_results(
   212→    result: ReconcileResult,
   213→    plan: dict,
   214→    *,
   215→    mid_cycle: bool,
   216→) -> None:
   217→    subjective = result.subjective
   218→    if subjective and subjective.resurfaced:
   219→        print(
   220→            colorize(
   221→                f"  Plan: {len(subjective.resurfaced)} skipped subjective dimension(s) resurfaced — never reviewed.",
   222→                "yellow",
   223→            )
   224→        )
   225→    if subjective and subjective.pruned:
   226→        print(
   227→            colorize(
   228→                f"  Plan: {len(subjective.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   229→                "cyan",
   230→            )
   231→        )
   232→    if subjective and subjective.injected:
   233→        print(
   234→            colorize(
   235→                f"  Plan: {len(subjective.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   236→                "cyan",
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→    if mid_cycle and not result.auto_cluster_changes:
   240→        print(
   241→            colorize(
   242→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to preserve queue state.",
   243→                "dim",
   244→            )
   245→        )
   246→    if result.create_plan and result.create_plan.injected:
   247→        print(
   248→            colorize(
   249→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   250→                "cyan",
   251→            )
   252→        )
   253→    if (
   254→        result.triage
   255→        and result.triage.deferred
   256→        and plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_recommended")
   257→    ):
   258→        print(
   259→            colorize(
   260→                "  Plan: review work items changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   261→                "dim",
   262→            )
   263→        )
   264→    if result.triage and result.triage.injected:
   265→        print(
   266→            colorize(
   267→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review work items changed since last triage.",
   268→                "cyan",
   269→            )
   270→        )
   271→
   272→
   273→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   274→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   275→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   276→    try:
   277→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   278→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   279→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   280→        return
   281→
   282→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   283→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   284→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   285→
   286→    boundary_crossed = live_planned_queue_empty(plan)
   287→    if boundary_crossed:
   288→        result = reconcile_plan(
   289→            plan,
   290→            runtime.state,
   291→            target_strict=target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config),
   292→        )
   293→        _display_reconcile_results(
   294→            result,
   295→            plan,
   296→            mid_cycle=_is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, runtime.state) or force_rescan,
   297→        )
   298→        dirty = result.dirty or dirty
   299→
   300→    if not force_rescan:
   301→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   302→            dirty = True
   303→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   304→            dirty = True
   305→
   306→    if dirty:
   307→        try:
   308→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   309→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   310→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   311→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan reconciliation for stale or disappeared issue references."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.lifecycle import clear_focus_if_cluster_empty
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.meta import append_log_entry
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.skip import resurface_stale_skips
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    15→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    16→    PlanModel,
    17→    SupersededEntry,
    18→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import skip_kind_state_status
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults, utc_now
    22→
    23→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass
    27→class ReconcileResult:
    28→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    29→
    30→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    31→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    changes: int = 0
    35→
    36→
    37→def _find_candidates(
    38→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    39→) -> list[str]:
    40→    """Find alive issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    41→    candidates: list[str] = []
    42→    for fid, issue in (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).items():
    43→        if issue.get("status") not in _ALIVE_STATUSES:
    44→            continue
    45→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    46→            candidates.append(fid)
    47→    return candidates
    48→
    49→
    50→_ALIVE_STATUSES = frozenset({"open", "deferred", "triaged_out"})
    51→
    52→
    53→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True if the issue exists and is actionable (open/deferred/triaged_out)."""
    55→    issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(issue_id)
    56→    if issue is None:
    57→        return False
    58→    return issue.get("status") in _ALIVE_STATUSES
    59→
    60→
    61→def _supersede_id(
    62→    plan: PlanModel,
    63→    state: StateModel,
    64→    issue_id: str,
    65→    now: str,
    66→) -> bool:
    67→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    68→    issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(issue_id)
    69→    detector = ""
    70→    file = ""
    71→    summary = ""
    72→    if issue:
    73→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    74→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    75→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    76→
    77→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    78→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    79→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    80→
    81→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    82→        "original_id": issue_id,
    83→        "original_detector": detector,
    84→        "original_file": file,
    85→        "original_summary": summary,
    86→        "status": "superseded",
    87→        "superseded_at": now,
    88→        "remapped_to": None,
    89→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    90→    }
    91→
    92→    # Preserve any existing override note
    93→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    94→    if override_note:
    95→        entry["note"] = override_note
    96→
    97→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    98→
    99→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
   100→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   101→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   102→    if issue_id in order:
   103→        order.remove(issue_id)
   104→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   105→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   106→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   107→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   108→        if issue_id in ids:
   109→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   110→
   111→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   112→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   113→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   114→        override["cluster"] = None
   115→        override["updated_at"] = now
   116→
   117→    return True
   118→
   119→
   120→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   121→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   122→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   123→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   124→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   125→
   126→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   127→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   128→        try:
   129→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   130→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   131→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   132→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   133→                to_prune.append(fid)
   134→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   135→            to_prune.append(fid)
   136→
   137→    for fid in to_prune:
   138→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   139→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   140→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   141→
   142→    return to_prune
   143→
   144→
   145→def _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
   146→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   147→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   148→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
   150→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   151→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   152→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   153→    return {
   154→        fid for fid in referenced_ids - already_superseded
   155→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   156→    }
   157→
   158→
   159→def _prune_existing_superseded_references(
   160→    plan: PlanModel,
   161→    *,
   162→    result: ReconcileResult,
   163→) -> None:
   164→    superseded_ids = {
   165→        fid for fid in plan.get("superseded", {})
   166→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid
   167→    }
   168→    if not superseded_ids:
   169→        return
   170→
   171→    changes = 0
   172→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   173→    kept_order = [fid for fid in order if fid not in superseded_ids]
   174→    if len(kept_order) != len(order):
   175→        changes += len(order) - len(kept_order)
   176→        order[:] = kept_order
   177→
   178→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   179→    for fid in list(skipped):
   180→        if fid in superseded_ids:
   181→            skipped.pop(fid, None)
   182→            changes += 1
   183→
   184→    promoted_before = len(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   185→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, superseded_ids)
   186→    changes += max(0, promoted_before - len(plan.get("promoted_ids", [])))
   187→
   188→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   189→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   190→        kept_issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in superseded_ids]
   191→        if len(kept_issue_ids) != len(issue_ids):
   192→            changes += len(issue_ids) - len(kept_issue_ids)
   193→            cluster["issue_ids"] = kept_issue_ids
   194→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
   195→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   196→                continue
   197→            refs = step.get("issue_refs", [])
   198→            kept_refs = [fid for fid in refs if fid not in superseded_ids]
   199→            if len(kept_refs) != len(refs):
   200→                changes += len(refs) - len(kept_refs)
   201→                step["issue_refs"] = kept_refs
   202→
   203→    for fid in superseded_ids:
   204→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   205→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   206→            override["cluster"] = None
   207→            changes += 1
   208→
   209→    result.changes += changes
   210→
   211→
   212→def _supersede_dead_references(
   213→    plan: PlanModel,
   214→    state: StateModel,
   215→    *,
   216→    referenced_ids: set[str],
   217→    now: str,
   218→    result: ReconcileResult,
   219→) -> None:
   220→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   221→        if _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   222→            continue
   223→        if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   224→            result.superseded.append(fid)
   225→            result.changes += 1
   226→
   227→
   228→def _complete_empty_manual_clusters(
   229→    plan: PlanModel,
   230→    *,
   231→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int],
   232→    result: ReconcileResult,
   233→) -> None:
   234→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   235→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   236→        if prev_size == 0:
   237→            continue
   238→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   239→        if cluster is None or len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) != 0:
   240→            continue
   241→        result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   242→        append_log_entry(
   243→            plan,
   244→            "cluster_done",
   245→            issue_ids=[],
   246→            cluster_name=name,
   247→            actor="system",
   248→            detail={"reason": "cluster members no longer actionable in state"},
   249→        )
   250→        result.changes += 1
   251→
   252→
   253→def _reconcile_epic_clusters(
   254→    plan: PlanModel,
   255→    state: StateModel,
   256→    *,
   257→    result: ReconcileResult,
   258→) -> None:
   259→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   260→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   261→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   262→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   263→            continue
   264→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   265→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   266→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   267→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   268→            result.changes += 1
   269→        if not alive_ids:
   270→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   271→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   272→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   273→        result.changes += 1
   274→
   275→
   276→def _sync_skipped_issue_statuses(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> None:
   277→    """Sync state status for skipped issues that are still 'open'.
   278→
   279→    Ensures state is authoritative: temporary → deferred, triaged_out → triaged_out.
   280→    Runs on every reconcile so existing data gets migrated on next scan.
   281→    """
   282→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   283→    issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   284→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   285→        issue = issues.get(fid)
   286→        if issue is None or issue.get("status") != "open":
   287→            continue
   288→        kind = str(entry.get("kind", ""))
   289→        target_status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
   290→        if target_status and target_status != "open":
   291→            issue["status"] = target_status
   292→
   293→
   294→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   295→    plan: PlanModel,
   296→    state: StateModel,
   297→) -> ReconcileResult:
   298→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   299→
   300→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   301→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   302→    """
   303→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   304→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   305→    result = ReconcileResult()
   306→    now = utc_now()
   307→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   308→
   309→    _prune_existing_superseded_references(plan, result=result)
   310→    referenced_ids = _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan)
   311→
   312→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   313→    # clusters that become empty after state reconciliation.
   314→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   315→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   316→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   317→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   318→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   319→    }
   320→
   321→    # Sync state status for issues in plan.skipped that are still "open" in state.
   322→    # This migrates existing data: temporary skips → deferred, triaged_out skips → triaged_out.
   323→    _sync_skipped_issue_statuses(plan, state)
   324→
   325→    _supersede_dead_references(
   326→        plan,
   327→        state,
   328→        referenced_ids=referenced_ids,
   329→        now=now,
   330→        result=result,
   331→    )
   332→    _complete_empty_manual_clusters(plan, pre_sizes=pre_sizes, result=result)
   333→    _reconcile_epic_clusters(plan, state, result=result)
   334→
   335→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
   336→
   337→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   338→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   339→
   340→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   341→    if resurfaced:
   342→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   343→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   344→        # Reopen resurfaced issues in state (they were deferred)
   345→        issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   346→        for fid in resurfaced:
   347→            issue = issues.get(fid)
   348→            if issue and issue.get("status") == "deferred":
   349→                issue["status"] = "open"
   350→
   351→    # Prune old superseded entries
   352→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   353→    result.pruned = pruned
   354→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   355→
   356→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   357→    if result.changes > 0:
   358→        append_log_entry(
   359→            plan,
   360→            "reconcile",
   361→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   362→            actor="system",
   363→            detail={
   364→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   365→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   366→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   367→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   368→            },
   369→        )
   370→
   371→    return result
   372→
   373→__all__ = [
   374→    "ReconcileResult",
   375→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   376→]
   377→
```

```
   160→def _cluster_header_bits(item: dict) -> tuple[str, str, str, list[dict]]:
   161→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   162→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   163→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   164→    done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))

   171→def _render_cluster_steps(action_steps: list[dict]) -> None:

   174→    show_count = min(3, len(action_steps))
   175→    for i, step in enumerate(action_steps[:show_count], 1):
   176→        marker = "[x]" if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("done") else "[ ]"
   177→        print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
   178→    remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count
   179→    if remaining > 0:
   180→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more — drill in to view all", "dim"))
   181→
   182→
   183→def _render_cluster_priority(dep_order: object) -> None:
   184→    if dep_order is not None and dep_order <= 2:
   185→        print(colorize("  Priority: complete before other clusters", "cyan"))
   186→
   187→
   188→def _render_cluster_primary_action(item: dict) -> None:
   189→    autofix_hint = item.get("autofix_hint")
   190→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   191→    if autofix_hint:
   192→        print(colorize(f"\n  Try auto first: {autofix_hint}", "cyan"))
   193→        print(colorize("  If auto finds 0, drill into individual issues:", "dim"))
   194→    if primary_command:
   195→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   196→
   197→
   198→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   199→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   200→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   201→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   202→    is_transient = bool(item.get("cluster_transient"))
   203→    cluster_name, type_label, suffix, action_steps = _cluster_header_bits(item)
   204→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{suffix})", "bold"))
   205→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   206→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   207→
   208→    _render_cluster_steps(action_steps)
   209→    _render_cluster_priority(item.get("dependency_order"))
   210→
   211→    members = item.get("members", [])
   212→    if members:
   213→        _render_cluster_files(members)
   214→        _render_cluster_sample(members)
   215→
   216→    _render_cluster_primary_action(item)
   217→
   218→    if is_transient:
   219→        print(
   220→            colorize(
   221→                "  Note: complete the review batch, import it, then rescan — no cluster resolve step is needed.",
   222→                "dim",
   223→            )
   224→        )
   225→        return
   226→
   227→    if is_optional:
   228→        _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   229→        return
   230→
   231→    _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   232→
   233→
   234→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   235→    del explain
   236→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   237→    print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   238→    if total > 5:
   239→        print(colorize("  (Skip items only when explicitly requested.)", "dim"))
   240→
   241→
   242→def show_empty_queue(
   243→    queue: dict,
   244→    strict: float | None,
   245→    *,
   246→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   247→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   248→) -> bool:
   249→    del target_strict
   250→    if queue.get("items"):
   251→        return False
   252→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   253→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   254→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   255→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   256→        print(colorize(
   257→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   258→            "cyan",
   259→        ))
   260→        print(colorize(
   261→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   262→            "dim",
   263→        ))
   264→        return True
   265→
   266→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   267→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   268→    return True
   269→
   270→
   271→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   272→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   273→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   274→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   275→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   276→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   277→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) and (plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   278→        tag_str += " [plan]"
   279→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   280→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   281→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   282→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   283→
```

```
   151→    """Show queue block when score is not frozen (LIVE or PHASE_TRANSITION)."""

   154→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, plan_start_strict)
   155→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:

   164→def _render_subjective_bottleneck(dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   165→    try:
   166→        health_breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   167→        subjective_drag = sum(
   168→            float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   169→            for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   170→            if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   171→        )
   172→        mechanical_drag = sum(
   173→            float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   174→            for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   175→            if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   176→        )
   177→        if subjective_drag <= mechanical_drag or subjective_drag <= 5.0:
   178→            return
   179→        print(colorize(
   180→            f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   181→            f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   182→            "yellow",
   183→        ))
   184→        print(colorize(
   185→            "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   186→            "`desloppify review --prepare` and follow your "
   187→            "skill doc's review workflow to re-score.",
   188→            "yellow",
   189→        ))
   190→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   191→        log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   192→
   193→
   194→def _subjective_summary_parts(
   195→    *,
   196→    followup,
   197→    unassessed_subjective: list[str],
   198→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   199→    issues_scoped: dict,
   200→    coverage_open: int,
   201→) -> list[str]:
   202→    parts: list[str] = []
   203→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   204→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   205→    stale_count = sum(1 for entry in subjective_entries if entry.get("stale"))
   206→    open_review_count = sum(
   207→        1
   208→        for issue in issues_scoped.values()
   209→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") == "review"
   210→    )
   211→    if low_dims:
   212→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   213→    if stale_count:
   214→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   215→    if unassessed_count:
   216→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   217→    if open_review_count:
   218→        parts.append(
   219→            f"{open_review_count} review work item{'s' if open_review_count != 1 else ''} open"
   220→        )
   221→    if coverage_open > 0:
   222→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   223→    return parts
   224→
   225→
   226→def render_followup_nudges(
   227→    state: dict,
   228→    dim_scores: dict,
   229→    issues_scoped: dict,
   230→    *,
   231→    strict_score: float | None,
   232→    target_strict_score: float,
   233→    queue_total: int = 0,
   234→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   235→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   236→) -> None:
   237→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   238→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   239→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   240→        state,
   241→        subjective_entries,
   242→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   243→        max_quality_items=3,
   244→        max_integrity_items=5,
   245→    )
   246→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   247→    rendered_frozen = _render_frozen_queue_status(
   248→        strict_score=strict_score,
   249→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   250→        breakdown=breakdown,
   251→    )
   252→    if not rendered_frozen:
   253→        _render_north_star(
   254→            strict_score=strict_score,
   255→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   256→        )
   257→    _render_live_queue_block(
   258→        breakdown=breakdown,
   259→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Subjective bottleneck banner — only shown when the objective queue is
   263→    # clear.  While objective items remain, the queue is the single authority
   264→    # on what to work on next; no need to distract with subjective advice.
   265→    objective_remaining = breakdown.objective_actionable if breakdown else queue_total
   266→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores and objective_remaining <= 0:
   267→        _render_subjective_bottleneck(dim_scores)
   268→
   269→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   270→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   271→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   272→
   273→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   274→    if queue_total > 10:
   275→        print(colorize(

   277→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   278→            "dim",
   279→        ))
   280→
   281→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   282→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   283→        issues_scoped
   284→    )
   285→    parts = _subjective_summary_parts(

   287→        unassessed_subjective=unassessed_subjective,
   288→        subjective_entries=subjective_entries,
   289→        issues_scoped=issues_scoped,
   290→        coverage_open=coverage_open,
   291→    )
   292→    if parts:
   293→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   294→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
```
