1→"""Internal sync helpers for auto-cluster regeneration.
     2→
     3→Creates and updates auto-clusters from current queue state.  Does NOT
     4→manage queue membership — ``sync_subjective_dimensions`` is the sole authority
     5→on which subjective IDs belong in ``queue_order``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.context import (
    14→    has_objective_backlog as _has_objective_backlog,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster_sync_issue import (
    17→    _sync_auto_cluster,
    18→    sync_issue_clusters,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    21→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.auto_prune import prune_stale_clusters
    23→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import (
    24→    current_under_target_ids,
    25→    current_unscored_ids,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    28→
    29→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    30→_REVIEW_REFRESH_KEY = "subjective::review-refresh"
    31→_REVIEW_REFRESH_NAME = "auto/subjective-review"
    32→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    33→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    34→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    35→_MIN_REVIEW_REFRESH_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    36→
    37→
    38→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    39→class SubjectiveClusterSpec:
    40→    cluster_key: str
    41→    cluster_name: str
    42→    member_ids: list[str]
    43→    description: str
    44→    action: str
    45→    optional: bool = False
    46→
    47→
    48→def _subjective_state_sets(
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    *,
    51→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    52→    target_strict: float,
    53→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
    54→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""
    55→    if policy is not None:
    56→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
    57→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
    58→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
    59→    else:
    60→        unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
    61→        stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    62→            state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    63→        )
    64→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
    65→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids
    66→
    67→
    68→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    69→    """Return skipped subjective IDs so auto-clusters don't re-queue them."""
    70→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    71→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    72→        return set()
    73→    return {
    74→        str(fid)
    75→        for fid in skipped
    76→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    77→    }
    78→
    79→
    80→def _subjective_cli_keys(issue_ids: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    81→    return [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in issue_ids]
    82→
    83→
    84→def _subjective_action(issue_ids: list[str]) -> str:
    85→    return "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(_subjective_cli_keys(issue_ids))
    86→
    87→
    88→def _sync_subjective_cluster(
    89→    *,
    90→    plan: dict,
    91→    clusters: dict,
    92→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
    93→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
    94→    cluster_key: str,
    95→    cluster_name: str,
    96→    member_ids: list[str],
    97→    description: str,
    98→    action: str,
    99→    now: str,
   100→    optional: bool = False,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Sync one subjective auto-cluster when it has enough members."""
   103→    min_size = _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE
   104→    if cluster_key == _UNSCORED_KEY:
   105→        min_size = _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE
   106→    elif cluster_key == _REVIEW_REFRESH_KEY:
   107→        min_size = _MIN_REVIEW_REFRESH_CLUSTER_SIZE
   108→    if len(member_ids) < min_size:
   109→        return 0
   110→    active_auto_keys.add(cluster_key)
   111→    sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   112→        plan,
   113→        clusters,
   114→        existing_by_key,
   115→        cluster_key=cluster_key,
   116→        cluster_name=cluster_name,
   117→        member_ids=member_ids,
   118→        description=description,
   119→        action=action,
   120→        now=now,
   121→        optional=optional,
   122→    )
   123→    return int(sync_result.changed)
   124→
   125→
   126→def _queue_subjective_members(
   127→    order: list[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    stale_state_ids: set[str],
   130→    under_target_ids: set[str],
   131→    unscored_state_ids: set[str],
   132→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], int]:
   133→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   134→        fid
   135→        for fid in order
   136→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   137→    )
   138→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   139→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   140→    )
   141→    stale_review_ids = sorted(stale_state_ids - unscored_state_ids)
   142→    review_refresh_ids = sorted((stale_state_ids | under_target_ids) - unscored_state_ids)
   143→    return unscored_queue_ids, review_refresh_ids, len(stale_review_ids)
   144→
   145→
   146→def _subjective_cluster_specs(
   147→    *,
   148→    unscored_queue_ids: list[str],
   149→    review_refresh_queue_ids: list[str],
   150→    stale_review_count: int,
   151→    skipped_subjective_ids: set[str],
   152→    has_objective_items: bool,
   153→) -> list[SubjectiveClusterSpec]:
   154→    specs = [
   155→        SubjectiveClusterSpec(
   156→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   157→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   158→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   159→            description=(
   160→                f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   161→            ),
   162→            action=_subjective_action(unscored_queue_ids),
   163→        ),
   164→    ]
   165→    if has_objective_items:
   166→        return specs
   167→
   168→    filtered_review_refresh_ids = sorted(
   169→        fid for fid in review_refresh_queue_ids if fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   170→    )
   171→    stale_suffix = (
   172→        f" ({stale_review_count} stale)"
   173→        if stale_review_count
   174→        else ""
   175→    )
   176→    specs.append(
   177→        SubjectiveClusterSpec(
   178→            [REDACTED],
   179→            cluster_name=_REVIEW_REFRESH_NAME,
   180→            member_ids=filtered_review_refresh_ids,
   181→            description=(
   182→                f"Re-review {len(filtered_review_refresh_ids)} "
   183→                f"subjective dimensions below target{stale_suffix}"
   184→            ),
   185→            action=_subjective_action(filtered_review_refresh_ids),
   186→        )
   187→    )
   188→    return specs
   189→
   190→
   191→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   192→    plan: dict,
   193→    state: StateModel,
   194→    issues: dict,
   195→    clusters: dict,
   196→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   197→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   198→    now: str,
   199→    *,
   200→    target_strict: float,
   201→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   202→) -> int:
   203→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters.
   204→
   205→    Creates/updates cluster groupings from IDs currently in the queue.
   206→    Does NOT inject or evict IDs — that is sync_stale's responsibility.
   207→    """
   208→    changes = 0
   209→    skipped_subjective_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   210→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   211→
   212→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   213→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   214→    )
   215→    unscored_queue_ids, review_refresh_queue_ids, stale_review_count = _queue_subjective_members(
   216→        order,
   217→        stale_state_ids=stale_state_ids,
   218→        under_target_ids=under_target_ids,
   219→        unscored_state_ids=unscored_state_ids,
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   223→    for spec in _subjective_cluster_specs(
   224→        unscored_queue_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   225→        review_refresh_queue_ids=review_refresh_queue_ids,
   226→        stale_review_count=stale_review_count,
   227→        skipped_subjective_ids=skipped_subjective_ids,
   228→        has_objective_items=has_objective_items,
   229→    ):
   230→        changes += _sync_subjective_cluster(
   231→            plan=plan,
   232→            clusters=clusters,
   233→            existing_by_key=existing_by_key,
   234→            active_auto_keys=active_auto_keys,
   235→            [REDACTED],
   236→            cluster_name=spec.cluster_name,
   237→            member_ids=spec.member_ids,
   238→            description=spec.description,
   239→            action=spec.action,
   240→            now=now,
   241→            optional=spec.optional,
   242→        )
   243→
   244→    return changes
   245→
   246→
   247→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   248→
```

    65→
    66→def triage_phase_banner(
    67→    plan: PlanModel,
    68→    state: dict | None = None,
    69→    *,
    70→    snapshot: TriageSnapshot | None = None,
    71→) -> str:
    72→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    73→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    74→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    75→    run_hint = (
    76→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    77→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    78→    )
    79→    resolved_state = state or {}
    80→    resolved_snapshot = snapshot or build_triage_snapshot(plan, resolved_state)
    81→
    82→    if not resolved_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
    83→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
    84→        if undispositioned:
    85→            return (
    86→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    87→                f"{undispositioned} review work item(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    88→                f"{run_hint}"
    89→            )
    90→        if resolved_snapshot.is_triage_stale or meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    91→            return (
    92→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review work items changed since last triage. "
    93→                f"{run_hint}"
    94→            )
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    if resolved_state and has_objective_backlog(resolved_state, None):
    98→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
    99→        if undispositioned:
   100→            return (
   101→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
   102→                f"{undispositioned} review work item(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
   103→                f"{run_hint}"
   104→            )
   105→        return (
   106→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
   107→            "is complete."
   108→        )
   109→    progress = resolved_snapshot.progress
   110→    if progress.completed_count:
   111→        total_stages = len(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   112→        return (
   113→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({progress.completed_count}/{total_stages} stages recorded) — "
   114→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
   115→        )
   116→    return (
   117→        "TRIAGE MODE — review work items need analysis before fixing. "
   118→        f"{run_hint}"
   119→    )
   120→
   121→__all__ = [

1→"""Canonical triage coverage and status snapshot helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import TRIAGE_IDS, is_synthetic_id
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.playbook import TriageProgress, compute_triage_progress
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→
    14→def _normalized_issue_id_list(raw_ids: object) -> list[str]:
    15→    normalized: list[str] = []
    16→    seen: set[str] = set()
    17→    if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
    18→        return normalized
    19→    for raw_id in raw_ids:
    20→        if not isinstance(raw_id, str):
    21→            continue
    22→        issue_id = raw_id.strip()
    23→        if not issue_id or issue_id in seen:
    24→            continue
    25→        seen.add(issue_id)
    26→        normalized.append(issue_id)
    27→    return normalized
    28→
    29→
    30→def _cluster_issue_ids(cluster: Cluster) -> list[str]:
    31→    ordered: list[str] = []
    32→    seen: set[str] = set()
    33→
    34→    def _append(raw_ids: object) -> None:
    35→        if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
    36→            return
    37→        for raw_id in raw_ids:
    38→            if not isinstance(raw_id, str):
    39→                continue
    40→            issue_id = raw_id.strip()
    41→            if not issue_id or issue_id in seen:
    42→                continue
    43→            seen.add(issue_id)
    44→            ordered.append(issue_id)
    45→
    46→    _append(cluster.get("issue_ids"))
    47→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps")
    48→    if steps is not None:
    49→        for step in steps:
    50→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
    51→                continue
    52→            _append(step.get("issue_refs"))
    53→    return ordered
    54→
    55→
    56→def plan_review_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    57→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    58→    return [
    59→        issue_id
    60→        for issue_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
    61→        if isinstance(issue_id, str)
    62→        and not is_synthetic_id(issue_id)
    63→        and (issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::"))
    64→    ]
    65→
    66→
    67→def coverage_open_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    68→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    69→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    70→    active_ids = _normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids"))
    71→    if active_ids:
    72→        return set(active_ids)
    73→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    74→    review_ids = open_review_ids(state)
    75→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    76→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    77→    return review_ids
    78→
    79→
    80→def active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel | None = None) -> set[str]:
    81→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    82→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    83→    active_ids = _normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids"))
    84→    if active_ids:
    85→        return set(active_ids)
    86→    if state is None:
    87→        return set()
    88→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    89→
    90→
    91→def _explicit_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
    92→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    93→    return set(_normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")))
    94→
    95→
    96→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel | None = None) -> set[str]:
    97→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    98→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    99→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
   100→        return frozen_ids
   101→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
   102→
   103→
   104→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel | None = None) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   106→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   107→    if not target_ids:
   108→        return []
   109→
   110→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   111→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   112→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   113→            continue
   114→        covered_ids.update(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   115→
   116→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   117→    covered_ids.update(issue_id for issue_id in skipped if isinstance(issue_id, str))
   118→
   119→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   120→    covered_ids.update(_normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("dismissed_ids")))
   121→
   122→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions", {})
   123→    for issue_id, disposition in dispositions.items():
   124→        if disposition.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto" and issue_id in skipped:
   125→            covered_ids.add(issue_id)
   126→
   127→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   128→
   129→
   130→def triage_coverage(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   133→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
   134→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   135→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   136→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   137→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   138→        all_cluster_ids.update(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   139→    review_ids = list(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else plan_review_ids(plan)
   140→    organized = sum(1 for issue_id in review_ids if issue_id in all_cluster_ids)
   141→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   142→
   143→
   144→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
   145→    """Return manual clusters that currently own at least one issue."""
   146→    return [
   147→        name
   148→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   149→        if _cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   150→    ]
   151→
   152→
   153→def find_cluster_for(issue_id: str, clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> str | None:
   154→    """Return the owning cluster name for an issue ID, if any."""
   155→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   156→        if issue_id in _cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   157→            return name
   158→    return None
   159→
   160→
   161→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   162→class TriageSnapshot:
   163→    """Single shared triage-status snapshot for command surfaces."""
   164→
   165→    progress: TriageProgress
   166→    frozen_issue_ids: frozenset[str]
   167→    live_open_ids: frozenset[str]
   168→    new_since_triage_ids: frozenset[str]
   169→    undispositioned_ids: frozenset[str]
   170→    organized_count: int
   171→    total_in_scope: int
   172→    has_triage_in_queue: bool
   173→    is_triage_stale: bool
   174→    cycle_active: bool
   175→    triage_has_run: bool
   176→
   177→
   178→def build_triage_snapshot(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageSnapshot:
   179→    """Build a canonical triage snapshot from plan and state."""
   180→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   181→    triaged_ids = set(_normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("triaged_ids")))
   182→    frozen_ids = _explicit_active_triage_issue_ids(plan) & open_review_ids(state)
   183→    live_open = open_review_ids(state)
   184→    known_ids = triaged_ids | frozen_ids
   185→    new_since = live_open - known_ids if known_ids else set()
   186→    in_scope_ids = coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
   187→    organized_count, total_in_scope, _clusters = triage_coverage(
   188→        plan,
   189→        open_review_ids=in_scope_ids,
   190→    )
   191→
   192→    return TriageSnapshot(
   193→        progress=compute_triage_progress(meta.get("triage_stages", {})),
   194→        frozen_issue_ids=frozenset(frozen_ids),
   195→        live_open_ids=frozenset(live_open),
   196→        new_since_triage_ids=frozenset(new_since),
   197→        undispositioned_ids=frozenset(undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)),
   198→        organized_count=organized_count,
   199→        total_in_scope=total_in_scope,
   200→        has_triage_in_queue=bool(set(plan.get("queue_order", [])) & TRIAGE_IDS),
   201→        is_triage_stale=bool(new_since),
   202→        cycle_active=bool(frozen_ids),
   203→        triage_has_run=bool(triaged_ids),
   204→    )
   205→
   206→
   207→__all__ = [
   208→    "TriageSnapshot",
   209→    "active_triage_issue_ids",
   210→    "build_triage_snapshot",
   211→    "coverage_open_ids",
   212→    "find_cluster_for",
   213→    "live_active_triage_issue_ids",
   214→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   215→    "plan_review_ids",
   216→    "triage_coverage",
   217→    "undispositioned_triage_issue_ids",
   218→]
   219→
```

     4→

     6→
     7→TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (

     9→    ("reflect", "Form strategy & present to user"),

## Comprehensive Postflight Lifecycle Architecture Report

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete picture of how queue contents flow from scan through review, score, triage, and execute:

### **1. QUEUE SNAPSHOT & PHASE DETERMINATION** (`snapshot.py`)

The canonical entry point is `build_queue_snapshot()` which creates a `QueueSnapshot` dataclass. This is the single source of truth for:
- **Phase determination** via `_phase_for_snapshot()`
- **Queue partitioning** into 8 distinct collections:
  - `all_objective_items` — discovered issues that count toward backlog
  - `all_initial_review_items` — unassessed subjective dimensions (unscored)
  - `all_postflight_assessment_items` — stale/placeholder review items + assessment requests
  - `all_postflight_review_items` — triage findings (review::* / concerns::*)
  - `all_scan_items` — workflow scan/disposition items
  - `all_postflight_workflow_items` — score checkpoints, import scores, communicate-score, create-plan
  - `all_postflight_triage_items` — triage stage workflow items
  - `execution_items` — the actual queue for this invocation

**Phase Decision Logic** (`_phase_for_snapshot`):
1. **Fresh boundary** (no plan or `reset: true` in `plan_start_scores`): if initial-review items exist → `PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL`
2. **Postflight sticky** (plan has scores, no pending scan, in review/workflow/triage): 
   - If assessment items → `PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT`
   - If workflow items → `PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT`
   - If triage + review backlog → `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`
   - If review items → `PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT`
   - If triage items → `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`
3. **Opportunistic fallback** (no fresh boundary, no postflight sticky):
   - If anchored execution items → `PHASE_EXECUTE`
   - If scan items → `PHASE_SCAN`
   - Otherwise: sequential postflight order (assessment → workflow → triage → review)

**Key boundary checks**:
- `_is_fresh_boundary()`: checks if `plan.plan_start_scores` is empty or has `reset: true`
- `_merge_execution_candidates()`: merges explicit queue items with objective defaults, respects `live_planned_queue_ids()`

---

### **2. WORK QUEUE TYPES & ITEM BUILDERS** (`types.py`, `synthetic.py`, `synthetic_workflow.py`)

**Item Kinds**:
- `issue` — real scanned findings
- `cluster` — grouped/auto-clustered issues
- `workflow_stage` — triage stages (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check, commit)
- `workflow_action` — synthetic helpers (score-checkpoint, create-plan, import-scores, communicate-score, run-scan, deferred-disposition)
- `subjective_dimension` — re-review prompts for dims below target or unassessed

**Synthetic Item Builders**:

1. **`build_triage_stage_items()`**: Creates workflow_stage items for pending `triage::*` stage IDs
   - Returns empty list if no triage stages are pending
   - Builds `blocked_by` dependencies (stages that are still in queue and unconfirmed)
   - Reads from `plan.queue_order`, `plan.epic_triage_meta.triage_stages`

2. **`build_subjective_items()`**: Creates subjective_dimension items for dimensions below target
   - Reads from `state.dimension_scores`
   - Flags `initial_review: true` for unassessed/placeholder dimensions
   - Flags `initial_review: false` for stale or under-target assessed dimensions
   - Splits into initial vs. postflight candidates in `_subjective_partitions()`

3. **Workflow Action Builders**:
   - `build_run_scan_item()` — Only if `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` is True
   - `build_deferred_disposition_item()` — If plan has temporary skipped items
   - `build_score_checkpoint_item()` — If `WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID` in queue_order
   - `build_create_plan_item()` — If `WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID` in queue_order
   - `build_import_scores_item()` — If `WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID` in queue_order + metadata matches
   - `build_communicate_score_item()` — If `WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID` in queue_order

---

### **3. LIFECYCLE PHASE & REFRESH STATE** (implicit in `snapshot.py` + `refresh_lifecycle.py`)

**Persisted Lifecycle Phases** (coarse, stored in `plan.refresh_state.lifecycle_phase`):
- `scan` — post-scan reconciliation running
- `review` — review/assessment/triage phases (initial or postflight)
- `workflow` — workflow gates (score checkpoints, planning, imports)
- `triage` — triage stage orchestration (observe → commit)
- `execute` — draining objective queue

**Postflight Entry Point**: `live_planned_queue_empty(plan)` returns True when:
- `plan.queue_order` contains only synthetic items (`is_synthetic_id()`) or items in `plan.skipped`
- Once this is True, the boundary-triggered reconciliation pipeline activates

---

### **4. BOUNDARY-TRIGGERED RECONCILIATION PIPELINE** (`pipeline.py`)

`reconcile_plan()` is called when `live_planned_queue_empty(plan)` is True. It runs in sequence:

1. **`sync_subjective_dimensions()`** — Inject/prune subjective review items based on policy
   - Reads unscored/stale/under-target dimensions from policy
   - Manages `subjective::*` IDs in queue

2. **`auto_cluster_issues()`** — Regenerate auto-clusters from subjective queue members
   - Creates `auto/initial-review` cluster for unscored items
   - Creates `auto/subjective-review` cluster for stale/under-target items

3. **`sync_communicate_score_needed()`** — Inject communicate-score workflow item
   - Precondition: no unscored dims remain
   - Freezes `plan.plan_start_scores` → new baseline
   - Preserves old baseline in `previous_plan_start_scores` (for delta display)
   - Sets sentinel to prevent mid-cycle re-injection

4. **`sync_create_plan_needed()`** — Inject create-plan workflow item
   - Precondition: no unscored dims, no triage stages active, objective backlog exists

5. **`sync_triage_needed()`** — Inject/prune triage stages based on review issue changes
   - Guard: if objective backlog active, defer stages (set `triage_recommended` flag)
   - Tracks review issue hash; escalates deferral after N cycles
   - Prunes stale stages when no new issues since injection

6. **`_persist_lifecycle()`** — Update `plan.refresh_state.lifecycle_phase`

All results logged to `plan.execution_log` with action names like `"sync_subjective"`, `"sync_triage"`, etc.

---

### **5. REVIEW IMPORT SYNC** (`plan_sync.py`)

`sync_plan_after_import()` runs post-review import in one load/save cycle:

1. **`_sync_review_delta()`** — If issue counts changed (new/reopened/resolved)
   - Calls `sync_plan_after_review_import()` with `inject_triage=False`
   - Prunes stale review items, injects new ones

2. **`sync_import_scores_needed()`** — If import was `issues_only` mode
   - Injects `workflow::import-scores` to prompt user for trusted re-import
   - Records pending metadata (file, packet_sha256) for re-import validation

3. **`reconcile_plan()` (if `live_planned_queue_empty()`)** — Run full boundary pipeline

4. **Logging** — Appends detail to execution_log with counts of all mutations

**Key contract**: Import scores are cleared at cycle boundaries to reset the communicate-score sentinel.

---

### **6. POST-SCAN RECONCILIATION** (`plan_reconcile.py`)

`reconcile_plan_post_scan()` runs during scan command finalization:

1. **`_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan()`** — If `--force-rescan` used
   - Removes all synthetic items from queue
   - Clears `plan_start_scores` and sentinels

2. **`_apply_plan_reconciliation()`** — If plan has user content
   - Calls `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` to supersede dead references + resurface stale skips

3. **Boundary crossing check**: `live_planned_queue_empty(plan)`
   - If True, run full `reconcile_plan()` pipeline

4. **Score lifecycle**:
   - `_seed_plan_start_scores()` — Freeze baseline when cycle begins (objective work exists)
   - `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` — Unfreeze when queue drains (all work done)
   - `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready()` — Record completion once import scores are trusted

**Key insight**: Plan-start scores freeze only when an objective cycle is detected. Once the cycle drains and communicate-score has been resolved, the baseline is cleared and score unfreezes for reveal.

---

### **7. ISSUE RECONCILIATION** (`scan_issue_reconcile.py`)

`reconcile_plan_after_scan()` handles stale/dead references:

1. **Prune existing superseded references** — Remove already-superseded IDs from queue/clusters
2. **Find disappeared issues** — Check if queued/clustered IDs are still alive in state
3. **Supersede dead references** — Archive disappeared issues with remapping candidates
4. **Complete empty clusters** — Mark manual clusters empty when all members are gone
5. **Resurrect stale temporary skips** — If deferred skip review_after period elapsed, reopen the issue
6. **Prune old superseded entries** — Remove entries older than 90 days

---

### **8. TRIAGE SNAPSHOT & COVERAGE** (`snapshot.py`, `playbook.py`)

`build_triage_snapshot()` provides:
- `progress` — Stage completion tracking (observed/confirmed counts)
- `frozen_issue_ids` — Active triage cycle's frozen issue set
- `live_open_ids` — Current open review issues
- `new_since_triage_ids` — Issues added since last triage run
- `undispositioned_ids` — Issues without cluster/skip/dismiss coverage
- `has_triage_in_queue` — True if any `triage::*` stage ID in queue_order
- `is_triage_stale` — True if new issues appeared since triage started
- `cycle_active` — True if frozen_ids exist
- `triage_has_run` — True if triaged_ids recorded

**Triage Stages** (in order):
- `observe` — Analyze issues & spot contradictions
- `reflect` — Form strategy & present to user
- `organize` — Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize
- `enrich` — Make steps executor-ready
- `sense-check` — Verify accuracy, structure & value
- `commit` — Write strategy & confirm

---

### **9. QUEUE RANKING & FINALIZATION** (`ranking.py`, `finalize.py`)

`finalize_queue()` applies in sequence:

1. **`enrich_with_impact()`** — Stamp `estimated_impact` based on dimension headroom
   - Impact = `overall_per_point × (100 - score)`

2. **`_plan_presort()`** — Enrich plan metadata, mark new IDs, stamp sort keys
   - Separates skipped items

3. **`items.sort(key=item_sort_key)`** — Unified ranking:
   - Plan position first (tier 0: planned, tier 1: existing, tier 2: new)
   - Natural ranking: initial-review → triage → workflow → clusters → issues

4. **`_collapse_postflight_subjective_review()`** — Auto-group 2+ postflight subjective items into a cluster

5. **`_plan_postsort()`** — Re-append skipped items, stamp queue positions

6. **`item_explain()`** — Add detailed reasoning for each item (confidence, impact, etc.)

---

### **10. NEXT COMMAND RENDERING** (`render_support.py`, `render_nudges.py`)

`desloppify next` renders the current execution queue:

1. **Queue header** — "Queue: N items"
2. **Single item vs. cluster rendering** — Different UI based on kind
3. **Frozen score display** — If `plan_start_scores` exists and score hasn't changed
4. **North star** — Shows gap to target strict score
5. **Queue block** — Live queue status (objective/subjective breakdown)
6. **Subjective bottleneck warning** — If dims are the main blocker (not shown while objective work exists)
7. **Subjective summary** — Collapsed status (below-target, stale, unassessed, open review count)
8. **Post-render nudges** — Uncommitted reminder, single-item resolution hints

**Frozen Score Logic**:
- If `plan_start_scores.strict` is set and queue is not fully drained, score is frozen
- Users see frozen value + live estimate delta
- Communicate-score workflow item gate prevents mid-cycle score updates

---

### **11. AUTO-CLUSTER SYNC** (`auto_cluster_sync.py`)

`sync_subjective_clusters()` creates auto-clusters for subjective queue members:

1. **Compute subjective state sets**:
   - `unscored_ids` — Placeholder dimensions (not yet reviewed)
   - `stale_ids` — Dimensions that haven't been reviewed since state changed
   - `under_target_ids` — Dimensions below target score

2. **Build cluster specs**:
   - `auto/initial-review` — unscored dimensions (always created if members exist)
   - `auto/subjective-review` — stale/under-target (only if no objective backlog)

3. **Sync into plan.clusters**:
   - Merge with existing clusters by `cluster_key`
   - Update descriptions with counts
   - Set action commands to `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <keys>`

---

### **12. QUEUE ORDER & PLANNED EXECUTION**

`queue_order` is the authoritative source for:
- **Execution sequence** — items are processed in list order
- **Explicit planning** — only items in `queue_order` (not in `skipped`) are eligible
- **Synthetic prefixes** — `workflow::`, `triage::`, `subjective::` items are auto-injected/pruned
- **Workflow prefix** — Workflow items are normalized to the front (ahead of triage)

`executable_objective_ids()` logic:
- If plan has NO queue work yet → all objective IDs are executable
- If plan has queue work → ONLY objectives in `queue_order` are executable (queue-gated)
- Synthetic items never expand the live queue

---

### **13. POSTFLIGHT SEQUENCE (Summary)**

When a cycle completes (queue drains):

```
[Scan → Reconcile] → [Assessment] → [Workflow] → [Triage] → [Execute]
                           ↓              ↓          ↓         ↓
                      subjective     score/plan   stages    drain
                      dimensions     gates        work      queue
```

1. **Assessment Postflight**: Subjective dimension review work
   - Auto-create `auto/initial-review` cluster
   - Suppress if review issues already cover same dimensions

2. **Workflow Postflight** (in order):
   - `workflow::score-checkpoint` — Show current score when reviews complete
   - `workflow::import-scores` — If issues-only import queued
   - `workflow::communicate-score` — Rebaseline frozen score at cycle boundary
   - `workflow::create-plan` — Plan creation (triage entry point)

3. **Triage Postflight**:
   - Inject `triage::observe`, `triage::reflect`, etc. stages
   - Guard against objective backlog (defer with banner if active)
   - Escalate deferral if new review issues keep appearing

4. **Execute**: Drain objective queue, then cycles back to scan

---

### **14. Key Data Structures**

**QueueSnapshot**:
- `phase` — Current execution phase (string)
- Partitioned item collections (8 different slices)
- Count aggregations (planned, execution, backlog)
- Blocker signals (`has_unplanned_objective_blockers`)

**ReconcileResult** (from pipeline):
- `subjective`, `auto_cluster_changes`, `communicate_score`, `create_plan`, `triage` — all `QueueSyncResult` or None
- `lifecycle_phase`, `lifecycle_phase_changed`
- `dirty` property — True if any mutations occurred
- `workflow_injected_ids` — IDs that were added to queue

**TriageSnapshot**:
- `progress` — Stage completion state
- Frozen/live/new/undispositioned issue IDs (sets)
- Organized count, total in scope
- Flags: `has_triage_in_queue`, `is_triage_stale`, `cycle_active`, `triage_has_run`

---

### **15. Important Boundary Checks & Sentinels**

| Check | Location | Purpose |
|-------|----------|---------|
| `live_planned_queue_empty(plan)` | pipeline.py | Gate for boundary reconciliation |
| `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` | refresh_lifecycle.py | Gate for run-scan item injection |
| `_is_fresh_boundary(plan)` | snapshot.py | Distinguish new cycles from mid-cycle |
| `previous_plan_start_scores` | workflow.py | Sentinel to prevent mid-cycle communicate-score re-injection |
| `plan_start_scores` | Multiple | Frozen baseline during objective queue drain |
| `triage_recommended` | triage.py | Banner hint when triage deferred |
| `issue_snapshot_hash` | triage.py | Detect review issue changes |

---

### **Key Architectural Insights**

1. **Phase-driven queue building**: The phase decision is deterministic and sticky (postflight phases persist until next boundary crossing).

2. **Synthetic item management**: All `workflow::*` and `triage::*` items are ephemeral — created/pruned by sync functions, never user-created.

3. **Subjective dimension lifecycle**: Unscored → scored → stale/under-target → resurface for re-review.

4. **Frozen score semantics**: Baseline is frozen only when objective work exists AND communicate-score hasn't run. Score unfreezes once queue drains completely.

5. **Triage gating**: Triage stages only inject when objective backlog is empty OR deferral escalates after repeated failures to drain.

6. **Review import integration**: Review imports trigger boundary reconciliation, ensuring workflow items (score gates, triage stages) surface at the right time.

7. **Plan atomicity**: All mutations are collected and saved in one atomic save, with execution_log entries for audit.

---

1→"""Integration tests for lifecycle transitions through reconcile → work queue.
     2→
     3→Exercises the full lifecycle by walking through each stage:
     4→  scan → initial reviews → objectives → postflight scan → subjective review
     5→  → communicate-score/create-plan → triage
     6→
     7→Between scans, items are completed via ``purge_ids`` (what ``plan resolve``
     8→does) and the queue is re-checked without reconciling.  ``reconcile`` only
     9→runs at scan boundaries — matching the real CLI flow.
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from types import SimpleNamespace
    16→
    17→import desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_reconcile as reconcile_mod
    18→from desloppify.base.subjective_dimensions import DISPLAY_NAMES
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    20→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    21→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    22→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.lifecycle import purge_ids
    25→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    27→    QueueBuildOptions,
    28→    build_work_queue,
    29→)
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Dimensions we use throughout (3 is enough to be readable)
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→DIM_KEYS = ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity", "type_safety")
    35→DIM_DISPLAY = {k: DISPLAY_NAMES[k] for k in DIM_KEYS}
    36→
    37→OBJECTIVE_ISSUES = [
    38→    {"id": "obj-1", "detector": "complexity", "file": "src/app.py", "status": "open",
    39→     "tier": 2, "confidence": "high"},
    40→    {"id": "obj-2", "detector": "naming", "file": "src/util.py", "status": "open",
    41→     "tier": 3, "confidence": "medium"},
    42→]
    43→
    44→
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→# State / plan helpers
    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→
    49→def _placeholder_dim_entries(key: str) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    50→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) for a placeholder."""
    51→    return (
    52→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    53→        {
    54→            "score": 0, "strict": 0, "failing": 0, "checks": 0,
    55→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": True, "dimension_key": key}},
    56→        },
    57→        {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _scored_dim_entries(key: str, score: float) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    62→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) with a real score."""
    63→    return (
    64→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    65→        {
    66→            "score": score, "strict": score, "failing": 0, "checks": 1,
    67→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": False, "dimension_key": key}},
    68→        },
    69→        {"score": score, "placeholder": False},
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _build_state(
    74→    issues: list[dict],
    75→    dims: list[tuple[str, dict, dict]],
    76→    *,
    77→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    78→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    79→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    80→) -> dict:
    81→    work_items = {i["id"]: dict(i) for i in issues}
    82→    state: dict = {
    83→        "work_items": work_items,
    84→        "issues": work_items,
    85→        "scan_count": 1,
    86→        "dimension_scores": {},
    87→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    88→    }
    89→    for display, dim_entry, assessment in dims:
    90→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
    91→        dim_key = dim_entry["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]["dimension_key"]
    92→        state["subjective_assessments"][dim_key] = assessment
    93→    if strict_score is not None:
    94→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    95→    if overall_score is not None:
    96→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    97→    if objective_score is not None:
    98→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    99→    return state
   100→
   101→
   102→def _reconcile(state: dict, plan: dict, monkeypatch) -> dict:
   103→    """Simulate a scan boundary: run reconcile_plan_post_scan with mocked I/O."""
   104→    saved: list[dict] = []
   105→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
   106→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
   107→    reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(SimpleNamespace(
   108→        state=state, state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"), config={},
   109→    ))
   110→    return saved[-1] if saved else plan
   111→
   112→
   113→def _queue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   114→    result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=plan, count=None))
   115→    return [item["id"] for item in result["items"]]
   116→
   117→
   118→def _spoof_reviews_complete(state: dict, *, score: float = 95.0) -> None:
   119→    """Mutate state in place: replace all placeholder dims with scored ones."""
   120→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   121→        display, dim_entry, assessment = _scored_dim_entries(key, score)
   122→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
   123→        state["subjective_assessments"][key] = assessment
   124→    state["strict_score"] = score
   125→    state["overall_score"] = score
   126→    state["objective_score"] = score
   127→
   128→
   129→def _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Mark scored subjective dimensions as fully current/high to clear rerun queue."""
   131→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   132→        assessment = state["subjective_assessments"].setdefault(key, {})
   133→        assessment["placeholder"] = False
   134→        assessment["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   135→        assessment["score"] = 100.0
   136→        dim = state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY[key]]
   137→        dim["score"] = 100.0
   138→        dim["strict"] = 100.0
   139→
   140→
   141→def _add_review_issues(state: dict) -> None:
   142→    """Mutate state in place: add review detector issues that trigger triage."""
   143→    work_items = state.setdefault("work_items", state.get("issues", {}))
   144→    state["issues"] = work_items
   145→    for key in ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity"):
   146→        fid = f"review-{key}"
   147→        work_items[fid] = {
   148→            "id": fid, "detector": "review", "file": "src/app.py",
   149→            "status": "open", "detail": {"dimension": key},
   150→        }
   151→
   152→
   153→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   154→# Test 1: Fresh scan — only initial reviews visible
   155→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   156→
   157→class TestPhase1OnlyInitialReviews:
   158→
   159→    def test_only_initial_reviews_visible(self, monkeypatch):
   160→        """Fresh scan with placeholders: only subjective initial review items appear."""
   161→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   162→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   163→
   164→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   165→
   166→        assert all(fid.startswith("subjective::") for fid in ids)
   167→        assert len(ids) == len(DIM_KEYS)
   168→        assert "obj-1" not in ids
   169→        assert "obj-2" not in ids
   170→
   171→
   172→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   173→# Test 2: Complete reviews (between scans) → objectives unlock immediately
   174→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   175→
   176→class TestCompleteReviewsUnlocksObjectives:
   177→
   178→    def test_objectives_visible_after_completing_reviews(self, monkeypatch):
   179→        """After completing reviews (no reconcile), lifecycle filter lets objectives through."""
   180→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   181→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   182→
   183→        # Complete reviews: update state + purge subjective IDs
   184→        subj_ids = [fid for fid in _queue_ids(state, plan) if fid.startswith("subjective::")]
   185→        _spoof_reviews_complete(state)
   186→        purge_ids(plan, subj_ids)
   187→
   188→        # No reconcile — just check the queue
   189→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   190→        assert "obj-1" in ids
   191→        assert "obj-2" in ids
   192→        # communicate-score hasn't been injected yet (no scan since reviews)
   193→        assert WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in ids
   194→
   195→
   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→# Test 3: Next scan after reviews → communicate-score + create-plan appear
   198→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   199→
   200→class TestScanAfterReviewsInjectsWorkflow:
   201→
   202→    def test_workflow_items_injected_on_next_scan(self, monkeypatch):
   203→        """Scan after completing reviews injects communicate-score and create-plan.
   204→
   205→        Postflight is exclusive once the scan boundary is crossed:
   206→        subjective reruns surface first, then score/workflow, and only then
   207→        does execute backlog reappear.
   208→        """
   209→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   210→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   211→
   212→        # Complete reviews between scans
   213→        subj_ids = [fid for fid in _queue_ids(state, plan) if fid.startswith("subjective::")]
   214→        _spoof_reviews_complete(state)
   215→        purge_ids(plan, subj_ids)
   216→
   217→        # --- Next scan (reconcile) ---
   218→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   219→
   220→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   221→        assert all(fid.startswith("subjective::") for fid in ids)
   222→
   223→        _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state)
   224→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   225→        assert WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in ids
   226→        assert WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in ids
   227→        assert ids.index(WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID) < ids.index(WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   228→
   229→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
   230→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   231→        assert "obj-1" in ids and "obj-2" in ids
   232→
   233→
   234→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   235→# Phase-order contract: assessment -> score -> triage -> review (after objective drains)
   236→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   237→
   238→class TestPhaseOrderInvariant:
   239→
   240→    def test_assessment_then_score_when_no_review_followup(self):
   241→        """Endgame queue order is fixed once objective backlog is drained."""
   242→        state = _build_state(
   243→            [],
   244→            [_scored_dim_entries("naming_quality", 80.0)],
   245→            strict_score=76.2,
   246→            overall_score=80.1,
   247→            objective_score=99.9,
   248→        )
   249→        state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"]["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   250→        plan = empty_plan()
   251→        plan["queue_order"] = [
   252→            WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   253→            "triage::observe",
   254→            "subjective::naming_quality",
   255→        ]
   256→        # Mark postflight scan as done so it doesn't block
   257→        plan["refresh_state"] = {"postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count": 1}
   258→
   259→        # Subjective follow-up surfaces before workflow items.
   260→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   261→        assert ids == ["subjective::naming_quality"]
   262→
   263→        # After subjective follow-up completion, workflow appears.
   264→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   265→        state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   266→        state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"]["score"] = 100.0
   267→        state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY["naming_quality"]]["score"] = 100.0
   268→        state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY["naming_quality"]]["strict"] = 100.0
   269→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   270→        assert ids == [WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID]
   271→
   272→        # After workflow completion, triage becomes visible.
   273→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID])
   274→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   275→        assert ids == ["triage::observe"]

   278→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   279→# Test 4: Triage injected when review issues appear
   280→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   281→
   282→class TestTriageInjectedOnScan:
   283→
   284→    def test_triage_after_review_issues_on_scan(self, monkeypatch):
   285→        """Review-driven triage waits behind postflight review and score steps."""
   286→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   287→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   288→
   289→        # Complete reviews between scans
   290→        subj_ids = [fid for fid in _queue_ids(state, plan) if fid.startswith("subjective::")]
   291→        _spoof_reviews_complete(state)
   292→        purge_ids(plan, subj_ids)
   293→
   294→        # Add review issues + scan
   295→        _add_review_issues(state)
   296→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   297→
   298→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   299→        assert not any(fid.startswith("triage::") for fid in ids), ids
   300→        assert ids == ["subjective::type_safety"]
   301→
   302→        # Triage stages are still injected in plan order.
   303→        assert all(sid in plan["queue_order"] for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   304→
   305→        _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state)
   306→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   307→        assert WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in ids, ids
   308→        assert WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in ids, ids
   309→
   310→        # After completing workflow items, triage becomes visible before execute resumes.
   311→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
   312→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   313→        triage_ids = [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("triage::")]
   314→        assert len(triage_ids) == len(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS), ids
   315→
   316→        # After triage completes for the live review issue set, the findings surface.
   317→        plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})["triaged_ids"] = sorted(
   318→            fid for fid in state["work_items"] if state["work_items"][fid].get("detector") == "review"
   319→        )
   320→        purge_ids(plan, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   321→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   322→        review_ids = [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("review")]
   323→        assert len(review_ids) > 0, f"Expected review issues after triage: {ids}"
   324→
   325→
   326→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   327→# Test 5: Full lifecycle golden path
   328→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   329→
   330→class TestFullLifecycleGoldenPath:
   331→
   332→    def test_golden_path(self, monkeypatch):
   333→        """Walk through every lifecycle stage, completing items between scans."""
   334→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   335→        plan = empty_plan()
   336→
   337→        # ── Scan 1: initial reviews ──
   338→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   339→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   340→        assert all(fid.startswith("subjective::") for fid in ids), f"Scan 1: {ids}"
   341→        assert len(ids) == len(DIM_KEYS)
   342→
   343→        # ── Between scans: complete reviews ──
   344→        _spoof_reviews_complete(state)
   345→        purge_ids(plan, [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("subjective::")])
   346→
   347→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   348→        # Objectives unlocked immediately, no workflow items yet
   349→        assert "obj-1" in ids, f"Post-reviews (no scan): {ids}"
   350→        assert WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in ids, f"Post-reviews (no scan): {ids}"
   351→
   352→        # ── Scan 2: postflight review work surfaces before execute resumes ──
   353→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   354→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   355→        assert all(fid.startswith("subjective::") for fid in ids), f"Scan 2: {ids}"
   356→
   357→        _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state)
   358→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   359→        assert WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in ids, f"Post-rerun-workflow: {ids}"
   360→        assert WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in ids, f"Post-rerun-workflow: {ids}"
   361→
   362→        # ── Complete workflow items; clear cycle baseline so execute resumes ──
   363→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
   364→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   365→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   366→        assert "obj-1" in ids and "obj-2" in ids, f"Post-workflow: {ids}"
   367→
   368→        # ── Scan 3: add review issues + reopen objectives for mid-cycle test ──
   369→        state["work_items"]["obj-1"]["status"] = "open"
   370→        state["work_items"]["obj-2"]["status"] = "open"
   371→        # Place objectives in queue_order so the plan is mid-cycle (non-empty).
   372→        plan["queue_order"] = ["obj-1", "obj-2"]
   373→        _add_review_issues(state)
   374→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   375→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   376→        # Mid-cycle guard: triage NOT injected while objective work remains
   377→        assert not any(fid.startswith("triage::") for fid in ids), f"Scan 3: {ids}"
   378→        assert not any(sid in plan["queue_order"] for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS), (
   379→            f"Triage should be deferred mid-cycle: {plan['queue_order']}"
   380→        )
   381→        assert "obj-1" in ids and "obj-2" in ids, f"Scan 3: {ids}"
   382→
   383→        # ── Complete objective queue → rescan injects triage in plan, but
   384→        #    postflight still starts with workflow items ──
   385→        state["work_items"]["obj-1"]["status"] = "fixed"
   386→        state["work_items"]["obj-2"]["status"] = "fixed"
   387→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   388→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   389→        workflow_ids = [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("workflow::")]
   390→        assert len(workflow_ids) > 0, f"Expected workflow phase: {ids}"
   391→
   392→        # ── Complete workflow items → triage unlocks ──
   393→        purge_ids(plan, workflow_ids)
   394→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   395→        triage_ids = [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("triage::")]
   396→        assert len(triage_ids) == len(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS), f"Triage unlock: {ids}"
   397→        assert not plan["epic_triage_meta"].get("triage_recommended"), (
   398→            "triage_recommended should be cleared after injection"
   399→        )
   400→
   401→        # ── Complete triage → review findings finally become executable ──
   402→        plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})["triaged_ids"] = sorted(
   403→            fid for fid in state["work_items"] if state["work_items"][fid].get("detector") == "review"
   404→        )
   405→        purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS))
   406→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   407→        assert not any(fid.startswith("triage::") for fid in ids), f"Post-triage: {ids}"
   408→        review_ids = [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("review")]
   409→        assert len(review_ids) > 0, f"Expected review execution after triage: {ids}"
   410→

     2→

    16→
    17→def _issue(
    18→    fid: str,
    19→    *,

    47→

    49→def test_collapse_clusters_preserves_order():
    50→    """Cluster meta-item appears at position of first member, not re-sorted."""
    51→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    52→
    53→    plan: dict = {
    54→        "queue_order": [],
    55→        "skipped": {},
    56→        "overrides": {},
    57→        "clusters": {},
    58→        "active_cluster": None,
    59→    }
    60→    plan["clusters"]["auto/unused"] = {
    61→        "name": "auto/unused",
    62→        "auto": True,
    63→        "cluster_key": "auto::unused",
    64→        "issue_ids": ["u1", "u2"],
    65→        "description": "Remove 2 unused issues",
    66→        "action": "desloppify autofix unused-imports --dry-run",
    67→        "user_modified": False,
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    # Place a non-cluster item first, then the two cluster members
    71→    items = [
    72→        {"id": "other", "kind": "issue", "detector": "structural",
    73→         "confidence": "medium", "detail": {}},
    74→        {"id": "u1", "kind": "issue", "detector": "unused",
    75→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
    76→        {"id": "u2", "kind": "issue", "detector": "unused",
    77→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
    78→    ]
    79→
    80→    result = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
    81→    # Non-cluster item stays at position 0
    82→    assert result[0]["id"] == "other"
    83→    # Cluster meta-item appears at position 1 (where first member was)
    84→    assert result[1]["kind"] == "cluster"
    85→    assert result[1]["id"] == "auto/unused"
    86→    assert len(result) == 2  # other + cluster
    87→
    88→
    89→# -- Plan-ordered subjective items surface despite objective backlog --------
    90→
    91→
    92→def test_plan_ordered_stale_subjective_gated_with_objective_backlog():
    93→    """Stale subjective items are gated by lifecycle filter while objective
    94→    issues exist, even when the plan includes them in queue_order.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
    97→
    98→    objective_issues = [
    99→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   100→        for c in "abcd"
   101→    ]
   102→    state = _state(
   103→        objective_issues,
   104→        dimension_scores={
   105→            "Naming quality": {
   106→                "score": 70.0,
   107→                "strict": 70.0,
   108→                "failing": 1,
   109→                "detectors": {
   110→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   111→                },
   112→            },
   113→        },
   114→    )
   115→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   116→        "naming_quality": {
   117→            "score": 70.0,
   118→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   119→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   120→        }
   121→    }
   122→
   123→    # Without an explicit queue, pre-triage objective work still blocks stale
   124→    # subjective review items from surfacing.
   125→    queue_no_plan = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   126→    subj_no_plan = [
   127→        i["id"] for i in queue_no_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   128→    ]
   129→    assert subj_no_plan == []
   130→
   131→    # With plan that includes the stale dim in queue_order: still gated
   132→    plan = empty_plan()
   133→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   134→        "subjective::naming_quality",
   135→        "smells::src/a.py::x",
   136→        "smells::src/b.py::x",
   137→        "smells::src/c.py::x",
   138→        "smells::src/d.py::x",
   139→    ]
   140→    queue_with_plan = build_work_queue(
   141→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan,
   142→    )
   143→    subj_with_plan = [
   144→        i["id"] for i in queue_with_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   145→    ]
   146→    # Stale subjective items gated by lifecycle filter even with plan ordering
   147→    assert len(subj_with_plan) == 0
   148→

   383→                confidence="high",
   384→                detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
   385→            ),
   386→        ],
   387→        dimension_scores={
   388→            "Naming quality": {
   389→                "score": 70.0,
   390→                "strict": 70.0,
   391→                "failing": 1,
   392→                "detectors": {
   393→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},

   396→        },

   398→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   399→        "naming_quality": {
   400→            "score": 70.0,
   401→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   402→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   403→        }

   407→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {

   409→        "last_completed_at": "2026-03-13T00:00:00+00:00",
   410→    }

   412→
   413→    queue = build_execution_queue(

   417→            include_subjective=True,
   418→            plan=plan,
   419→        ),
   420→    )
   421→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   422→    # Subjective dimension item is suppressed when review issues cover the
   423→    # same dimension — the assessment request alone surfaces.
   424→    assert ids == ["subjective_review::naming_quality"]

   479→def test_unplanned_objective_items_dont_block_postflight():
   480→    """Unplanned objective items don't keep the system in EXECUTE phase.
   481→
   482→    When all planned work drains, unplanned objective items should not block
   483→    phase transitions to postflight work. The has_unplanned_objective_blockers
   484→    flag is still set for informational purposes.
   485→
   486→    The split only applies when the plan has at least one tracked objective
   487→    (i.e. post-triage). Pre-triage plans treat all objectives as planned.
   488→    """
   489→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   490→
   491→    state = _state(
   492→        [
   493→            _issue("smells::src/a.py::planned", detector="smells"),
   494→            _issue("smells::src/b.py::unplanned", detector="smells"),
   495→        ],
   496→        dimension_scores={
   497→            "Naming quality": {
   498→                "score": 70.0,
   499→                "strict": 70.0,
   500→                "failing": 1,
   501→                "detectors": {
   502→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   503→                },
   504→            },
   505→        },
   506→    )
   507→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   508→        "naming_quality": {
   509→            "score": 70.0,
   510→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   511→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   512→        }
   513→    }
   514→
   515→    # Plan has one tracked objective — simulates post-triage state
   516→    plan = empty_plan()
   517→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"strict": 75.0}
   518→    plan["queue_order"] = ["smells::src/a.py::planned"]
   519→
   520→    queue = build_execution_queue(
   521→        state,
   522→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   523→            count=None,
   524→            include_subjective=True,
   525→            plan=plan,
   526→        ),
   527→    )
   528→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   529→    # Only planned item appears — unplanned item does NOT block
   530→    assert ids == ["smells::src/a.py::planned"]
   531→    assert "smells::src/b.py::unplanned" not in ids
   532→
   533→
   534→# ── Lifecycle filter runs after plan_presort ───────────
   535→
   536→
   537→def test_skipped_objective_items_dont_block_subjective():
   538→    """Plan-skipped objective items are removed before lifecycle filter,
   539→    so they don't block subjective reassessment items.
   540→    """
   541→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   542→
   543→    objective_issues = [
   544→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   545→        for c in "abcd"
   546→    ]
   547→    state = _state(
   548→        objective_issues,
   549→        dimension_scores={
   550→            "Naming quality": {
   551→                "score": 70.0,
   552→                "strict": 70.0,
   553→                "failing": 1,
   554→                "detectors": {
   555→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   556→                },
   557→            },
   558→        },
   559→    )
   560→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   561→        "naming_quality": {
   562→            "score": 70.0,
   563→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   564→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   565→        }
   566→    }
   567→
   568→    # Skip ALL objective issues in the plan
   569→    plan = empty_plan()
   570→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   571→        "subjective::naming_quality",
   572→        "smells::src/a.py::x",
   573→        "smells::src/b.py::x",
   574→        "smells::src/c.py::x",
   575→        "smells::src/d.py::x",
   576→    ]
   577→    plan["skipped"] = {
   578→        "smells::src/a.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   579→        "smells::src/b.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   580→        "smells::src/c.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   581→        "smells::src/d.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   582→    }
   583→
   584→    queue = build_work_queue(
   585→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan,
   586→    )
   587→    ids = [i["id"] for i in queue["items"]]
   588→    # All objective items skipped → lifecycle filter sees no objective work
   589→    # → but deferred disposition precedes subjective in lifecycle
   590→    assert "workflow::deferred-disposition" in ids
   591→    # Subjective items are gated behind deferred disposition
   592→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   593→    assert len(subj_ids) == 0
   594→

   596→# ── Wontfix / resolved issues excluded (#193) ──────────

   598→
   599→def test_wontfixed_issues_excluded_from_queue():
   600→    """Issues with status 'wontfix' never appear in the default queue.
   601→
   602→    Regression test for #193: wontfixed issues were leaking into the
   603→    auto-generated queue because filtering was spread across multiple
   604→    modules and easy to miss.
   605→    """
   606→    state = _state(
   607→        [
   608→            _issue("a", status="open"),
   609→            _issue("b", status="wontfix"),
   610→            _issue("c", status="fixed"),
   611→            _issue("d", status="open"),
   612→        ]
   613→    )
   614→
   615→    queue = build_backlog_queue(
   616→        state,
   617→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   618→            count=None,
   619→            include_subjective=False,
   620→        ),
   621→    )
   622→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
   623→    assert "a" in ids
   624→    assert "d" in ids
   625→    assert "b" not in ids  # wontfix excluded
   626→    assert "c" not in ids  # fixed excluded
   627→

   636→    state = _state(

   641→            _issue("d", status="open"),

   645→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False, plan=plan)
   646→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
   647→    assert "a" in ids

   656→def test_triage_stages_hidden_during_initial_reviews():
   657→    """Phase 1 hides triage stages and workflow actions — only initial reviews visible."""
   658→    objective_issues = [
   659→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   660→        for c in "ab"
   661→    ]
   662→    state = _state(
   663→        objective_issues,
   664→        dimension_scores={
   665→            "Naming quality": {
   666→                "score": 0.0,
   667→                "strict": 0.0,
   668→                "failing": 0,
   669→                "detectors": {

   673→                    },
   674→                },

   678→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {

   681→
   682→    # Inject a triage stage and a workflow action into queue_order so they
   683→    # would appear if the lifecycle filter didn't hide them.
   684→    plan = {

   687→            "workflow::communicate-score",
   688→            "subjective::naming_quality",
   689→        ],
   690→        "queue_skipped": {},
   691→    }
   692→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan)
   693→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   694→
   695→    # Only initial review visible — triage and workflow hidden
   696→    assert ids == ["subjective::naming_quality"]

   699→def test_subjective_phase_precedes_score_and_triage_when_objective_drained():
   700→    """Subjective reruns stay ahead of workflow and triage once postflight begins."""
   701→    state = _state(

   711→                        "placeholder": False,
   712→                    },
   713→                },
   714→            },
   715→        },
   716→    )
   717→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {

   719→            "score": 80.0,
   720→            "placeholder": False,
   721→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   722→        }
   723→    }
   724→
   725→    plan = {

   727→            "workflow::communicate-score",
   728→            "triage::observe",
   729→            "subjective::naming_quality",
   730→        ],
   731→        "queue_skipped": {},
   732→        "refresh_state": {"postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count": 1},
   733→    }
   734→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan)
   735→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   736→    assert ids == ["subjective::naming_quality"]

   738→
   739→def test_triage_stages_sort_after_workflow_in_natural_ranking():
   740→    """In natural ranking (no plan), workflow actions sort before triage stages."""
   741→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
   742→
   743→    workflow_item = {
   744→        "id": "workflow::communicate-score",
   745→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   746→        "tier": 1,
   747→        "confidence": "high",
   748→        "detector": "workflow",
   749→        "file": ".",
   750→    }
   751→    triage_item = {
   752→        "id": "triage::observe",
   753→        "kind": "workflow_stage",
   754→        "tier": 1,
   755→        "confidence": "high",
   756→        "detector": "triage",
   757→        "file": ".",
   758→        "detail": {"stage": "observe"},
   759→        "is_blocked": False,
   760→    }
   761→
   762→    wf_key = item_sort_key(workflow_item)
   763→    tr_key = item_sort_key(triage_item)
   764→    assert wf_key < tr_key, "workflow actions should sort before triage stages"
   765→

     2→
     3→Tests exercise the real reconciliation logic with realistic plan and state
     4→data structures, mocking only at I/O boundaries (load_plan, save_plan).
     5→"""

    12→
    13→import desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_reconcile as reconcile_mod

    22→def _runtime(*, state=None, config=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    23→    return SimpleNamespace(
    24→        state=state or {},
    25→        state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"),
    26→        config=config or {},
    27→    )
    28→
    29→
    30→def _make_state(
    31→    *,
    32→    issues: dict | None = None,
    33→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    34→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    35→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    36→    verified_strict_score: float | None = None,
    37→    scan_count: int = 1,
    38→) -> dict:
    39→    """Build a minimal but realistic state dict."""
    40→    state: dict = {
    41→        "issues": issues or {},
    42→        "scan_count": scan_count,
    43→        "dimension_scores": {},
    44→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    45→    }
    46→    if overall_score is not None:
    47→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    48→    if objective_score is not None:
    49→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    50→    if strict_score is not None:
    51→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    52→    if verified_strict_score is not None:
    53→        state["verified_strict_score"] = verified_strict_score
    54→    return state
    55→
    56→
    57→def _make_issue(
    58→    detector: str = "complexity",
    59→    file: str = "src/app.py",
    60→    status: str = "open",
    61→    **extra,
    62→) -> dict:
    63→    return {"detector": detector, "file": file, "status": status, **extra}
