Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive view of the existing test patterns. Let me create a detailed summary document.

## Key Test Patterns Summary: Postflight Queue Lifecycle

Based on reading the three test files and related fixtures, here are the established patterns and helper functions for testing postflight queue lifecycle:

---

### 1. **Plan/State Construction Patterns**

#### **Helper: `_build_state()` (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py`)**
```python
def _build_state(
    issues: list[dict],
    dims: list[tuple[str, dict, dict]],
    *,
    strict_score: float | None = None,
    overall_score: float | None = None,
    objective_score: float | None = None,
) -> dict
```
- **Purpose**: Builds a minimal state with issues and dimension scores
- **Returns**: Dict with keys: `work_items`, `issues`, `scan_count`, `dimension_scores`, `subjective_assessments`
- **Pattern**: Issues are both in `work_items` and `issues` (duplicated key)
- **Reuse this**: Yes, it's designed for integration testing

#### **Helper: `_placeholder_dim_entries()` and `_scored_dim_entries()` (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py`)**
```python
def _placeholder_dim_entries(key: str) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]
def _scored_dim_entries(key: str, score: float) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]
```
- **Purpose**: Creates `(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment)` tuples
- **Key structure**: 
  - `dim_scores_entry` has: `score`, `strict`, `failing`, `checks`, `detectors.subjective_assessment`
  - `subjective_assessment` has: `score`, `placeholder` (bool)
- **Pattern**: Paired construction for state building

#### **Helper: `_make_state()` (from `test_plan_reconcile.py`)**
```python
def _make_state(
    *,
    issues: dict | None = None,
    overall_score: float | None = None,
    objective_score: float | None = None,
    strict_score: float | None = None,
    verified_strict_score: float | None = None,
    scan_count: int = 1,
) -> dict
```
- **Purpose**: Simpler state builder for reconcile tests
- **Difference from `_build_state()`**: No dimension scores, focused on score fields
- **Reuse this**: For plan reconciliation tests

#### **Helper: `_make_issue()` (from `test_plan_reconcile.py`)**
```python
def _make_issue(
    detector: str = "complexity",
    file: str = "src/app.py",
    status: str = "open",
    **extra,
) -> dict
```
- **Purpose**: Lightweight issue builder with defaults
- **Pattern**: Minimal required fields, extensible via `**extra`

---

### 2. **Plan Construction & Modification**

#### **Basic plan structure (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py` and `test_work_queue_plan_order_and_triage.py`)**
```python
plan = empty_plan()  # from desloppify.engine._plan.schema

# Key fields to manipulate:
plan["queue_order"] = [...]           # Main execution order
plan["skipped"] = {...}               # Deferred/skipped items with metadata
plan["refresh_state"] = {...}         # Postflight tracking
plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {...}      # Triage completion markers
plan["plan_start_scores"] = {...}     # Score baseline for cycle
plan["clusters"] = {...}              # Grouped issues
plan["overrides"] = {...}             # User customizations
plan["subjective_defer_meta"] = {...} # Legacy deferral (ignored in execute)
```

#### **Key `refresh_state` fields**
```python
plan["refresh_state"] = {
    "postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count": 1,  # Marks postflight done
    "lifecycle_phase": "scan",                       # Current phase
}
```

#### **Triage completion markers**
```python
plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {
    "triaged_ids": [...],              # IDs processed by triage
    "last_completed_at": "...",        # ISO timestamp
    "triage_recommended": False,       # If true, triage queued
}
```

---

### 3. **Queue Building & Assertion Patterns**

#### **Helper: `_queue_ids()` (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py`)**
```python
def _queue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=plan, count=None))
    return [item["id"] for item in result["items"]]
```
- **Purpose**: Extract just the IDs from a built queue for simple assertions
- **Pattern**: Reusable test helper, common assertion pattern
- **Reuse this**: Yes, this is the standard pattern

#### **Wrapper: `build_work_queue()` (from `test_work_queue_plan_order_and_triage.py`)**
```python
def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))
```
- **Purpose**: Convenience wrapper to avoid verbose QueueBuildOptions syntax
- **Pattern**: Tests use this for cleaner code

### 4. **Scan Boundary & Reconciliation**

#### **Helper: `_reconcile()` (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py`)**
```python
def _reconcile(state: dict, plan: dict, monkeypatch) -> dict:
    """Simulate a scan boundary: run reconcile_plan_post_scan with mocked I/O."""
    saved: list[dict] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
    reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(SimpleNamespace(
        state=state, state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"), config={},
    ))
    return saved[-1] if saved else plan
```
- **Purpose**: Simulates a full `scan` command's reconciliation phase
- **Pattern**: Mock load/save at I/O boundaries; return saved plan
- **Reuse this**: Yes, this is the standard pattern for reconciliation tests

#### **Helper: `_runtime()` (from `test_plan_reconcile.py`)**
```python
def _runtime(*, state=None, config=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    return SimpleNamespace(
        state=state or {},
        state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"),
        config=config or {},
    )
```
- **Purpose**: Build a simulated CLI runtime object
- **Pattern**: Used when directly calling reconciliation functions
- **Reuse this**: For lower-level reconciliation tests

### 5. **Subjective Dimension State Mutations**

#### **Helper: `_spoof_reviews_complete()` (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py`)**
```python
def _spoof_reviews_complete(state: dict, *, score: float = 95.0) -> None:
    """Mutate state in place: replace all placeholder dims with scored ones."""
    for key in DIM_KEYS:
        display, dim_entry, assessment = _scored_dim_entries(key, score)
        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
        state["subjective_assessments"][key] = assessment
    state["strict_score"] = score
    state["overall_score"] = score
    state["objective_score"] = score
```
- **Purpose**: Fast-track from initial placeholder reviews to completed subjective assessment
- **Pattern**: Mutates state in place; updates both `dimension_scores` and `subjective_assessments`
- **Reuse this**: Yes, for accelerating test progression through phases

#### **Helper: `_complete_endgame_subjective_reruns()` (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py`)**
```python
def _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state: dict) -> None:
    """Mark scored subjective dimensions as fully current/high to clear rerun queue."""
    for key in DIM_KEYS:
        assessment = state["subjective_assessments"].setdefault(key, {})
        assessment["placeholder"] = False
        assessment["needs_review_refresh"] = False
        assessment["score"] = 100.0
        dim = state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY[key]]
        dim["score"] = 100.0
        dim["strict"] = 100.0
```
- **Purpose**: Clear postflight rerun queue by marking subjective items as non-stale and 100%
- **Pattern**: Sets `needs_review_refresh=False` and high scores
- **Reuse this**: Yes, for clearing postflight blocks

#### **Helper: `_add_review_issues()` (from `test_lifecycle_transitions.py`)**
```python
def _add_review_issues(state: dict) -> None:
    """Mutate state in place: add review detector issues that trigger triage."""
    work_items = state.setdefault("work_items", state.get("issues", {}))
    state["issues"] = work_items
    for key in ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity"):
        fid = f"review-{key}"
        work_items[fid] = {
            "id": fid, "detector": "review", "file": "src/app.py",
            "status": "open", "detail": {"dimension": key},
        }
```
- **Purpose**: Inject review issues mid-cycle to trigger triage injection
- **Pattern**: Updates both `work_items` and `issues`
- **Reuse this**: For testing triage injection flows

### 10. **Test File Organization**

**Lifecycle transitions tests** (`test_lifecycle_transitions.py`):
- **Setup**: Per-test `_build_state()` + `_reconcile()`
- **Flow**: Phase → complete → query queue → assert
- **Monkeypatch scope**: Reconciliation I/O only

**Work queue ordering tests** (`test_work_queue_plan_order_and_triage.py`):
- **Setup**: `_state()` + `_issue()` helper builders
- **Flow**: Build queue → extract IDs → assert order/presence
- **Monkeypatch scope**: None (pure logic, no I/O)

**Review import tests** (`test_review_importing_support_direct.py`):
- **Setup**: `_patch_basic_plan_sync_runtime()` + `_sync_request()`
- **Flow**: Import → sync → verify plan mutations
- **Monkeypatch scope**: Entire plan sync layer

### Summary: Recommended Patterns for Your Postflight Queue Lifecycle Tests

1. **State fixtures**: Use `_build_state()` for full lifecycle; `_make_state()` for reconciliation only
2. **Queue queries**: Use `_queue_ids()` pattern; always extract IDs for assertions
3. **Plan mutations**: Use `purge_ids()` for completions; directly mutate dict for other ops
4. **Reconciliation**: Use `_reconcile()` with monkeypatch for scan boundaries
5. **Subjective progression**: Use `_spoof_reviews_complete()` + `_complete_endgame_subjective_reruns()` helpers
6. **Assertions**: Check queue presence/order, plan markers (triaged_ids, triage_recommended), and lifecycle phase fields

> DEVELOPER

Design the implementation for 5 targeted postflight queue bug fixes in the desloppify project. This is a research-only task — do NOT write any code.

## Background

The postflight lifecycle is: scan → subjective review → score/workflow → triage → execute

There are 4 bugs + 1 cleanup improvement to implement:

### Bug 1: Review-import boundary check happens too late
**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`

In `sync_plan_after_import()` (line 233), the flow is:
1. `_sync_review_delta()` — appends `review::xxx` IDs to `plan["queue_order"]`
2. `sync_import_scores_needed()` — may inject workflow items
3. `live_planned_queue_empty(plan)` check — **BUG**: now returns False because review IDs were appended
4. `reconcile_plan()` is skipped

Fix: Capture `was_boundary_ready = live_planned_queue_empty(plan)` BEFORE step 1. Use `was_boundary_ready and trusted` as the gate for reconcile instead of post-mutation check.

### Bug 2: Covered subjective placeholders not pruned after import
**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`

When a trusted review import covers dimensions (e.g., naming_quality), the matching `subjective::naming-quality` IDs should be removed from `plan["queue_order"]`. Currently they're not.

The `_build_import_sync_inputs()` function (line 154) already computes `covered_ids` as `subjective::<dim_key>` for each assessment key. We just need to actually remove them from queue_order and auto-cluster membership.

Fix: Add a pruning step that removes `sync_inputs.covered_ids` from `queue_order` and auto-cluster `issue_ids`. Create a shared helper for this.

### Bug 3: Freshly-reviewed dimensions immediately re-requested
**Root cause chain**:
1. Trusted import updates state, marking some dims with `needs_review_refresh=True` (because open review findings exist for that dimension)
2. `all_subjective_entries()` in `scorecard_projection.py` (line 116-120) computes `stale=True` when `assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")` is True
3. `current_stale_ids()` includes these dims
4. `sync_subjective_dimensions()` injects `subjective::xxx` for these stale dims during reconcile
5. `_phase_for_snapshot()` sees postflight_assessment_items and picks PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT over PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT

This is a layered bug fixed by a combination of:
- Fix 4 (persisted phase wins) prevents the rendering layer from showing assessment items during workflow
- Fix 5 (phase transition cleanup) prevents stale subjective items from remaining in queue_order after workflow items are injected

But we also need a guard in `build_subjective_items()` or `_subjective_partitions()`: when lifecycle phase is "workflow" or "triage", suppress postflight assessment items from the execution view. The data stays available in backlog.

The simplest approach: in `_subjective_partitions()` in `snapshot.py`, check the persisted lifecycle phase. If it's "workflow" or "triage", return empty postflight list (initial review items pass through since they override everything).

### Bug 4: Phase selection overrides persisted lifecycle too aggressively
**File**: `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`

In `_phase_for_snapshot()` (line 258), the `postflight_sticky` block checks items in this order:
```python
if postflight_assessment_items:  return PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
if postflight_workflow_items:    return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT  
if triage_items:                 return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
```

Assessment ALWAYS wins over workflow, even when the persisted phase says "workflow". The persisted phase (from `current_lifecycle_phase(plan)`) is computed but only used to gate `postflight_sticky`, not to prioritize phases.

Fix: When `postflight_sticky` is True and `persisted_phase` is valid:
- If persisted_phase is "workflow" and postflight_workflow_items exist → PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
- If persisted_phase is "triage" and triage_items exist → PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
- Fall through to partition-based inference only when persisted phase doesn't match available items

The persisted phase values are coarse: "review", "workflow", "triage", "execute", "scan"
The snapshot phases are fine-grained: PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL, PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT, etc.

For "review" persisted phase, keep existing partition-based inference (initial vs assessment vs review-postflight).

### Fix 5: Normalize synthetic cleanup at phase transitions
**File**: `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`

After `_persist_lifecycle()` runs in `reconcile_plan()`, add a cleanup step:
- If phase is "workflow": remove `subjective::...` IDs from queue_order (they were assessment work, now superseded)
- If phase is "triage": remove remaining `subjective::...` IDs + completed workflow IDs
- If phase is "execute": remove `triage::...` IDs + `workflow::...` IDs

This uses the same pruning logic as fix #2 but triggered by phase transition instead of import.

Create a shared function like `prune_synthetic_for_phase(plan, phase)` that can be called from both the reconcile pipeline and import sync.

## Key code structures

**Constants** (`desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py`):
- `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"`
- `WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"`
- `TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"`
- `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")`

**Lifecycle phases** (`desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`):
- `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"`
- `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"`
- `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"`

**Queue snapshot phases** (`desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`):
- `PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT`, `PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT`, `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`, etc.

**Plan model**: dict with keys `queue_order` (list[str]), `skipped` (dict), `clusters` (dict), `refresh_state` (dict), etc.

## Design questions

1. Where should `prune_synthetic_for_phase()` live? Options: `constants.py` (already has synthetic helpers), `pipeline.py` (where it's primarily used), or a new `cleanup.py` in the sync package.

2. For fix 3's rendering guard: should it be in `_subjective_partitions()` (filters items before phase selection), `build_queue_snapshot()` (has plan access), or `_phase_for_snapshot()` (already makes phase decisions)?

3. Should fix 5's cleanup prune ALL subjective IDs or only the ones that correspond to "covered" dimensions? The plan says "entering workflow prunes covered subjective IDs" but the reconcile pipeline doesn't have the import's covered-dimension info. It would need to derive it (e.g., prune all non-unscored subjective IDs).

Please design a concrete implementation plan with specific code changes for each file, considering these design questions.

```
   200→def _prune_covered_subjective_ids_from_plan(
   201→    plan: dict,
   202→    *,
   203→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   204→) -> list[str]:
   205→    """Prune subjective queue placeholders that were just covered by review import."""
   206→    covered = {
   207→        issue_id
   208→        for issue_id in covered_ids
   209→        if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id.startswith("subjective::")
   210→    }
   211→    if not covered:
   212→        return []
   213→
   214→    order = plan.get("queue_order")
   215→    if not isinstance(order, list):
   216→        return []
   217→
   218→    pruned = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id in covered]
   219→    if not pruned:
   220→        return []
   221→
   222→    pruned_set = set(pruned)
   223→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in pruned_set]
   224→
   225→    overrides = plan.get("overrides")
   226→    if isinstance(overrides, dict):
   227→        for issue_id in pruned_set:
   228→            overrides.pop(issue_id, None)
   229→
   230→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   231→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   232→            continue
   233→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
   234→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   235→            continue
   236→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
   237→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in pruned_set
   238→        ]
   239→
   240→    return pruned
   241→
   242→
   243→def _append_review_import_sync_log(
   244→    plan: dict,
   245→    diff: dict,
   246→    import_result: ReviewImportSyncResult | None,
   247→    import_scores_result,
   248→    pipeline_result: ReconcileResult,
   249→    *,
   250→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   251→) -> None:
   252→    if not (
   253→        import_result is not None
   254→        or import_scores_result.changes
   255→        or pipeline_result.dirty
   256→        or covered_ids
   257→    ):
   258→        return
   259→    subjective = pipeline_result.subjective
   260→    triage = pipeline_result.triage
   261→    append_log_entry(
   262→        plan,
   263→        "review_import_sync",
   264→        actor="system",
   265→        detail={
   266→            "trigger": "review_import",
   267→            "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   268→            "added_to_queue": import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else [],
   269→            "workflow_injected_ids": pipeline_result.workflow_injected_ids,
   270→            "triage_injected": bool(triage and triage.injected),
   271→            "triage_injected_ids": list(triage.injected) if triage is not None else [],
   272→            "triage_deferred": bool(triage and triage.deferred),
   273→            "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   274→            "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   275→            "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   276→            "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   277→                import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   278→            ),
   279→            "covered_subjective_pruned_from_queue": (
   280→                import_result.covered_subjective_pruned_from_queue
   281→                if import_result is not None
   282→                else []
   283→            ),
   284→            "covered_subjective": list(covered_ids),
   285→            "stale_sync_injected": sorted(subjective.injected) if subjective is not None else [],
   286→            "stale_sync_pruned": sorted(subjective.pruned) if subjective is not None else [],
   287→            "auto_cluster_changes": pipeline_result.auto_cluster_changes,
   288→            "import_scores_injected": list(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", []) or []),
   289→            "import_scores_pruned": list(getattr(import_scores_result, "pruned", []) or []),
   290→        },
   291→    )
   292→
   293→
   294→def sync_plan_after_import(
   295→    state: dict,
   296→    diff: dict,
   297→    assessment_mode: str,
   298→    *,
   299→    request: PlanImportSyncRequest | None = None,
   300→) -> None:
   301→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
   302→    try:
   303→        state_file = request.state_file if request is not None else None
   304→        config = request.config if request is not None else None
   305→        import_file = request.import_file if request is not None else None
   306→        import_payload = request.import_payload if request is not None else None
   307→
   308→        plan_path = None
   309→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   310→        if state_file is not None:
   311→            plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   312→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   313→            return
   314→
   315→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   316→        sync_inputs = _build_import_sync_inputs(diff, import_payload)
   317→        trusted = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   318→        was_boundary_ready = live_planned_queue_empty(plan)
   319→
   320→        import_result = _sync_review_delta(plan, state, sync_inputs)
   321→        covered_pruned = (
   322→            _prune_covered_subjective_ids_from_plan(plan, covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids)
   323→            if trusted
   324→            else []
   325→        )
   326→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   327→            plan,
   328→            state,
   329→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   330→            import_file=import_file,
   331→            import_payload=import_payload,
   332→        )
   333→        if trusted:
   334→            clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   335→
   336→        result = ReconcileResult()
   337→        if trusted and sync_inputs.covered_ids and was_boundary_ready:
   338→            result = reconcile_plan(plan, state, target_strict=target_strict)
   339→        if import_result is not None and covered_pruned:
   340→            import_result = ReviewImportSyncResult(
   341→                new_ids=import_result.new_ids,
   342→                added_to_queue=import_result.added_to_queue,
   343→                triage_injected=import_result.triage_injected,
   344→                stale_pruned_from_queue=import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue,
   345→                covered_subjective_pruned_from_queue=covered_pruned,
   346→                triage_injected_ids=import_result.triage_injected_ids,
   347→                triage_deferred=import_result.triage_deferred,
   348→            )
   349→
   350→        dirty = bool(
   351→            import_result is not None
   352→            or covered_pruned
   353→            or import_scores_result.changes
   354→            or result.dirty
   355→        )
   356→        if dirty:
   357→            _append_review_import_sync_log(
   358→                plan,
   359→                diff,
   360→                import_result,
   361→                import_scores_result,
   362→                result,
   363→                covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids,
   364→            )
   365→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   366→
   367→        if import_result is not None:
   368→            _print_review_import_sync(
   369→                state,
   370→                import_result,
   371→                workflow_injected=bool(result.workflow_injected_ids),
   372→                triage_injected=bool(result.triage and result.triage.injected),
   373→            )
   374→        _print_workflow_injected_message(result.workflow_injected_ids)
   375→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   376→        print(
   377→            colorize(
   378→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   379→                "dim",
   380→            )
   381→        )
   382→
   383→
   384→__all__ = ["PlanImportSyncRequest", "sync_plan_after_import"]
   385→
```

```
   130→def _active_cluster_issue_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
   131→    """Return issue IDs owned by clusters that are active planned work."""
   132→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   133→        return set()
   134→    active_ids: set[str] = set()

   136→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   137→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict) or not cluster_is_active(cluster):
   138→            continue
   139→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   140→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id and issue_id not in skipped_ids:
   141→                active_ids.add(issue_id)

   149→    all_ids: set[str] = set()

   153→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):

   159→def _merge_execution_candidates(

   163→    plan: dict | None,

   175→        and item.get("id", "") not in assessment_request_ids

   178→            or item.get("id", "") in active_cluster_ids
   179→        )
   180→    ]
   181→
   182→    execution_candidates: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   183→    seen_execution_ids: set[str] = set()
   184→    for item in [*explicit_objective_items, *queued_non_review_items]:
   185→        item_id = str(item.get("id", ""))
   186→        if not item_id or item_id in seen_execution_ids:
   187→            continue
   188→        seen_execution_ids.add(item_id)
   189→        execution_candidates.append(item)
   190→
   191→    anchored_execution_items = [
   192→        item

   194→        if item.get("id", "") in explicit_queue_ids

   196→    return execution_candidates, anchored_execution_items

   200→    plan: dict | None,

   258→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   259→    plan: dict | None,
   260→    *,
   261→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   262→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   263→    anchored_execution_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   264→    explicit_queue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   265→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   266→    review_backlog_present: bool,
   267→    postflight_assessment_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   268→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   269→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   270→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   271→) -> str:
   272→    persisted_phase = (
   273→        current_lifecycle_phase(plan) if isinstance(plan, dict) else None
   274→    )
   275→    postflight_sticky = (
   276→        isinstance(plan, dict)
   277→        and bool(plan.get("plan_start_scores"))
   278→        and not postflight_scan_pending(plan)
   279→        and persisted_phase in {
   280→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
   281→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
   282→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
   283→        }
   284→    )
   285→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   286→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   287→    if postflight_sticky:
   288→        if persisted_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW:
   289→            if postflight_workflow_items:
   290→                return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   291→            if triage_items and review_backlog_present:
   292→                return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   293→            if triage_items:
   294→                return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   295→            if postflight_assessment_items:
   296→                return PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
   297→            if postflight_review_items:
   298→                return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   299→        elif persisted_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE:
   300→            if triage_items and review_backlog_present:
   301→                return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   302→            if triage_items:
   303→                return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   304→            if postflight_workflow_items:
   305→                return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   306→            if postflight_assessment_items:
   307→                return PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
   308→            if postflight_review_items:
   309→                return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   310→        else:
   311→            if postflight_assessment_items:
   312→                return PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
   313→            if postflight_review_items:
   314→                return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   315→            if postflight_workflow_items:
   316→                return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   317→            if triage_items and review_backlog_present:
   318→                return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   319→            if triage_items:
   320→                return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   321→    if anchored_execution_items:
   322→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   323→    if scan_items:
   324→        return PHASE_SCAN
   325→    if explicit_queue_items:
   326→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   327→    # Postflight sequence is ordered, not opportunistic:
   328→    # scan -> subjective review -> score/workflow -> triage.
   329→    if postflight_assessment_items:
   330→        return PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
   331→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   332→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   333→    if triage_items and review_backlog_present:
   334→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   335→    if postflight_review_items:
   336→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   337→    if triage_items:
   338→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   339→    return PHASE_SCAN
   340→
   341→
   342→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   343→    phase: str,
   344→    *,
   345→    explicit_queue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   346→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   347→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   348→    postflight_assessment_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   349→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   350→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   351→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   352→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   353→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   354→        return initial_review_items
   355→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   356→        return explicit_queue_items
   357→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   358→        deferred_items = [
   359→            item for item in scan_items
   360→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   361→        ]
   362→        if deferred_items:
   363→            return deferred_items
   364→        return [
   365→            item for item in scan_items
   366→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   367→        ]
   368→    if phase == PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT:
   369→        return postflight_assessment_items
   370→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   371→        return postflight_review_items
   372→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   373→        return postflight_workflow_items
   374→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   375→        return triage_items
   376→    return []
   377→
   378→
   379→def build_queue_snapshot(
   380→    state: StateModel,
   381→    *,
   382→    options: object | None = None,
   383→    plan: dict | None = None,
   384→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   385→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   386→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   387→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   388→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   389→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   390→    )
   391→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   392→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   393→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues((state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})), scan_path)
   394→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   395→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   396→
   397→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   398→        state,
   399→        scan_path=scan_path,
   400→        status_filter="open",
   401→        scope=scope,
   402→        chronic=chronic,
   403→        forced_ids=_live_planned_queue_ids(effective_plan),
   404→    )
   405→    objective_items = [
   406→        item for item in all_issue_items
   407→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   408→    ]
   409→    executable_objective_ids = _executable_objective_ids(
   410→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   411→        effective_plan,
   412→    )
   413→    all_clustered_ids = _all_cluster_issue_ids(effective_plan)
   414→    if (
   415→        isinstance(effective_plan, dict)
   416→        and not _live_planned_queue_ids(effective_plan)
   417→        and all_clustered_ids & executable_objective_ids
   418→    ):
   419→        executable_objective_ids -= all_clustered_ids
   420→    explicit_objective_items = [
   421→        item for item in objective_items
   422→        if item.get("id", "") in executable_objective_ids
   423→    ]
   424→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   425→    assessment_request_items = _assessment_request_items(all_issue_items)
   426→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   427→        effective_plan,
   428→        state,
   429→        review_issue_items,
   430→    )
   431→    review_issue_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in review_issue_items}
   432→    assessment_request_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in assessment_request_items}
   433→    explicit_queue_items, anchored_execution_items = _merge_execution_candidates(
   434→        all_issue_items=all_issue_items,
   435→        explicit_objective_items=explicit_objective_items,
   436→        plan=effective_plan,
   437→        review_issue_ids=review_issue_ids,
   438→        assessment_request_ids=assessment_request_ids,
   439→    )
   440→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   441→        state,
   442→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   443→        threshold=target_strict,
   444→    )
   445→    # Suppress subjective dimension items when review issues already cover
   446→    # the same dimension — the review issues are more actionable and will
   447→    # surface in the review-postflight phase instead.
   448→    postflight_assessment_items = [
   449→        item
   450→        for item in subjective_postflight_items
   451→        if not (
   452→            item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   453→            and int((item.get("detail") or {}).get("open_review_issues", 0)) > 0
   454→        )
   455→    ] + list(assessment_request_items)
   456→    postflight_review_items = [
   457→        *executable_review_items,
   458→    ]
   459→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   460→        effective_plan,
   461→        state,
   462→    )
   463→
   464→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   465→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   466→        effective_plan,
   467→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   468→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   469→        anchored_execution_items=anchored_execution_items,
   470→        explicit_queue_items=explicit_queue_items,
   471→        scan_items=scan_items,
   472→        review_backlog_present=bool(review_issue_items),
   473→        postflight_assessment_items=postflight_assessment_items,
   474→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   475→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   476→        triage_items=triage_items,
   477→    )
   478→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   479→        phase,
   480→        explicit_queue_items=explicit_queue_items,
   481→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   482→        scan_items=scan_items,
   483→        postflight_assessment_items=postflight_assessment_items,
   484→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   485→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   486→        triage_items=triage_items,
   487→    )
   488→
   489→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   490→    backlog_items = [
   491→        item for item in (
   492→            [
   493→                *objective_items,
   494→                *initial_review_items,
   495→                *postflight_assessment_items,
   496→                *review_issue_items,
   497→                *scan_items,
   498→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   499→                *triage_items,
   500→            ]
   501→        )
   502→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   503→    ]
   504→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   505→        1 for item in objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   506→    )
   507→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(explicit_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   508→
   509→    return QueueSnapshot(
   510→        phase=phase,
   511→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   512→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   513→        all_postflight_assessment_items=tuple(postflight_assessment_items),
   514→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   515→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   516→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   517→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   518→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   519→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   520→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   521→        planned_objective_count=len(explicit_objective_items),
   522→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   523→            1
   524→            for item in execution_items
   525→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   526→            and counts_toward_objective_backlog(item)
   527→        ),
   528→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   529→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   530→        assessment_postflight_count=len(postflight_assessment_items),
   531→        subjective_postflight_count=len(subjective_postflight_items),
   532→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   533→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   534→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   535→    )
   536→
   537→
   538→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   539→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   540→    if phase in {
   541→        PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL,
   542→        PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT,
   543→        PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT,
   544→    }:
   545→        return "review"
   546→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   547→        return "workflow"
   548→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   549→        return "triage"
   550→    return phase
   551→
   552→
   553→__all__ = [
   554→    "PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT",
   555→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   556→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   557→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   558→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   559→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   560→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   561→    "QueueSnapshot",
   562→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   563→    "coarse_phase_name",
   564→]
   565→
```

```
1→"""Shared boundary-triggered plan reconciliation pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_issues
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import QueueSyncResult, is_synthetic_id
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.meta import append_log_entry
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import compute_subjective_visibility
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import set_lifecycle_phase
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import sync_subjective_dimensions
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import sync_triage_needed
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.workflow import (
    16→    ScoreSnapshot,
    17→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    18→    sync_create_plan_needed,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass
    23→class ReconcileResult:
    24→    """Mutation summary for one boundary-triggered reconcile pass."""
    25→
    26→    subjective: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    27→    auto_cluster_changes: int = 0
    28→    communicate_score: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    29→    create_plan: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    30→    triage: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    31→    lifecycle_phase: str = ""
    32→    lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def dirty(self) -> bool:
    36→        return any(
    37→            (
    38→                self.subjective is not None and bool(self.subjective.changes),
    39→                self.auto_cluster_changes > 0,
    40→                self.communicate_score is not None
    41→                and bool(self.communicate_score.changes),
    42→                self.create_plan is not None
    43→                and bool(self.create_plan.changes),
    44→                self.triage is not None
    45→                and bool(
    46→                    self.triage.changes
    47→                    or getattr(self.triage, "deferred", False)
    48→                ),
    49→                self.lifecycle_phase_changed,
    50→            )
    51→        )
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def workflow_injected_ids(self) -> list[str]:
    55→        injected: list[str] = []
    56→        for result in (self.communicate_score, self.create_plan):
    57→            if result is None:
    58→                continue
    59→            injected.extend(list(result.injected))
    60→        return injected
    61→
    62→
    63→def _current_scores(state: dict) -> ScoreSnapshot:
    64→    snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
    65→    return ScoreSnapshot(
    66→        strict=snapshot.strict,
    67→        overall=snapshot.overall,
    68→        objective=snapshot.objective,
    69→        verified=snapshot.verified,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _log_gate_changes(plan: dict, action: str, detail: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    74→    append_log_entry(plan, action, actor="system", detail=detail)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _persist_lifecycle(
    78→    plan: dict, state: dict, *, target_strict: float,
    79→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    80→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    81→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import coarse_phase_name
    82→
    83→    phase = coarse_phase_name(
    84→        queue_context(state, plan=plan, target_strict=target_strict).snapshot.phase
    85→    )
    86→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    87→
    88→
    89→def live_planned_queue_empty(plan: dict) -> bool:
    90→    """Return True when queue_order has no remaining substantive items.
    91→
    92→    Overrides and clusters are ownership metadata — they must never expand
    93→    the live queue.  Only explicit ``queue_order`` entries count.
    94→    """
    95→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    96→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    97→    return not any(
    98→        isinstance(item_id, str)
    99→        and item_id not in skipped
   100→        and not is_synthetic_id(item_id)
   101→        for item_id in order
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→def reconcile_plan(plan: dict, state: dict, *, target_strict: float) -> ReconcileResult:
   106→    """Run the shared boundary reconciliation pipeline."""
   107→    result = ReconcileResult()
   108→    if not live_planned_queue_empty(plan):
   109→        return result
   110→
   111→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   112→        state,
   113→        target_strict=target_strict,
   114→        plan=plan,
   115→    )
   116→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   117→
   118→    result.subjective = sync_subjective_dimensions(
   119→        plan,
   120→        state,
   121→        policy=policy,
   122→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   123→    )
   124→    if result.subjective.changes:
   125→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_subjective", {"changes": True})
   126→
   127→    result.auto_cluster_changes = int(
   128→        auto_cluster_issues(
   129→            plan,
   130→            state,
   131→            target_strict=target_strict,
   132→            policy=policy,
   133→        )
   134→    )
   135→    if result.auto_cluster_changes:
   136→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "auto_cluster", {"changes": True})
   137→
   138→    result.communicate_score = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   139→        plan,
   140→        state,
   141→        policy=policy,
   142→        current_scores=_current_scores(state),
   143→    )
   144→    if result.communicate_score.changes:
   145→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_communicate_score", {"injected": True})
   146→
   147→    result.create_plan = sync_create_plan_needed(
   148→        plan,
   149→        state,
   150→        policy=policy,
   151→    )
   152→    if result.create_plan.changes:
   153→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_create_plan", {"injected": True})
   154→
   155→    result.triage = sync_triage_needed(
   156→        plan,
   157→        state,
   158→        policy=policy,
   159→    )
   160→    if result.triage.injected:
   161→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_triage", {"injected": True})
   162→
   163→    result.lifecycle_phase, result.lifecycle_phase_changed = _persist_lifecycle(
   164→        plan,
   165→        state,
   166→        target_strict=target_strict,
   167→    )
   168→    if result.lifecycle_phase_changed:
   169→        _log_gate_changes(
   170→            plan,
   171→            "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   172→            {"phase": result.lifecycle_phase},
   173→        )
   174→
   175→    return result
   176→
   177→
   178→__all__ = ["ReconcileResult", "live_planned_queue_empty", "reconcile_plan"]
   179→
```

```
1→"""Shared constants and helpers for plan internals."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
     9→
    10→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    11→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    12→
    13→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    14→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    15→    "triage::observe",
    16→    "triage::reflect",
    17→    "triage::organize",
    18→    "triage::enrich",
    19→    "triage::sense-check",
    20→    "triage::commit",
    21→)
    22→# value-check was folded into sense-check as a subagent in v0.9.9.
    23→# Old plan.json files may still contain triage_stages["value-check"] data;
    24→# it is silently ignored because _TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES no longer includes it.
    25→TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS = tuple(
    26→    (stage_id.removeprefix("triage::"), stage_id) for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    27→)
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER = {
    29→    stage_name: index for index, (stage_name, _stage_id) in enumerate(TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS)
    30→}
    31→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    32→_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES = {
    33→    stage_id.removeprefix("triage::") for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    34→}
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"
    40→WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID = "workflow::run-scan"
    41→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    42→WORKFLOW_IDS = {
    43→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    44→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    45→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    46→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    47→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    48→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    49→}
    50→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (
    51→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    52→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    53→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    54→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    55→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    56→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    57→)
    58→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    59→
    60→
    61→def is_synthetic_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    62→    """Return True when a raw plan ID refers to synthetic queue work."""
    63→    return any(issue_id.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    64→
    65→
    66→def is_workflow_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    67→    """Return True when a raw plan ID is a workflow synthetic."""
    68→    return issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX)
    69→
    70→
    71→def is_triage_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    72→    """Return True when a raw plan ID is a triage synthetic."""
    73→    return issue_id.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX)
    74→
    75→
    76→@dataclass
    77→class QueueSyncResult:
    78→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    79→
    80→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    81→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    82→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    83→    deferred: bool = False
    84→
    85→    @property
    86→    def changes(self) -> int:
    87→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    88→
    89→
    90→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    91→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    92→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    93→        return {}
    94→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    95→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    96→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    97→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    98→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    99→        return meta_or_stages
   100→    return {}
   101→
   102→
   103→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   104→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
   105→    return {
   106→        str(name)
   107→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
   108→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
   109→    }
   110→
   111→
   112→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   113→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
   114→    return {
   115→        str(name)
   116→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
   117→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
   118→    }
   119→
   120→
   121→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
   122→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
   123→    seen: set[str] = set()
   124→    normalized: list[str] = []
   125→
   126→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   127→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   128→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   129→            seen.add(issue_id)
   130→
   131→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   132→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   133→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   134→            seen.add(issue_id)
   135→
   136→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   137→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   138→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   139→            seen.add(issue_id)
   140→
   141→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   142→        if issue_id in seen:
   143→            continue
   144→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   145→        seen.add(issue_id)
   146→
   147→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   148→
   149→
   150→__all__ = [
   151→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   152→    "QueueSyncResult",
   153→    "normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix",
   154→    "confirmed_triage_stage_names",
   155→    "is_synthetic_id",
   156→    "is_triage_id",
   157→    "is_workflow_id",
   158→    "recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names",
   159→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   160→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   161→    "TRIAGE_IDS",

   163→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER",
   164→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
```

```
1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→

    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults

    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    24→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    25→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    26→    {
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    31→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    32→    }
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    37→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    38→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    39→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    40→        refresh_state = {}
    41→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    42→    return refresh_state
    43→
    44→
    45→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    46→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _touches_objective_issue(
    50→    *,
    51→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None,
    52→    state: dict[str, object] | None,
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    if issue_ids is None:
    55→        return True
    56→
    57→    real_issue_ids = [
    58→        issue_id
    59→        for issue_id in issue_ids
    60→        if _is_real_queue_issue(str(issue_id))
    61→    ]
    62→    if not real_issue_ids:
    63→        return False
    64→    if not isinstance(state, dict):
    65→        return True
    66→
    67→    issues = state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})
    68→    if not isinstance(issues, dict):
    69→        return True
    70→
    71→    objective_seen = False
    72→    for issue_id in real_issue_ids:
    73→        issue = issues.get(issue_id)
    74→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    75→            return True
    76→        if counts_toward_objective_backlog(issue):
    77→            objective_seen = True
    78→    return objective_seen
    79→
    80→
    81→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    82→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    83→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    84→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    85→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    86→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    87→            return phase
    88→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    89→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    90→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    91→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    92→    return None
    93→
    94→
    95→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    96→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    97→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    98→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    99→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   100→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
   101→        return False
   102→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
   107→    plan: PlanModel,
   108→    *,
   109→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   110→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   111→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   112→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   113→    has_triage: bool,
   114→    has_deferred: bool,
   115→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
   116→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
   117→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
   118→        plan,
   119→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
   120→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
   121→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
   122→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
   123→        has_triage=has_triage,
   124→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
   125→    )
   126→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
   127→
   128→
   129→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    *,
   132→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   133→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   134→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   135→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   136→    has_triage: bool,
   137→    has_deferred: bool,
   138→) -> str:
   139→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   140→    if has_initial_reviews:
   141→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   142→    if has_objective_backlog:
   143→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   144→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   145→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   146→    if has_postflight_review:
   147→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   148→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   149→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   150→    if has_triage:
   151→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   152→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   153→    if persisted is not None:
   154→        return persisted
   155→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   156→
   157→
   158→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   159→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   160→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   161→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   162→        return True
   163→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   164→
   165→
   166→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   167→    plan: PlanModel,
   168→    *,
   169→    scan_count: int | None,
   170→) -> bool:
   171→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   172→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   173→    try:
   174→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   175→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   176→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   177→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   178→        return False
   179→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   180→    return True
   181→
   182→
   183→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   184→    plan: PlanModel,
   185→    *,
   186→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   187→    state: dict[str, object] | None = None,
   188→) -> bool:
   189→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on objective issues."""
   190→    if not _touches_objective_issue(issue_ids=issue_ids, state=state):
   191→        return False
   192→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   193→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   194→        return False
   195→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   196→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   197→    return True
   198→
   199→
   200→__all__ = [
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Single-pass sync for all three subjective categories:
     4→
     5→- **unscored** — placeholder dimensions never yet reviewed.  Injected at cycle
     6→  boundaries only; pruned mid-cycle.
     7→- **stale** — dimensions whose assessment needs a review refresh.
     8→- **under_target** — scored below target but not stale.
     9→
    10→Stale and under-target IDs share injection rules: injected when no objective
    11→backlog exists, evicted when it does, force-promoted after repeated deferrals.
    12→
    13→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue
    14→(except escalation promotion, which moves deferred IDs ahead of objective work).
    15→"""
    16→
    17→from __future__ import annotations
    18→
    19→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    21→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    22→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    23→    QueueSyncResult,
    24→    is_triage_id,
    25→    is_workflow_id,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    28→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    29→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    30→
    31→from .context import has_objective_backlog, is_mid_cycle
    32→from .defer_policy import (
    33→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    34→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    35→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    36→    update_defer_state,
    37→)
    38→
    39→
    40→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→# Defer-meta keys (shared with work_queue readers via plan dict)
    42→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43→
    44→[REDACTED]
    45→_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    46→[REDACTED]
    47→
    48→
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50→# ID helpers
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→
    53→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    54→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    55→
    56→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    57→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    58→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    59→    """
    60→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    61→        state,
    62→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    63→    )
    64→
    65→
    66→def current_under_target_ids(
    67→    state: StateModel,
    68→    *,
    69→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    70→) -> set[str]:
    71→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    72→
    73→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    74→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    75→    """
    76→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    77→        state,
    78→        target_strict=target_strict,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    84→# Helpers
    85→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    86→
    87→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    88→    order: list[str],
    89→    *,
    90→    keep_ids: set[str],
    91→    pruned: list[str],
    92→) -> None:
    93→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    94→    to_remove = [
    95→        fid for fid in order
    96→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    97→        and fid not in keep_ids
    98→    ]
    99→    for fid in to_remove:
   100→        order.remove(fid)
   101→        pruned.append(fid)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
   105→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
   106→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   107→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   108→        return set()
   109→    return {
   110→        str(fid)
   111→        for fid in skipped
   112→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   113→    }
   114→
   115→
   116→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
   117→    order: list[str],
   118→    *,
   119→    skipped_ids: set[str],
   120→    pruned: list[str],
   121→) -> None:
   122→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   123→    if not skipped_ids:
   124→        return
   125→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   126→    for fid in to_remove:
   127→        order.remove(fid)
   128→        pruned.append(fid)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   132→    order: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    inject_ids: set[str],
   135→    injected: list[str],
   136→) -> None:
   137→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   138→
   139→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   140→    """
   141→    existing = set(order)
   142→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   143→        if sid not in existing:
   144→            order.append(sid)
   145→            injected.append(sid)
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Deferral tracking and escalation
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→def _clear_defer_meta(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   153→    plan.pop(_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
   154→
   155→
   156→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
   157→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order.
   158→
   159→    Inserts after any workflow/triage items at the front of the queue.
   160→    """
   161→    target_ids: list[str] = []
   162→    seen: set[str] = set()
   163→    for fid in ids:
   164→        sid = str(fid).strip()
   165→        if not sid or sid in seen:
   166→            continue
   167→        target_ids.append(sid)
   168→        seen.add(sid)
   169→    if not target_ids:
   170→        return 0
   171→
   172→    insert_at = 0
   173→    while insert_at < len(order):
   174→        current = str(order[insert_at])
   175→        if not (is_workflow_id(current) or is_triage_id(current)):
   176→            break
   177→        insert_at += 1
   178→
   179→    changes = 0
   180→    for sid in target_ids:
   181→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   182→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   183→        if existing_idx is not None:
   184→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   185→                insert_at += 1
   186→                continue
   187→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   188→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   189→                target_idx -= 1
   190→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   191→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   192→        changes += 1
   193→    return changes
   194→
   195→
   196→def _update_deferral(
   197→    plan: PlanModel,
   198→    state: StateModel,
   199→    *,
   200→    injectable_ids: set[str],
   201→    stale_ids: set[str],
   202→    under_target_ids: set[str],
   203→    skipped_ids: set[str],
   204→) -> bool:
   205→    """Update defer-meta and return True if escalation threshold is reached."""
   206→    stale_deferred = sorted(stale_ids - skipped_ids)
   207→    under_target_deferred = sorted(under_target_ids - skipped_ids)
   208→
   209→    defer_state = update_defer_state(
   210→        plan.get(_DEFER_META_KEY),
   211→        state=state,
   212→        deferred_ids=injectable_ids,
   213→        options=DeferUpdateOptions(deferred_ids_field=_DEFER_IDS_FIELD),
   214→    )
   215→    defer_state["deferred_stale_ids"] = stale_deferred
   216→    defer_state["deferred_under_target_ids"] = under_target_deferred
   217→
   218→    escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   219→        defer_state,
   220→        state=state,
   221→        options=DeferEscalationOptions(deferred_ids_field=_DEFER_IDS_FIELD),
   222→    )
   223→
   224→    if escalated:
   225→        defer_state[_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = sorted(injectable_ids)
   226→    else:
   227→        defer_state.pop(_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
   228→
   229→    plan[_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   230→    return escalated
   231→
   232→
   233→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   234→# Unified subjective dimension sync
   235→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   236→
   237→def sync_subjective_dimensions(
   238→    plan: PlanModel,
   239→    state: StateModel,
   240→    *,
   241→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   242→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   243→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   244→    """Single-pass sync for all subjective dimensions in the plan queue.
   245→
   246→    Handles three categories with unified prune/inject logic:
   247→
   248→    - **unscored**: placeholder dimensions not yet reviewed.  Injected at cycle
   249→      boundaries; pruned mid-cycle.
   250→    - **stale**: dimensions needing review refresh.
   251→    - **under_target**: scored below target but not stale.
   252→
   253→    Stale/under-target injection is gated by objective backlog:
   254→
   255→    1. **No backlog** (or cycle just completed) — inject to back of queue.
   256→    2. **Backlog, not escalated** — evict from queue.  Update deferral counter.
   257→    3. **Backlog, escalated** — force-promote to front (after workflow/triage).
   258→
   259→    Unscored injection is unconditional at cycle boundaries, independent of
   260→    backlog state.
   261→    """
   262→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   263→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   264→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   265→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   266→
   267→    # --- Compute all ID sets once -----------------------------------------
   268→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   269→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   270→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   271→    )
   272→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   273→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   274→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   275→
   276→    # --- Resurface: clear skips for never-reviewed dimensions -------------
   277→    if not mid_cycle:
   278→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   279→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   280→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   281→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   282→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   283→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   284→
   285→    # --- Repair: remove skipped IDs from queue_order ----------------------
   286→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   287→
   288→    # --- Backlog and deferral ---------------------------------------------
   289→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   290→    should_defer = objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed
   291→    escalated = False
   292→    if should_defer and injectable_ids:
   293→        escalated = _update_deferral(
   294→            plan, state,
   295→            injectable_ids=injectable_ids,
   296→            stale_ids=stale_ids,
   297→            under_target_ids=under_target_ids,
   298→            skipped_ids=skipped_ids,
   299→        )
   300→    else:
   301→        _clear_defer_meta(plan)
   302→
   303→    # --- Single prune pass ------------------------------------------------
   304→    evicting = should_defer and not escalated
   305→    if evicting:
   306→        keep_ids = set() if mid_cycle else unscored_ids
   307→    else:
   308→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if mid_cycle else (unscored_ids | injectable_ids)
   309→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   310→
   311→    # --- Inject unscored (cycle boundaries only) --------------------------
   312→    if not mid_cycle:
   313→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   314→            order,
   315→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   316→            injected=result.injected,
   317→        )
   318→
   319→    # --- Inject or promote stale/under_target -----------------------------
   320→    if escalated:
   321→        _promote_subjective_ids(order, sorted(injectable_ids))
   322→    elif not evicting and injectable_ids:
   323→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   324→
   325→    return result
   326→
   327→
   328→__all__ = [
   329→    "current_under_target_ids",
   330→    "current_unscored_ids",
   331→    "sync_subjective_dimensions",
   332→]
   333→
```

```
    33→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    34→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    35→    current_lifecycle_phase,
    36→)
    37→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    38→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    39→    TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
    40→    TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
    41→    triage_manual_stage_command,
```
