```
    52→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→# Dimension key normalization
    54→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55→
    56→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    57→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    58→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    59→    target = cleaned.lower()
    60→
    61→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    62→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    63→            return str(dim_key)
    64→    return slugify(cleaned)
    65→
    66→
    67→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    68→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    69→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    70→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    71→    return {
    72→        cleaned.lower(),
    73→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    74→        slugify(cleaned),
    75→        canonical.lower(),
    76→        slugify(canonical),
    77→    }
    78→
    79→
    80→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81→# Subjective strict scores
    82→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    83→
    84→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    85→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    86→    if not dim_scores:
    87→        return {}
    88→
    89→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    90→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    91→    for entry in entries:
    92→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    93→        if not name:
    94→            continue
    95→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    96→        [REDACTED](name)
    97→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    98→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    99→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   100→            if not key:
   101→                continue
   102→            aliases.add(key)
   103→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   104→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   105→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   106→        for alias in aliases:
   107→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   108→    return scores
   109→
   110→
   111→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   112→# Synthetic item builders
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→
   115→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   116→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   117→
   118→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   119→    """
   120→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   121→    order_set = set(order)
   122→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   123→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   124→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   125→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   126→    present_names = {
   127→        name
   128→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   129→        if sid in present_ids
   130→    }
   131→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   132→    if not present_names:
   133→        return []
   134→
   135→    issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   136→    open_review_count = sum(
   137→        1 for f in issues.values()
   138→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   139→        and is_triage_finding(f)
   140→    )
   141→
   142→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   143→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   144→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   145→        if name not in present_names:
   146→            continue
   147→        if name in confirmed:
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   151→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   152→        blocked_by = sorted(
   153→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   154→        )
   155→
   156→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   157→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   158→
   159→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   160→            "id": sid,
   161→            "tier": 1,
   162→            "confidence": "high",
   163→            "detector": "triage",
   164→            "file": ".",
   165→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   166→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   167→            "detail": {
   168→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   169→                "stage": name,
   170→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   171→                "runner_commands": [
   172→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   173→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   174→                ],
   175→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   176→            },
   177→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   178→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   179→        }
   180→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   181→        items.append(item)
   182→    return items
   183→
   184→
   185→def build_subjective_items(
   186→    state: dict,
   187→    issues: dict,
   188→    *,
   189→    threshold: float = 100.0,
   190→    plan: dict | None = None,
   191→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   192→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   193→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   194→    if not dim_scores:
   195→        return []
   196→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   197→
   198→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   199→    if not subjective_entries:
   200→        return []
   201→    unassessed_dims = {
   202→        str(name).strip()
   203→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   204→            dim_scores
   205→        )
   206→    }
   207→
   208→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   209→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   210→    for issue in issues.values():
   211→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   212→            continue
   213→        if is_triage_finding(issue):
   214→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   215→            if dim_key:
   216→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   217→
   218→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   219→    latest_trusted_audit_ts = ""
   220→    for raw_entry in reversed(state.get("assessment_import_audit", []) or []):
   221→        if not isinstance(raw_entry, dict):
   222→            continue
   223→        if raw_entry.get("mode") not in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   224→            continue
   225→        latest_trusted_audit_ts = str(raw_entry.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   226→        if latest_trusted_audit_ts:
   227→            break
   228→    current_phase = current_lifecycle_phase(plan) if isinstance(plan, dict) else None
   229→
   230→    def _suppressed_same_cycle_refresh(dimension_key: str, *, stale: bool) -> bool:
   231→        if not stale or latest_trusted_audit_ts == "":
   232→            return False
   233→        if current_phase not in {LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW, LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE}:
   234→            return False
   235→        assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments", {}) or {}
   236→        payload = assessments.get(dimension_key)
   237→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   238→            return False
   239→        refresh_reason = str(payload.get("refresh_reason", "")).strip()
   240→        if not refresh_reason.startswith("review_issue_"):
   241→            return False
   242→        assessed_at = str(payload.get("assessed_at", "")).strip()
   243→        if assessed_at == "":
   244→            return False
   245→        return assessed_at >= latest_trusted_audit_ts
   246→
   247→    def _prepare_command(
   248→        cli_keys: list[str],
   249→        *,
   250→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   251→    ) -> str:
   252→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        if cli_keys:
   254→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   255→        if force_review_rerun:
   256→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   257→        return command
   258→
   259→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   260→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   261→        if not name:
   262→            continue
   263→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   264→        [REDACTED](name)
   265→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   266→        cli_keys = [
   267→            str(key).strip().lower()
   268→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   269→            if str(key).strip()
   270→        ]
   271→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   272→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   273→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   274→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   275→            name in unassessed_dims
   276→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   277→        )
   278→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   279→        if _suppressed_same_cycle_refresh(dim_key, stale=is_stale):
   280→            continue
   281→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   282→            continue
   283→        # Postflight assessment work covers every dimension still below the
   284→        # target threshold. Unassessed and stale dimensions remain special
   285→        # cases, but fresh under-target scores surface alongside them instead
   286→        # of being relegated to advisory-only status.
   287→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   288→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   289→        if open_review > 0:
   290→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   291→        else:
   292→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   293→        reason_tags = ["below target"]
   294→        if is_stale:
   295→            reason_tags.append("stale")
   296→        reasons = ", ".join(reason_tags)
   297→        summary = f"Subjective review needed: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%) [{reasons}]"
   298→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   299→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   300→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   301→            "file": ".",
   302→            "confidence": "medium",
   303→            "summary": summary,
   304→            "detail": {
   305→                "dimension_name": name,
   306→                "dimension": dim_key,
   307→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   308→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   309→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   310→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   311→            },
   312→            "status": "open",
   313→        }
   314→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   315→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   316→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   317→        items.append(item)
   318→    return items
   319→
   320→
   321→__all__ = [
   322→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   323→    "build_create_plan_item",
   324→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   325→    "build_import_scores_item",
   326→    "build_run_scan_item",
   327→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   328→    "build_subjective_items",
   329→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   330→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   331→]
```

```
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES

    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→    "delegation density": "abstraction_fitness",
    17→    "definition directness": "abstraction_fitness",
    18→    "type discipline": "abstraction_fitness",
    19→}
    20→
    21→
    22→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    23→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    24→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    25→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    26→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    27→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    28→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    29→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    30→            return key
    31→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    32→
    33→
    34→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    36→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    37→        components = (
    38→            data.get("detectors", {})
    39→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    40→            .get("components", [])
    41→        )
    42→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    43→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    44→        else:
    45→            keys = [
    46→                "high_level_elegance",
    47→                "mid_level_elegance",
    48→                "low_level_elegance",
    49→            ]
    50→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    51→        components = (
    52→            data.get("detectors", {})
    53→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    54→            .get("components", [])
    55→        )
    56→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    57→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    58→        else:
    59→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    60→    else:
    61→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    62→
    63→    seen: set[str] = set()
    64→    ordered: list[str] = []
    65→    for key in keys:
    66→        if not key or key in seen:
    67→            continue
    68→        ordered.append(key)
    69→        seen.add(key)
    70→    return ordered
    71→

    73→def all_subjective_entries(
    74→    state: dict,
    75→    *,

    77→) -> list[dict]:

    79→
    80→    Unlike ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` this reads ``dimension_scores``
    81→    directly — no elegance collapsing, no row-count limit, no placeholder
    82→    hiding.  Use this for *enumeration* (work-queue construction, stale/
    83→    under-target detection) where every individual dimension must be visible.
    84→    """
    85→    if dim_scores is None:
    86→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    87→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:

    89→
    90→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}

    99→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
   100→            or str(name).strip().lower() in subjective_display_names
   101→        )

   110→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
   111→                and data.get("failing", 0) == 0
   112→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   113→            )
   114→        )
   115→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   116→        stale = bool(data.get("stale")) or bool(
   117→            dim_key
   118→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   119→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   120→        )
   121→        entries.append(
   122→            {
   123→                "name": name,
   124→                "score": score,
   125→                "strict": strict,
   126→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   127→                "failing": int(data.get("failing", 0) or 0),
   128→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   129→                "placeholder": placeholder,

   132→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   133→            }
   134→        )
```

```
1→"""Sync subdomain for plan queue reconciliation helpers.
     2→
     3→This package groups sync concerns that were previously in the flat
     4→``engine._plan`` namespace:
     5→- ``context``: shared cycle/objective backlog predicates
     6→- ``dimensions``: subjective dimension queue sync
     7→- ``pipeline``: shared boundary-triggered reconcile pipeline
     8→- ``review_import``: review-import-specific queue mutation before boundary sync
     9→- ``triage``: triage-stage queue sync
    10→- ``workflow``: workflow gate queue sync
    11→- ``auto_prune``: auto-cluster stale pruning helper
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→__all__ = [
    19→    "ReconcileResult",
    20→    "auto_prune",
    21→    "context",
    22→    "dimensions",
    23→    "live_planned_queue_empty",
    24→    "pipeline",
    25→    "reconcile_plan",
    26→    "review_import",
    27→    "triage",
    28→    "workflow",
    29→]
    30→
    31→
    32→def __getattr__(name: str) -> Any:
    33→    if name in {"ReconcileResult", "live_planned_queue_empty", "reconcile_plan"}:
    34→        from .pipeline import ReconcileResult, live_planned_queue_empty, reconcile_plan
    35→
    36→        return {
    37→            "ReconcileResult": ReconcileResult,
    38→            "live_planned_queue_empty": live_planned_queue_empty,
    39→            "reconcile_plan": reconcile_plan,
    40→        }[name]
    41→    raise AttributeError(f"module {__name__!r} has no attribute {name!r}")
    42→
```

```
    19→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
    20→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
    21→
    22→    new_ids: set[str]
    23→    added_to_queue: list[str]
    24→    triage_injected: bool
    25→    stale_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    26→    covered_subjective_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    27→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    31→def _has_triage_baseline(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    32→    """Return True when triage has recorded at least one baseline issue ID."""
    33→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    34→    triaged_ids = meta.get("triaged_ids", [])
    35→    return bool(triaged_ids)
    36→
    37→
    38→def _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
    39→    plan: PlanModel,
    40→    state: StateModel,
    41→    *,
    42→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    43→) -> set[str]:
    44→    """Return review IDs that should be synced into queue_order after import.
    45→
    46→    No triage baseline yet: include all currently-open review IDs so the
    47→    first import cannot drop follow-up work.
    48→    Existing triage baseline: include only IDs that are new since triage.
    49→    """
    50→    if _has_triage_baseline(plan):
    51→        return compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
    52→    return set(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else compute_open_issue_ids(state)
    53→
    54→
    55→def _is_review_queue_id(issue_id: str, state: StateModel) -> bool:
    56→    """Return True for queue IDs representing triage findings.
    57→
    58→    Falls back to known review queue ID prefixes only when the issue payload
    59→    is absent.
    60→    """
    61→    issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(issue_id)
    62→    if isinstance(issue, dict):
    63→        return is_triage_finding(issue)
    64→    return issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::")
    65→
    66→
    67→def _prune_stale_triage_meta(
    68→    plan: PlanModel,
    69→    *,
    70→    stale_ids: set[str],
    71→) -> None:
    72→    """Drop stale review IDs from triage recovery metadata."""
    73→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    74→    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not stale_ids:
    75→        return
    76→
    77→    active_ids = meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")
    78→    if isinstance(active_ids, list):
    79→        filtered_active = [
    80→            issue_id for issue_id in active_ids
    81→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    82→        ]
    83→        if filtered_active:
    84→            meta["active_triage_issue_ids"] = filtered_active
    85→        else:
    86→            meta.pop("active_triage_issue_ids", None)
    87→
    88→    undispositioned_ids = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_ids")
    89→    if isinstance(undispositioned_ids, list):
    90→        filtered_undispositioned = [
    91→            issue_id for issue_id in undispositioned_ids
    92→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    93→        ]
    94→        if filtered_undispositioned:
    95→            meta["undispositioned_issue_ids"] = filtered_undispositioned
    96→            meta["undispositioned_issue_count"] = len(filtered_undispositioned)
    97→        else:
    98→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_ids", None)
    99→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_count", None)
   100→
   101→
   102→def _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   103→    plan: PlanModel,
   104→    state: StateModel,
   105→    *,
   106→    live_open_review_ids: set[str],
   107→) -> list[str]:
   108→    """Remove stale review IDs from queue-related plan containers.
   109→
   110→    Review imports can auto-resolve prior review IDs. These IDs must be removed
   111→    from queue_order (and linked queue metadata) or the queue can drift from
   112→    state reality.
   113→    """
   114→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   115→    stale_ids = sorted(
   116→        {
   117→            issue_id
   118→            for issue_id in order
   119→            if _is_review_queue_id(issue_id, state) and issue_id not in live_open_review_ids
   120→        }
   121→    )
   122→    if not stale_ids:
   123→        return []
   124→
   125→    stale_set = set(stale_ids)
   126→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in stale_set]
   127→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, stale_set)
   128→    _prune_stale_triage_meta(plan, stale_ids=stale_set)
   129→
   130→    deferred = plan.get("deferred")
   131→    if isinstance(deferred, list):
   132→        plan["deferred"] = [issue_id for issue_id in deferred if issue_id not in stale_set]
   133→
   134→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
   135→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   136→        for issue_id in stale_set:
   137→            skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   138→
   139→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   140→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
   141→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   142→            continue
   143→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
   144→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in stale_set
   145→        ]
   146→
   147→    return stale_ids
   148→
   149→
   150→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    state: StateModel,
   153→    *,
   154→    policy=None,
   155→    inject_triage: bool = True,
   156→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   157→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   158→
   159→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order, prunes stale review IDs from queue
   160→    containers, and injects triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard
   161→    — defers when objective work remains). Returns ``None`` when no queue
   162→    changes are required.
   163→    """
   164→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   165→    open_review_ids = compute_open_issue_ids(state)
   166→    stale_pruned_from_queue = _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   167→        plan,
   168→        state,
   169→        live_open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   170→    )
   171→    new_ids = _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
   172→        plan,
   173→        state,
   174→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   175→    )
   176→    if not new_ids and not stale_pruned_from_queue:
   177→        return None
   178→
   179→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   180→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   181→    existing = set(order)
   182→    added: list[str] = []
   183→    for issue_id in sorted(new_ids):
   184→        if issue_id not in existing:
   185→            order.append(issue_id)
   186→            added.append(issue_id)
   187→
   188→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = []
   189→    triage_injected = False
   190→    triage_deferred = False
   191→    if inject_triage:
   192→        triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   193→        triage_injected_ids = list(getattr(triage_result, "injected", []) or [])
   194→        triage_injected = bool(triage_injected_ids)
   195→        triage_deferred = bool(triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "deferred", False))
   196→
   197→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   198→        new_ids=new_ids,
   199→        added_to_queue=added,
   200→        stale_pruned_from_queue=stale_pruned_from_queue,
   201→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   202→        triage_injected_ids=triage_injected_ids,
   203→        triage_deferred=triage_deferred,
   204→    )
   205→
   206→
   207→__all__ = ["ReviewImportSyncResult", "sync_plan_after_review_import"]
   208→
```

## Implementation Plan: 5 Postflight Queue Bug Fixes

### Design Decisions (answers to the 3 questions)

**Q1: Where should `prune_synthetic_for_phase()` live?**

It should live in a new file `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/phase_cleanup.py`. Rationale: `constants.py` is for data constants and simple predicate functions; `pipeline.py` orchestrates the reconcile flow and should call cleanup but not define it; a dedicated module in the sync package follows the existing pattern (there is already `auto_prune.py`, `dimensions.py`, etc.). The import sync in `plan_sync.py` can also import from this module for the shared pruning helper.

**Q2: Where should Fix 3's rendering guard go?**

In `_subjective_partitions()` in `snapshot.py`. This is the right layer because: (a) it filters items *before* phase selection, preventing the cascade where stale items cause wrong phase inference; (b) `_subjective_partitions` already has access to `state` and can accept the plan as an additional parameter; (c) it keeps `_phase_for_snapshot` clean -- that function reasons about what phase to pick given items, not about which items should exist.

**Q3: Should Fix 5 prune ALL subjective IDs or only covered ones?**

Prune ALL subjective IDs when entering workflow phase. At the reconcile pipeline level, we don't have import-specific "covered" info. But entering workflow means the scoring/workflow gates have been injected, which means assessment is done. Any remaining `subjective::` IDs are leftover artifacts. The phase transition is the semantic boundary. This is both simpler and more correct than trying to derive coverage.

---

Line 318 captures `was_boundary_ready = live_planned_queue_empty(plan)` before `_sync_review_delta` on line 320, and line 337 uses `was_boundary_ready` as the gate. No further work needed.

### Fix 3: Suppress same-cycle stale re-request in rendering

**Current state of the code**: `build_subjective_items()` in `synthetic.py` already has a `_suppressed_same_cycle_refresh()` inner function (lines 230-245) that does exactly this guard. It checks if `current_phase` is workflow or triage, and if the dimension's `assessed_at` timestamp is >= the latest trusted audit timestamp, it suppresses the item (line 279: `if _suppressed_same_cycle_refresh(dim_key, stale=is_stale): continue`).

However, `build_subjective_items()` receives `plan` as an optional kwarg, and the call site in `_subjective_partitions()` (snapshot.py line 224) does **not** pass `plan`:

```python
def _subjective_partitions(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
    threshold: float,
) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
```

The `plan` is never forwarded, so `current_phase` in `build_subjective_items()` resolves to `None`, and the suppression check is dead code in the snapshot path.

**Changes needed:**

That is all. The guard logic already exists in `build_subjective_items`, it just needs the plan forwarded so it can read the lifecycle phase.

- Lines 299-309: `persisted_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE` -- triage items checked first, then workflow, then assessment

### Fix 5: Phase-transition synthetic cleanup

This is the one remaining substantial change needed. After `_persist_lifecycle()` runs in `reconcile_plan()`, stale synthetic IDs from prior phases should be pruned.

**New file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/phase_cleanup.py`**

```python
"""Prune synthetic queue IDs that belong to prior lifecycle phases."""

from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    TRIAGE_PREFIX,
    WORKFLOW_PREFIX,
)
from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
)

def prune_synthetic_for_phase(plan: dict, phase: str) -> list[str]:
    """Remove synthetic IDs from queue_order that are superseded by *phase*.

    Returns list of pruned IDs (empty if nothing changed).
    """
    order = plan.get("queue_order")
    if not isinstance(order, list):
        return []

    remove_prefixes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    if phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW:
        remove_prefixes = (SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,)
    elif phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE:
        remove_prefixes = (SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, WORKFLOW_PREFIX)
    elif phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE:
        remove_prefixes = (SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, WORKFLOW_PREFIX, TRIAGE_PREFIX)

    if not remove_prefixes:
        return []

    pruned = [
        issue_id for issue_id in order
        if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id.startswith(remove_prefixes)
    ]
    if not pruned:
        return []

    pruned_set = set(pruned)
    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in pruned_set]

    # Also prune from cluster issue_ids
    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
            continue
        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
            continue
        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids
            if issue_id not in pruned_set
        ]

    return pruned
```

**Modify: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`**

In `reconcile_plan()`, after the `_persist_lifecycle()` call (line 163), add the cleanup step:

```python
from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.phase_cleanup import prune_synthetic_for_phase

# After line 167 (lifecycle_phase_changed logging):
phase_pruned = prune_synthetic_for_phase(plan, result.lifecycle_phase)
if phase_pruned:
    _log_gate_changes(
        plan,
        "phase_transition_cleanup",
        {"phase": result.lifecycle_phase, "pruned": phase_pruned},
    )
```

The `ReconcileResult` dataclass does not need a new field for this; the pruned IDs are logged, and `dirty` will already be True because `lifecycle_phase_changed` would be True (phase transitions only prune when the phase actually changed -- if the phase stays the same, no pruning happens since it was already cleaned up on the prior transition).

Wait -- there is an edge case: `prune_synthetic_for_phase` should also run when the phase did NOT change (e.g., if cleanup was missed in a prior cycle). But `dirty` needs to reflect this. Let me revise:

Add a `phase_cleanup_pruned: list[str]` field to `ReconcileResult`:

```python
@dataclass
class ReconcileResult:
    subjective: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    auto_cluster_changes: int = 0
    communicate_score: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    create_plan: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    triage: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    lifecycle_phase: str = ""
    lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False
    phase_cleanup_pruned: list[str] | None = None  # NEW

    @property
    def dirty(self) -> bool:
        return any(
            (
                self.subjective is not None and bool(self.subjective.changes),
                self.auto_cluster_changes > 0,
                self.communicate_score is not None
                and bool(self.communicate_score.changes),
                self.create_plan is not None
                and bool(self.create_plan.changes),
                self.triage is not None
                and bool(
                    self.triage.changes
                    or getattr(self.triage, "deferred", False)
                ),
                self.lifecycle_phase_changed,
                bool(self.phase_cleanup_pruned),  # NEW
            )
        )
```

And in `reconcile_plan()`, always run cleanup after lifecycle persist:

```python
result.phase_cleanup_pruned = prune_synthetic_for_phase(plan, result.lifecycle_phase)
if result.phase_cleanup_pruned:
    _log_gate_changes(
        plan,
        "phase_transition_cleanup",
        {"phase": result.lifecycle_phase, "pruned": result.phase_cleanup_pruned},
    )
```
